### [1] [GroundedGEO: Auditing the Evidence Gap in Generative Search Rankings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25189)
*Yihan Xia,Huiling Fan,Kangrong Zhong,Taotao Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文揭示了生成式搜索系统中的"可识别性缺口"：证据状态是主张-证据关系而非文本属性，纯文本排序器无法区分真实详述与伪造详述。作者构建了证据配对基准（50个电商查询、1950个案例）和主张级重排器GroundedGEO，验证了该缺口的存在，并识别出证据信道受限于标签质量和证据包覆盖率两个因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式搜索系统对产品和服务进行排序，出版商可以低成本地让候选文本看起来相关。然而证据状态并非文本属性，而是主张与证据之间的关系，这使得纯文本排序器和防御手段无法区分诚实的详细内容与捏造的细节，从而产生可识别性缺口。作者旨在审计这一缺口并测试基于证据的防御方法。

Method: 1) 构建证据配对基准：50个电商查询、1950个案例；2) 设计匹配的丰富变体以控制格式和体量；3) 使用"证据包孪生"在文本不变的情况下添加证明，或通过稀释证据包撤回证明；4) 提出主张级重排器GroundedGEO，对缺乏所提供证据包支持的查询相关主张进行惩罚；5) 在冻结的listwise排序器（Qwen2.5-7B、MiMo-v2.5、GLM-5.3-Flash）和pointwise打分器上进行测试；6) 以370条人工黄金标准评估自动评判器的可靠性（含预注册的门槛）。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B上，无支持丰富变体相比干净候选者获得显著归一化排名提升（+0.065至+0.092，Holm校正），而有支持和中性对照则无此效应；该效应具有模型依赖性（MiMo-v2.5上微弱，GLM-5.3-Flash上缺失）。在pointwise打分器上，oracle证据标签在lambda=40时将无支持丰富的top-3比率从0.65降至0.43（洗白率从0.61降至0.39），且零误抑制；证据包孪生在不改变文本的情况下恢复原始比率。对照370条人工黄金标准，所有测试的自动评判器均未通过预注册的可靠性门槛，但最佳本地评判器保留了79-100%的oracle抑制效果，且在受保护臂上测得零误抑制。剥离证明覆盖率会增加0.307的误抑制。

Conclusion: 这些诊断效应识别出证据信道的两个限制：标签质量和证据包覆盖率。研究并未验证自动防御的有效性，且对不利的人工黄金标准臂的解释仍有待裁定。

Abstract: Generative search systems rank products and services for consequential decisions, and publishers can cheaply make candidate text look relevant. Yet evidence status is not a text property but a claim-evidence relation: text-only rankers and defenses cannot separate honest detailed content from fabricated detail, creating an identifiability gap. We audit this gap with an evidence-paired benchmark (50 e-commerce queries, 1,950 cases) and a claim-level reranker, GroundedGEO, that penalizes query-relevant claims lacking support in a supplied packet. Matched rich variants control format and volume; packet twins add attestations at fixed text, while thinned packets withdraw them. On the frozen listwise ranker Qwen2.5-7B, unsupported-rich variants show significant normalized rank gain over clean candidates (+0.065 to +0.092 across claim profiles, Holm-corrected), while supported and neutral controls do not; the effect is model-dependent (marginal on MiMo-v2.5, absent on GLM-5.3-Flash). On a frozen pointwise scorer, oracle evidence labels cut the unsupported-rich top-3 rate from 0.65 to 0.43 (laundering from 0.61 to 0.39) at lambda=40 with zero false suppression; packet twins restore the original rates without changing text. Against a 370-claim human gold, all tested automatic judges fail the preregistered reliability gate, although the best local judge retains 79-100% of oracle suppression with zero measured false suppression on protected arms. Separately, stripping attestation coverage increases false suppression by 0.307. These diagnostic effects identify two limits on the evidence channel: label quality and packet coverage. They do not validate an automatic defense, and interpretation of the adverse human-gold arm remains pending adjudication.

</details>


### [2] [ReFilter: Bridging Embeddings and LLM Filtering for Similar Mobile App Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25306)
*Buthayna AlMulla,Maram Assi,Safwat Hassan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ReFilter 是一个混合框架，先用嵌入检索语义相关的候选应用，再用大语言模型进行上下文过滤，从而更精准地识别功能相似的应用，F1 达到 90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索相似移动应用对研究者、开发者和终端用户都很重要。现有方法依赖嵌入检索，只能捕捉语义相似度，无法识别功能相似的应用；而直接用大语言模型评估大量应用对计算成本过高，尚无先例。

Method: 提出 ReFilter 混合框架：首先用嵌入检索召回语义相关的候选应用，然后应用基于大语言模型的上下文过滤来判定真正功能相似的应用，从而在效率与准确性间取得平衡。

Result: 在相似应用检索任务上取得 90% 的 F1 分数。

Conclusion: ReFilter 通过提升应用替代项的相关性，支持更准确的应用比较，并有助于生态理解、竞品分析与推荐。

Abstract: Retrieving similar mobile applications (apps) is essential for researchers, developers, and end-users. Researchers use similarity detection to study app ecosystems and trends, developers for competitor analysis, and end-users for focused app recommendations. Existing approaches rely on embedding-based retrieval, which captures semantic similarity but fails to identify functionally similar apps. To our knowledge, no prior work has applied large language model (LLM)-based filtering to this task, due to the high computational cost of evaluating large numbers of app pairs. To address this gap, we propose ReFilter, a hybrid framework that first Retrieves semantically related candidate apps using embeddings and then applies LLM-based contextual Filtering to identify true functionally similar apps with higher precision. This design balances efficiency and accuracy, achieving an F1-score of 90% for retrieving similar apps. By improving the relevance of app alternatives, ReFilter enables more accurate app comparisons and supports improved ecosystem understanding, competitor analysis, and recommendations.

</details>


### [3] [Robust Fusion of Semantic and Behavioural Signals for LLM Reranking in Personalised Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25825)
*Aleksandr V. Petrov,Nathan Stein,Erik Lybecker,Emma Schüldt,Daniel Lazarovski,Hugues Bouchard,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在个性化搜索中向LLM交叉编码器注入行为特征易导致捷径学习，本文提出确定性双样本特征丢弃训练，在保留特征可用时的收益同时提升特征缺失时的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化搜索需兼顾查询意图与用户上下文及历史交互。基于LLM的交叉编码器提供统一重排接口，但将预测性行为统计注入提示词会诱发捷径学习——模型过度依赖历史信号，而牺牲了可泛化到稀疏或未见查询的语义与用户上下文模式。

Method: 在大规模音频流媒体平台的个性化搜索系统中，以查询切片统计（QSS）作为交互衍生的行为特征，概括查询-候选对的历史成功情况。针对朴素QSS注入在特征缺失时鲁棒性下降的问题，提出确定性双样本特征丢弃训练：每个样本分别以包含QSS和移除QSS两种形式各呈现一次。

Result: 离线评估中，QSS注入在特征可用时将排序质量提升13.3%。双样本训练在保留该收益的同时，相较朴素QSS训练在QSS移除评估下相对提升4.0%。在线实测中，两种QSS感知变体均使搜索成功率提升约2%；总体测试未能区分双样本训练与仅特征训练，但冷启动对比方向与离线结果一致。

Conclusion: 成对的特征存在与特征移除训练，能够缓解利用强行为统计与在其不可用时保持鲁棒性之间的张力。

Abstract: Personalised search must satisfy query intent while incorporating user context and historical interactions. LLM-based cross-encoders provide a single reranking interface, but injecting predictive behavioural statistics into their prompts can encourage shortcut learning: reliance on historical signals at the expense of semantic and user-context patterns that generalise to sparse or unseen searches.
  We study this problem in the personalised search system of a large-scale audio streaming platform using Query Slice Stats (QSS), an interaction-derived behavioural feature summarising historical success for query-candidate pairs. Naive QSS injection improves ranking when the feature is available but reduces robustness when it is removed. We address this with deterministic dual-sample feature-dropout training, which presents each example once with QSS included and once with QSS removed.
  Offline, QSS injection improves ranking quality by 13.3% when available. Dual-sample training preserves these gains while improving performance under QSS-removed evaluation by 4.0% relative to naive QSS training. In a live online test, both QSS-aware variants improve search success by roughly 2%. The aggregate test does not distinguish dual-sample from features-only training; the cold-start comparison is directionally consistent with the offline results.
  Paired feature-present and feature-removed training can therefore reduce the tension between exploiting strong behavioural statistics and remaining robust when they are unavailable.

</details>


### [4] [Knowledge-as-Skill: A Structural Design for Autonomous Knowledge-Base Use by LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25991)
*Jiangxu Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 Knowledge-as-Skill，将知识库组织为可发现、可导航、自描述的三层结构（SKILL.md 发现层、每目录 index.md 导航层、带 YAML 元数据的知识层），并提供从异构文档转换的流水线；在 WixQA 上 Factuality 0.889、Context Recall 0.816，优于 Corpus2Skill 报告的 0.767 和 0.708，但 Faithfulness 与 Context Precision 略低、交互轮次更多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 的"检索-拼接-生成"流程替模型做检索决策；随着工具调用与智能体循环更可靠，智能体可自行决定是否检索、检查什么、何时停止。但新瓶颈在于智能体不了解知识库里有什么：传统知识库只暴露匿名文本块，缺少关于范围、目的、来源和关系的信息。

Method: 提出 Knowledge-as-Skill 组织方案，使知识库可发现、可导航、可自描述，含三层：以 SKILL.md 为中心的发现层；每个目录一个 index.md 的导航层；包含带 YAML frontmatter（主题、类型、来源、生命周期）文档的知识层。设计遵循 Open Knowledge Format (OKF) 与 Skill 协议且不修改智能体框架。另提供 knowledge-as-skill 流水线，把 PDF、Word、网页导出和笔记等异构集合转换为该结构。

Result: 在 WixQA 企业客服基准的初步评测中，Factuality 0.889、Context Recall 0.816，对比 Corpus2Skill 报告值 0.767 与 0.708；Faithfulness 略低、Context Precision 较低、交互轮次更多。由于模型、提示词与知识包构建不同，属方向性跨工作证据而非受控对比。

Conclusion: 通过让知识库具备自描述与可导航结构，可提升智能体式检索在事实性与上下文召回上的表现；但在忠实度、上下文精度与交互效率上仍有取舍，且结论需受控实验进一步验证。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) gives large language models (LLMs) access to external knowledge, but its conventional retrieve-concatenate-generate pipeline makes retrieval decisions on behalf of the model. As tool use and agent loops become more reliable, an agent can decide whether to retrieve, what to inspect, and when to stop. This shift exposes a new bottleneck: the agent may not know what a knowledge base contains. Traditional knowledge bases expose documents as anonymous text chunks with limited information about scope, purpose, provenance, or relations.
  We propose Knowledge-as-Skill, an organization scheme that makes a knowledge base discoverable, navigable, and self-descriptive. It has three layers: a discovery layer centered on SKILL.md; a navigation layer with one index.md per directory; and a knowledge layer containing documents with YAML frontmatter for topic, type, provenance, and lifecycle. The design follows the Open Knowledge Format (OKF) and the Skill protocol without modifying the agent framework. We also provide knowledge-as-skill, a pipeline for converting heterogeneous collections of PDFs, Word files, web exports, and notes into this structure.
  In a preliminary evaluation on the WixQA enterprise customer-support benchmark, our setup obtains 0.889 Factuality and 0.816 Context Recall, compared with reported Corpus2Skill values of 0.767 and 0.708. It obtains slightly lower Faithfulness, lower Context Precision, and more interaction turns. Because the models, prompts, and knowledge-package construction differ, these results are directional cross-work evidence rather than a controlled comparison.

</details>


### [5] [TailSpec-EASE: Knowledge-Graph-Regularized Linear Recommendation for Web Long-Tail Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26143)
*Jianru Shen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TailSpec-EASE 将关系感知的知识图谱谱先验注入局部闭式重建目标，并按物品流行度自适应调节先验强度，在整体精度、长尾性能与训练成本之间取得良好折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web 平台推荐系统过度推荐热门物品而忽视长尾；物品侧知识图谱可通过共享语义属性连接稀疏物品，但许多强竞争力 KG 推荐器依赖图神经架构，而 EASE-R 等强浅层线性模型忽略侧信息且全局闭式版本可能不可行。

Method: 提出 TailSpec-EASE：把关系感知的谱知识图谱先验注入局部闭式重建目标；先验强度随物品流行度自适应，为长尾物品提供更强的语义引导。

Result: 在四个公开基准及多类基线对比中表现良好；相比无 KG 版本，NDCG@20 最高提升 24%；长尾指标提升在配对 bootstrap 下统计显著，四个数据集中有三个整体 NDCG 显著提升；Amazon-book 计时研究中，CPU 训练仅 37 秒，对照 KGAT GPU 2,584 秒、LightGCN CPU 15,800 秒，且 NDCG@20 与 Tail Recall@20 更高；在全局闭式模型内存不足的目录上仍可行。

Conclusion: TailSpec-EASE 是一种轻量且有效的知识图谱感知推荐方法，能缓解长尾问题，并在精度、长尾性能与训练成本间实现有利权衡，同时保持良好可扩展性。

Abstract: Recommender systems on Web platforms tend to over-serve popular items and neglect the long tail. Item-side knowledge graphs (KGs), often available as linked data or RDF-style Web resources, can help by connecting sparse items through shared semantic attributes. Many competitive KG-aware recommenders rely on graph neural architectures, whereas strong shallow linear models such as EASE-R typically ignore side information and may become infeasible in their global closed-form version. We introduce TailSpec-EASE, a lightweight recommender that injects a relation-aware spectral KG prior into a local closed-form reconstruction objective. The prior strength adapts to item popularity, giving stronger semantic guidance to long-tail items. Across four public benchmarks and a broad set of classical, linear, graph-CF, KG-aware neural, and score-level KG baselines, TailSpec-EASE attains a favorable trade-off between overall accuracy, long-tail performance, and training cost. It improves NDCG@20 by up to 24% over its counterpart without KG information. All tail-metric improvements over the no-KG counterpart are statistically significant under a paired bootstrap, and overall NDCG improves significantly on three of the four datasets. In a representative Amazon-book timing study, TailSpec-EASE trains in 37 seconds on CPU, compared with 2,584 seconds for a GPU-trained KGAT run and 15,800 seconds for CPU LightGCN, while attaining higher NDCG@20 and Tail Recall@20 on that dataset. It also remains feasible on catalogs where the global closed-form model runs out of memory.

</details>


### [6] [Discovery-Driven Integration of Disjoint Tables via Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26658)
*Md Ataur Rahman,Dimitris Sacharidis,Oscar Romero,Sergi Nadal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究"发现驱动集成"任务，提出 LOKI 架构，通过文本中介的连接路径发现，将异构表格整合为带来源的可解释集成表格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据湖中语义相关但缺乏显式连接属性的表格难以整合，非结构化文本可作为连接证据，但现有方法多只能检索粗粒度列-文本关联，或在集成时假设已有行-文本链接、schema 或查询，无法在细粒度上发现行级连接关系。

Method: 形式化"文本中介连接路径发现"任务；提出横向双向交叉注意力架构 LOKI，学习表格行与句子的情境化表示；采用全局表-文本对比目标，在无显式局部监督下让行-句子细粒度关联自动涌现；再将隐式关联转化为显式可解释的连接路径，组织为关系一致的组，并物化为带句子级溯源的类型化集成表格。

Result: 在真实世界基准上持续优于最先进的多模态数据发现方法；物化类型化集成表格取得 0.982 的宏类型对精度；LLM API 成本比直接提示方法低至多 40 倍。

Conclusion: LOKI 能在缺乏显式连接结构的情况下，利用非结构化文本发现细粒度行级连接路径，并高效、可解释地物化为带溯源的类型化集成表格，兼顾效果与成本。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous datasets within data lakes is a critical challenge, particularly for semantically related tables that lack the explicit attributes needed to be joined. We study Discovery-Driven Integration, where the relevant sources and their missing relational structure must be discovered before integration. In this setting, unstructured text provides the evidence that connects otherwise disjoint tables. The fundamental challenge is to discover the relationships at a fine-grained level that connect individual rows from different tables through specific sentences. We formalize this task as Text-Mediated Join Path Discovery and propose a horizontal bidirectional cross-attention architecture called LOKI Latent-space Optimization for Knowledge Integration) that learns contextualized representations of table rows and sentences. Through a global table-text contrastive objective, fine-grained row-sentence associations emerge without explicit local supervision. Existing multi-modal discovery methods largely retrieve coarse-grained column-text associations, whereas integration systems assume supplied row-text links, schemas, or queries. LOKI instead transforms these implicit associations into explicit, interpretable join paths, organizes them into relation-consistent groups, and materializes them as typed integrated tables with sentence-level provenance. Comprehensive evaluations on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that LOKI consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multi-modal data discovery approaches, and materializes typed integrated tables with 0.982 macro typed-pair precision while being up to 40 times cheaper in LLM API cost than direct prompting.

</details>


### [7] [When Concealed Links Cannot Be Recovered: A Structural Identifiability Bound and Evaluation Pitfalls in Offshore Leak Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26171)
*Joseph Bingham*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文论证：离岸泄密数据中"谁实际拥有什么"这类被隐藏的关系，原则上大多无法通过链接预测从公开部分恢复；可恢复性的上限由节点的结构不可区分类大小决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Panama/Paradise Papers 等泄密暴露了庞大的离岸实体网络，一个显然的问题随之而来：这些结构被刻意隐藏的关系（尤其是受益所有权）能否仅凭泄密公开部分通过链接预测恢复？已有研究暗示可以，但作者认为这些分析衡量了错误的东西，因此需要给出一个理论上的可识别性界限来澄清问题。

Method: 提出分布无关的可识别性界（distribution-free identifiability bound）：对任何尊重图同构的恢复规则（涵盖所有拓扑链接预测分数与所有消息传递图神经网络），在观测图中被隔离的隐藏端点与其结构孪生节点可互换，因此其隐藏边无法以高于随机的概率恢复。推广到一般情形：恢复上限由节点结构不可区分类的大小决定，Weisfeiler–Leman 颜色类是可计算的代表，节点度只是粗糙代理。在完整 ICIJ Offshore Leaks 图（814K 实体、84K 标注受益所有权边）上用无分类器、度受控的探针验证，并与训练好的 GNN 对比。

Result: 在完整 ICIJ Offshore Leaks 图上，无分类器、度受控的探针为孤立所有者复现出精确的 0.5 下限，孤立所有者占所有所有者的 20.6%；训练好的图神经网络落到同一下限。恢复能力仅随结构独特性增长而提升，该下限在五个泄密数据集中均重现。此外记录了五种评估陷阱，每种都会让本不可超越的界限看似被超越，并给出规避规则。

Conclusion: 应将研究重点从近乎不可恢复的隐藏委托人，转向隐藏机制本身；并解释了为何融合外部数据的作用远低于预期。

Abstract: Leaks such as the Panama and Paradise Papers expose large networks of offshore entities, and they invite an obvious question for network learning. Can the relations these structures are built to hide---above all, who beneficially owns what---be recovered from the public part of the leak by link prediction? We argue that the answer is mostly no, and that the analyses which suggest otherwise are measuring the wrong thing. Our main result is a distribution-free identifiability bound. For any recovery rule that respects graph isomorphism, and that covers every topological link-prediction score together with every message-passing graph neural network, a concealed endpoint left isolated in the observed graph is interchangeable with its structural twins, so its hidden edge cannot be recovered above chance. Isolation is only the sharpest case. In general the ceiling on recovery is set by the size of a node's structural-indistinguishability class, for which the Weisfeiler--Leman colour class is a computable stand-in, and node degree is at best a loose proxy. On the full ICIJ Offshore Leaks graph (814K entities and 84K labelled beneficial-owner edges) a classifier-free, degree-controlled probe reproduces an exact $0.5$ floor for isolated owners, who make up $20.6\%$ of all owners, and a trained graph neural network lands on the same floor. Recovery climbs only as structural distinctiveness grows, and the floor reappears in every one of the five leaks. Along the way we document five evaluation traps. Each one makes a bound that cannot be beaten look beaten, and we give a short rule that avoids them. The practical upshot is to redirect effort from the hidden principal, which is close to unrecoverable, toward the machinery of concealment, and to spell out why fusing external data helps far less than one would hope.

</details>

*Robert Šamárek,Radek Martinek*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该工作为学术出版网络的结构图分析补充语义层，通过SPECTER2引文嵌入与向量数据库实现语义-结构融合，并在捷克奥斯特拉发理工大学语料上验证了融合信号在不同任务中的互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学术出版网络的结构图分析只能捕捉实体间的拓扑关系，无法感知论文内容本身，因此需要引入语义信息以弥补这一局限。

Method: 用SPECTER2引文嵌入表示科学文献，并以OpenAlex ID为键存入嵌入式向量数据库，使其与图结构层直接关联；定义模块化的后融合函数，将语义相似度（嵌入余弦）与结构相似度（文献耦合）以可调权重alpha组合，alpha值按具体任务选取。

Conclusion: 混合融合并非普遍更优的方法，而是一种根据任务显式调控互补信号的机制；作者将该方法作为apnet库的开源扩展发布，并提供可复现工作流。

Abstract: Structural graph analysis of the academic publishing network captures the topological relationships between entities but does not see the content of works. Building on our structural approach, this work complements it with a semantic layer and a parameterized structural-semantic fusion. We represent scientific documents by citation-informed vector embeddings (SPECTER2) and store them in an embedded vector database keyed by the stable OpenAlex ID, so that they connect directly to the graph layer. We define a modular late-fusion function that combines semantic similarity (cosine of embeddings) and structural similarity (bibliographic coupling) with a tunable weight alpha whose value is chosen according to the specific task. On the corpus of VSB - Technical University of Ostrava we show two things: citation-informed embeddings agree with the expert OpenAlex topical taxonomy better than a TF-IDF baseline, and in a recommendation use case the structural, semantic, and combined signals carry information in different regimes depending on the available data. Hybrid fusion here is not a universally better method but an explicit mechanism for steering complementary signals according to the task. We release the whole approach as an open-source extension of the apnet library with a reproducible workflow.

</details>

*Minhan Cho,Soyoung Park,Daejin Choi,Jinyoung Han*

Main category: cs.IR

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 包含 CLAIM（claim）成分的生成、使用句向量模型进行检索、对检索到的科学文献进行分类和摘要以提取支持该 claim 的信息、最后将 claim 相关的 web 搜索结果与科学文献提取出的 evidence 一起注入到最终生成步的 prompt 中，并在其中调用两个检索和分类的科学文献/网页证据抽取模型，以辅助生成 claim evidence 和精确引用，最终通过包含科学文献证据抽取和 web 证据抽取的信息来对 claim 进行回答。管（原 text 拆不开）

Result: In the official test set, the best F1 was 0.177, while the cross-validation F1 was 0.311. Under leakage-free protocols, threshold transfer costs 0.007 F1, decision quality is flat across chronological quartiles, and official test queries are not measurably farther from the training manifold than training queries are from each other. Misses split exactly evenly between never-retrieved candidates and retrieved-but-low-ranked candidates. BM25 length-normalisation tuning, an event-triple view, and full-content dense fusion lift top-200 recall by three to seven points; citation-graph features add 0.014 F1 over eight seeds once own-citation leakage is removed; per-query cutoff rules, zero-shot reranker swap, and a date filter do not help. Four evaluation artifacts were documented, each reversing a result once the protocol was corrected.

Conclusion: 性能差距并非主要由阈值转移或时间分布偏移造成；遗漏可等分为“未召回”和“召回但排名过低”两类。有效改进包括 BM25 长度归一化调参、事件三元组视图、全文稠密融合（提升 top-200 召回 3–7 个点），以及去除自身引用泄漏后的引文图特征（提升 0.014 F1）。同时记录了四个评估伪影，每个都会在协议修正后反转结果，强调严格评估协议的重要性。

Abstract: We present the ABAI submission to COLIEE 2026 Task 1, case law retrieval, together with a controlled study of why it underperformed. The task suppresses the cited passages themselves, which removes much of the lexical overlap a retriever would rely on. Our pipeline answers this with four independently trained stages: multi-view BM25 over citation-context windows with reciprocal rank fusion, neural reranking, graph-based features from entity communities and a graph attention network, and a LightGBM meta-learner over 34 features. Our best run reached F1=0.177 on the official test set, against a cross-validated 0.311, and we attributed that gap to a recall ceiling, temporal distribution shift, and threshold miscalibration. We then tested all three. Under leakage-free protocols threshold transfer costs 0.007 F1, decision quality is flat across chronological quartiles, and the official test queries are not measurably farther from the training manifold than training queries are from each other, in two independent embedding spaces. Decomposing the misses instead splits them exactly evenly between candidates never retrieved and candidates retrieved but ranked below the cut. Measuring the remedies for each half, BM25 length-normalisation tuning, an event-triple view, and full-content dense fusion lift top-200 recall by three to seven points, and citation-graph features add 0.014 F1 over eight seeds once own-citation leakage is removed, while per-query cutoff rules, a zero-shot reranker swap, and a date filter do not help. We also document four evaluation artifacts, each of which reversed a result once the protocol was corrected.

</details>


### [10] [Which Reranking Conclusions Survive the Answer Interface? A Prospective Finite-Orbit Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26250)
*Wenzhang Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文前瞻性审计了在读者答案接口变化下，BM25与BGE-v2-m3的检索策略效应、排序及选择价值是否稳定，发现六项确认性设置均未出现超过预设阈值0.015的接口变异，也无排序反转认证，表明不应笼统宣称复制性不稳定，但亦未证明普遍不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reranker日益通过下游语言模型答案来评估，这引出一个检索测量问题：如果只改变读者的答案接口，关于BM25与BGE-v2-m3的结论是否仍然一致。作者希望厘清检索评估中策略层面、接口稳定性、策略排序与选择价值的区别。

Method: 在RAGuard和FEVER上，使用四个读者，对BM25与BGE-v2-m3的claim-paired效应进行前瞻性审计。固定检索策略、证据、claim与上下文深度，通过语义到标签绑定、A/B与X/Y词表、选项顺序构造八种任务等价接口。检验估计的检索策略效应、其排序及选择价值是否变化。

Result: 六项确认性设置均未显示超过预设0.015实质性阈值的统计认证接口变异，也无BM25-BGE排序的认证反转。某一环境中选择器分歧达33.5%，但八个环境中均未建立预设的实质性held-out价值差异。五项设置不确定性过高，未能满足预设的高阶等价条件。

Conclusion: 结果不支持广泛的复制性不稳定，但也不能证明普遍不变性。检索评估应区分策略层面、接口稳定性、策略排序与选择价值。当稳定性未经验证时，宜对枚举接口取均匀平均并给出显式变异边界，以避免偏袒某一接口。

Abstract: Rerankers are increasingly evaluated through downstream language-model answers. This raises a retrieval-measurement question: if only the reader's answer interface changes, should we reach the same conclusion about BM25 versus BGE-v2-m3? We prospectively audit their claim-paired effect on RAGuard and FEVER with four readers. Retrieval policies, evidence, claims, and context depth remain fixed while semantic-to-label binding, A/B versus X/Y vocabulary, and option order form eight task-equivalent interfaces. We ask whether the estimated retrieval-policy effect, its ordering, or selection value changes. None of the six confirmatory settings showed statistically certified interface variation above the prespecified 0.015 materiality threshold, and none showed a certified reversal of the BM25-BGE ordering. Selector disagreement reaches 33.5% in one environment, yet none of eight environments establishes the prespecified material held-out value difference. These results do not support broad replicated instability, but they do not prove universal invariance: five settings remain too uncertain to satisfy the prespecified higher-order equivalence condition. They show why retrieval evaluations should separate policy level, interface stability, policy ordering, and selection value. When stability is unverified, a uniform average over the enumerated interfaces with explicit variation bounds avoids privileging one interface.

</details>


### [11] [When Does Permutation Instability Generalize? Independent-View Validation for Listwise LLM Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26251)
*Wenzhang Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 有限视角的不稳定性诊断若与验证统计量共享测量视角，会产生部分-整体关联，从而高估对未见排列的预测能力；只有观测-不相交的验证目标才能隔离预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 列表式语言模型重排器在等价候选排列间常出现不一致，研究者用有限不稳定性诊断来支持额外采样、聚合或选择性计算；但若验证统计量复用了探测视角，这种关联可能仅由共享测量导致，而非真正预测未见排列的信息。

Method: 推导精确的有限视角分解，前瞻性地比较零、一、两个复用视角，并设置完全不相交的四视角目标；在两个固定7B模型族和两个推荐数据集上，用受控列表做有符号离线分析，用未触碰的检索器列表做无目标复现。

Result: 四个受控块中，完全不相交相关性较弱或异质（-0.061至0.281），复用两个探测视角则为0.600至0.718；所有配对对比很大（0.436至0.661）且Holm显著。未触碰列表的四个块重叠效应均为正。探测从两视角增至四视角仅在一个块明显提升不相交可靠性。探测能预测聚合移动幅度（Spearman rho=0.142至0.426），但不能预测稳定的有符号目标收益；12个固定比例探测路由点中有7个在实测成本下被严格支配。

Conclusion: 当目标估计量是关于未见扰动行为的预测信息时，验证目标必须与探测观测-不相交才能隔离该信息；有符号效用和成本敏感决策仍是独立问题。

Abstract: Listwise language-model rerankers often disagree across equivalent candidate permutations. Finite instability diagnostics are therefore used to motivate additional sampling, aggregation, or selective computation. But an association with a validation statistic that reuses the probe views need not isolate predictive information about unseen permutations. Shared measurements can induce classical part-whole association. We study how this affects claims that a finite-view instability score predicts unseen permutations. We derive the exact finite-view decomposition and prospectively compare zero, one, and two reused views, including a fully disjoint four-view target.
  The study covers two pinned 7B model families and two recommendation datasets, with controlled lists for signed offline analysis and untouched retriever lists for target-free replication. On the four controlled blocks, fully disjoint correlations are weak or heterogeneous (-0.061 to 0.281), whereas reusing both probe views yields 0.600 to 0.718; all paired contrasts are large (0.436 to 0.661) and Holm-significant. The overlap effect is positive in all four untouched-list blocks. Increasing the probe from two to four views clearly improves disjoint reliability in only one block. Moreover, the probe predicts aggregation-movement magnitude (Spearman rho = 0.142 to 0.426) but not stable signed target benefit, and 7 of 12 fixed-fraction probe-routing points are strictly dominated at measured cost. Thus, when the intended estimand is predictive information about unseen perturbation behavior, validation targets must be observation-disjoint from the probe to isolate that information; signed utility and cost-sensitive decisions remain separate questions.

</details>

### [12] [CogenPVG: Cognitive-Enhanced Reflective Multi-Agent Framework for Persuasive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25821)
*Yuntian Xiao,Shoulong Zhang,Wenfeng Song,Yan Wang,Yi Chen,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出CogenPVG，一个认知增强的反思式多智能体框架，用于说服性视频生成（PVG）任务，通过四阶段流程与基于ELM理论的双智能体反思机制，实现了当前最佳的说服效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 说服性视频生成（PVG）是一个有价值但研究不足的课题。尽管多模态内容生成取得显著进展，AI自动创建具有人类制作水平且具备强说服力的视频仍是巨大挑战；且现有工作多局限于商业用途，缺乏通用说服主题的研究。

Method: 提出CogenPVG框架，将生成流程解耦为四个连续阶段：论点推理、故事板规划、素材创建、后期编辑，模拟人类视频制作工作流。每个阶段配备一对生成器与评论家智能体，采用基于说服心理学理论——精细加工可能性模型（ELM）的反思式优化方案。论点推理阶段在批判性思维理论指导下生成高逻辑性与可信度的推理思路，通过ELM中心路径实现认知增强；其余三阶段生成并优化多模态素材，在启发式理论指导下组装为说服性视频，作为ELM的外围路径。

Result: 大量实验与全面分析表明，CogenPVG框架取得了最佳的说服效果。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，CogenPVG是首个聚焦于通用说服主题、不局限于商业用途的工作，验证了所提出的多智能体框架在PVG任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Persuasive video generation (PVG) is a valuable yet under-explored research topic. Despite the significant advances in multimodal content generation, AI-empowered automated creation of human-made-like videos with substantial persuasiveness remains a formidable challenge. In this paper, we propose CogenPVG, a novel Cognitive-Enhanced reflective multi-agent framework tailored for Persuasive Video Generation task. Given the topic and stance from the user, we decouple the sophisticated generation process into four sequential stages: argument reasoning, storyboard planning, asset creation, and post-editing, imitating the workflow of human video producers. To ensure high persuasiveness, each stage is equipped with a pair of generator and critic agents, following a reflective refinement scheme grounded in a solid psychological theory of persuasion, the Elaboration Likelihood Model (ELM). In the argument reasoning stage, we generate highly logical and credible reasoning thoughts under the guidance of critical thinking theory, enabling cognitive enhancement via the central route of the ELM. For the other three stages, we generate and optimize multimodal assets, assembling them into a persuasive video guided by theories of heuristics, as the peripheral route of the ELM. To the best of our knowledge, CogenPVG is the first work focused on general persuasive topics, without being confined to commercial purposes. Extensive experiments and comprehensive analysis demonstrate that our framework achieves the best persuasion performance, thereby proving the effectiveness of our proposed multi-agent framework for the PVG task.

</details>


### [13] [TV-AudioRemover: Joint Text-Visual Guided Sound Removal with Multi-Task Hard-Mixture Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25864)
*Xinyue Guo,Jianxuan Yang,Daiguo Zhou,Jiagao Hu,Yuxuan Chen,Fei Wang,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出 TV-AudioRemover，一个结合视觉编辑后视频与自然语言指令的目标声音移除框架，用于消除视频中已移除物体残留的声音，并构建百万级数据集与 AV-Remove-Bench 基准，取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉物体移除后，声音痕迹仍残留在音轨中，造成明显的音视频不一致。现有视频修复模型仅处理像素，而声音移除模型多由文本驱动，依赖单一模态控制，缺乏多模态引导所提供的语义基础与时间同步线索，效果有限。

Method: 提出 TV-AudioRemover 框架，利用视觉编辑后的视频与自然语言指令共同抑制原始音频混合中与被移除物体相关的声音。构建百万级单物体音视频对齐数据集，并合成混合-目标配对用于训练。模型架构引入任务 token、可泛化指令建模与模态专属全局引导；采用多任务训练增强任务角色理解，并在微调阶段使用难混合课程学习（语义相似音频混合）以提升细粒度声源区分能力。

Result: 提出 AV-Remove-Bench 音视频物体移除基准，配套专用客观指标与基于 MLLM 的评估协议。实验表明，该方法在主客观指标上均达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: TV-AudioRemover 通过视觉与文本多模态引导有效解决视觉物体移除后的残留声音问题，并配套数据集与基准推动该方向发展。

Abstract: Visual object removal can eliminate a target from video frames, yet its acoustic trace persists in the soundtrack, causing obvious audio-visual inconsistency. Existing video inpainting models operate solely on pixels, while audio editing models, especially for the sound removal task, are typically driven by text and therefore rely on limited single-modal control, which is less effective than multimodal guidance that provides stronger semantic grounding and temporal synchronization cues. In this paper, we present Text-Visual Guided Sound Removal (TV-AudioRemover), a target sound removal framework that leverages the visually edited video together with a natural-language instruction to suppress the sound associated with the removed visual object from the original audio mixture. To acquire high-quality training data, we devise a pipeline to construct a million-scale dataset of single-object audio-visual aligned samples, from which we synthesize mixture-target pairs customized for model training. To effectively leverage visual context and follow instruction intent, we augment the model architecture with task tokens, generalizable instruction modeling, and modality-specific global guidance. We further adopt multi-task training to strengthen task-role comprehension, and employ a hard-mixture curriculum that leverages semantically similar acoustic mixtures during fine-tuning to enhance fine-grained source discrimination. To support evaluation, we present AV-Remove-Bench, a comprehensive audio-visual object removal benchmark, along with dedicated objective metrics and an MLLM-based evaluation protocol. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on both subjective and objective metrics. Project page: https://yjx-research.github.io/TV-AudioRemover/.

</details>


### [14] [KeyBound: Keyed and Host-Bound Learned Audio Watermarking for Speech Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26235)
*Bangshuo Zhu,Yuxin Cao,Weifei Jin,Fusen Guo,Huadong Mo,Jingling Xue,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: KeyBound 提出一种带密钥、宿主受限的学习式音频水印，恢复经典水印的密钥与宿主感知载波机制，实现检测公开、解码需密钥，并能抵抗去噪、重合成与载体移植攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式音频水印仅在噪声、压缩、滤波、重采样等固定失真目录下评估载荷恢复，这不足以支撑溯源：水印不应被未授权方读取，不应可移植到无关音频，也不应在被现代生成模型重合成后消失。

Method: KeyBound 用密钥对载荷做掩码，并通过由宿主冻结频谱表示调制的载波嵌入掩码比特，使密钥控制载荷访问、宿主条件载波抵抗直接移植；同时使用与密钥无关的存在性检测头，使任何人可检测水印，但仅密钥持有者可读取归属信息。

Result: 在 LibriSpeech 上对比 WavMark、AudioSeal 和 Timbre，在导致所有基线检测失效的频谱去噪器下，KeyBound 保持 1.00 检测准确率和 0.98 比特准确率；无密钥时解码接近随机，且拒绝移植载体。检测能力可迁移到未见过的 DAC 与 BigVGAN 重合成，但精确载荷恢复会下降。单样本均匀性假设下，错误密钥解码通过验证规则的概率至多 2.1×10⁻³。

Conclusion: 语音溯源更适合被建模为带密钥、宿主绑定的归属问题，而非在预先固定的信号失真目录下恢复载荷。

Abstract: Audio watermarking is a proactive route to attributing synthetic speech to its source. Learned audio watermarks are typically judged by payload recovery after a fixed catalog of signal distortions such as noise, compression, filtering, and resampling. That test is necessary but not sufficient for provenance. A mark offered as evidence of origin should not be readable by an unauthorized party, should not be transferable to unrelated audio, and should not vanish when the recording is re-synthesized by a modern generative model. We present KeyBound, a learned audio watermark that restores the two ingredients classical watermarking supplied and learned schemes set aside, a secret key and a host-aware carrier. KeyBound masks the payload with a secret key and embeds the masked bits through a carrier modulated by a frozen spectral representation of the host, so the key governs payload access while the host-conditioned carrier resists direct transplantation. A key-independent presence head lets any party detect a mark, whereas only a key holder reads its attribution, and under the single-sample uniformity assumption a wrong-key decode clears our verification rule with probability at most $2.1\times10^{-3}$. On LibriSpeech against WavMark, AudioSeal, and Timbre, KeyBound holds 1.00 detection accuracy and 0.98 bit accuracy under a spectral denoiser that costs every baseline its detection, decodes at chance without the key, and rejects transplanted carriers. Detection further transfers to held-out DAC and BigVGAN re-synthesis, though exact payload recovery degrades. Speech provenance is thus better posed as a keyed, host-bound attribution problem than as the recovery of a payload under a catalog of signal distortions fixed in advance.

</details>


### [15] [ROAM-ASD: Robust Open-World Active Speaker Detection with Flexible Multimodal Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26648)
*Pu Wang,Yujun Wang,Hugo Van hamme*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: ROAM-ASD是一个鲁棒的视听说话人检测框架，联合建模音频、全脸和细粒度嘴部表示，在五个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主动说话人检测（ASD）系统在困难场景或不完整观测（如缺少某一模态输入）下性能下降，需要更可靠的视听关联能力。

Method: 提出ROAM-ASD框架，联合建模音频、全脸及细粒度嘴部表示；采用统一联合自注意力机制，配以模态无关的查询token，使各可用模态输入直接交互；并引入模态dropout增强输入缺失时的鲁棒性。

Result: 在五个ASD基准上达到最优：WASD 98.8% mAP、UniTalk 87.9%、AVA 96.5%、ASW 99.3%、Talkies 98.2%，较此前最优分别提升5.1、4.7、0.9、1.0、2.1 mAP；同时显著提升零样本跨数据集泛化能力，并对观测缺失保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: ROAM-ASD通过多粒度视听联合建模与模态dropout，显著提升ASD在多种基准和跨域场景下的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Active speaker detection (ASD) requires reliable association between visible faces and acoustic speech, yet existing systems often degrade under challenging domains or incomplete observations. We introduce ROAM-ASD, a robust audiovisual framework that jointly models audio, full-face, and fine-grained mouth representations. A unified joint self-attention mechanism processes all input streams together with modality-agnostic query tokens, enabling direct interaction among available modality inputs. Modality dropout further improves robustness when input streams are unavailable. ROAM-ASD achieves state-of-the-art performance across five ASD benchmarks: 98.8% mAP on WASD, 87.9% on UniTalk, 96.5% on AVA, 99.3% on ASW, and 98.2% on Talkies, improving over previous best systems by 5.1, 4.7, 0.9, 1.0, and 2.1 mAP points, respectively. ROAM-ASD also substantially improves zero-shot cross-dataset generalization and remains robust to missing observations.

</details>

### [16] [Cloud, Edge, or Split? Profiling Onboard and Split Vision-Language Model Deployment for Drone AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25415)
*Zoha Azimi,Reza Farahani,Schahram Dustdar,Christian Timmerer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文以SmolVLM-256M为代表性轻量级视觉语言模型，系统对比了完全机载、云端与分割计算三种部署范式，发现不存在普适最优策略，最佳选择取决于网络条件与输入图像分辨率的交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）可让无人机等边缘设备利用自然语言指令理解复杂环境，但落地部署面临困境：机载推理受限于算力、内存和能耗，云端推理则带来通信时延、带宽开销和网络依赖。分割计算被视为潜在替代方案，但针对轻量级VLM，三种架构的性能权衡尚未被系统性地量化和剖析。

Method: 采用SmolVLM-256M作为代表性轻量级VLM，对完全机载、云端以及分割计算三种部署范式进行基准测试，在不同图像分辨率和网络条件下，量化其推理时延、计算资源利用率、通信开销与能耗。

Result: 结果表明没有任何一种部署策略是普遍最优的；更优策略取决于网络条件与输入图像分辨率之间的交互关系。

Conclusion: 面向无人机等边缘设备的轻量级VLM部署需要根据实际网络状况和图像分辨率自适应地选择部署方式，而非固定采用单一架构。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable edge devices like unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to interpret visual observations and reason about complex environments using natural-language instructions. However, their practical deployment remains challenging as onboard inference is constrained by limited computational, memory, and energy resources, whereas cloud-based inference introduces communication latency, bandwidth overhead, and dependence on network connectivity. To address these limitations, split computing offers a promising alternative by partitioning VLM inference between the resource-constrained UAVs and more capable remote servers. However, the performance trade-offs among fully onboard, cloud-based, and split-computing architectures for lightweight VLMs have not yet been systematically profiled. This paper benchmarks these three deployment paradigms using SmolVLM-256M as a representative lightweight VLM. We quantify their inference latency, computational resource utilization, communication overhead, and energy consumption across varying image resolutions and network conditions. Our results show that no deployment strategy is universally optimal; instead, the preferred strategy depends on the interaction between network conditions and input image resolution.

</details>


### [17] [WeightBridge: An Efficient Weight Transfer Library for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25442)
*Xuanlin Jiang,Samuel Hsia,Michael Kuchnik,Zachary DeVito,Minlan Yu,Carole-Jean Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: WeightBridge 是一个灵活高效的权重传输库，通过自动提取训练器与 rollout 权重布局对应关系，实现无冗余、负载均衡的权重传输，在多种 RL 配置下将 GPU 停顿时间最多降低 42 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 LLM 强化学习系统中，权重传输（将更新后的参数从训练器传播到 rollout 生成器）正成为重要的性能瓶颈。核心挑战在于：需在不牺牲效率的前提下，支持现代 RL 工作负载中多样化的训练器/rollout 布局与同步需求。现有方案仅在部分配置下高效，其他配置下性能差或缺乏支持。

Method: WeightBridge 首先自动提取训练器与 rollout 权重布局之间的对应关系，然后规划并执行无冗余且负载均衡的权重传输。它暴露一个小而通用的 API，同时协调不同同步模式下的工作节点。

Result: 在涵盖不同模型、并行化布局和同步模式的配置中，WeightBridge 相比最先进的开源 RL 框架，将平均 GPU 停顿时间最多降低 42 倍，并在所有设置下均达到高性能。一个编码智能体能够在无人工指导的情况下将 WeightBridge 集成到两个不同的 RL 框架中，证明了其 API 的通用性与易用性。

Conclusion: WeightBridge 提供了一种跨多样 RL 配置均高效、通用且易集成的权重传输方案，有效缓解了 LLM 强化学习系统中的权重传输瓶颈。

Abstract: Weight transfer - the propagation of updated parameters from trainers to rollout generators - is becoming an important performance bottleneck in reinforcement learning (RL) systems for LLMs. The central challenge is supporting the diverse trainer and rollout layouts and synchronization requirements of modern RL workloads without sacrificing efficiency. Existing solutions are efficient under some configurations but perform poorly or lack support under others. We present WeightBridge, a flexible, efficient weight-transfer library designed to deliver high performance across diverse RL configurations. WeightBridge first automatically extracts the correspondence between trainer and rollout weight layouts, then plans and executes redundancy-free and load-balanced weight transfer. It exposes a small, general API while coordinating workers across diverse synchronization modes. Across configurations spanning different models, parallelization layouts, and synchronization modes, WeightBridge reduces average GPU stall time by up to 42$\times$ over the state-of-the-art open-source RL framework and achieves high performance in all settings. A coding agent was able to integrate WeightBridge into two different RL frameworks without manual guidance, demonstrating the generality and ease of use of its APIs.

</details>


### [18] [Fast Recovery for LLM Serving via Decoupled Device Memory Lifetime in Dynamo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25451)
*Schwinn Saereesitthipitak,Mohammed Abdulwahhab,Hannah Zhang,Dan Feigin,Neelay Shah,Maksim Khadkevich,Itay Neeman,Vikram Sharma Mailthody,Wen-mei W. Hwu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于"保留已初始化引擎状态"原则的Dynamo快速恢复方案，通过GMS解耦设备内存所有权，实现故障副本7秒内恢复，比热重启快13-29倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理副本在紧耦合GPU上连续数周服务，硬件与软件故障不可避免，单worker故障即可中断整个副本。恢复需重新初始化引擎，即使权重与编译产物已缓存也需数分钟，生产部署只能通过超额配置服务容量来掩盖这一窗口。作者认为主要成本是就绪服务容量的损失而非请求进度，因此恢复应保留已初始化的引擎状态而非重建它。

Method: 1) 快照捕获一次已初始化引擎并加以恢复而非重新初始化；2) 基于18周Dynamo集群故障数据发现大多数故障是"设备保留型"：引擎进程失败但GPU及其驻留分配完好；3) 允许独立引擎进程复用同一GPU驻留状态，同时保持可变执行状态私有；4) 提出GPU Memory Service (GMS)，将设备内存所有权与引擎进程解耦，使引擎可共享并重新挂接存活的分配而无需拷贝；5) GMS保留模型权重并在替换引擎与Shadow Engine之间只读共享，避免权重重载；6) 在同一GPU上运行第二个已初始化runtime，将恢复简化为"提升(promotion)"。

Result: 在vLLM和SGLang上的四个模型中，这些机制可在7秒内恢复失败副本，比热重启快13-29倍；每GPU使用固定4-8 GiB设备内存，且与模型规模无关；回放生产trace估计可挽回79%因恢复损失的GPU-hours。

Conclusion: 通过GPU Memory Service解耦内存所有权并保留引擎初始化状态，Dynamo可在设备保留型故障下实现秒级恢复，显著减少服务容量损失与GPU资源浪费，且设备内存开销固定、不随模型规模增长。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference replicas run across tightly coupled GPUs and serve traffic continuously for weeks. Hardware and software failures are therefore inevitable, and one worker failure can disrupt an entire replica. Recovery requires reinitializing the engine, taking minutes even when weights and compilation artifacts are cached. Production deployments overprovision serving capacity to mask this window. We argue that the dominant cost is loss of ready serving capacity, not request progress, so recovery should preserve initialized engine state rather than reconstruct it. We present fast recovery for Dynamo based on this principle. Snapshots capture an initialized engine once and restore it instead of reinitializing it. Analysis of 18 weeks of failures from the Dynamo cluster shows that most failures are device-preserving: the engine process fails while the GPU and its resident allocations remain intact. Our key insight is that independent engine processes can reuse the same GPU-resident state while keeping mutable execution state private. The GPU Memory Service (GMS) decouples device-memory ownership from engine processes, enabling engines to share and reattach surviving allocations without copying them. GMS preserves model weights and shares them read-only between replacement and Shadow Engines, avoiding weight reloads. A second initialized runtime on the same GPUs reduces recovery to promotion. Across four models on vLLM and SGLang, these mechanisms recover a failed replica in under 7 seconds, 13-29 times faster than a warm restart, using a fixed 4-8 GiB of device memory per GPU independent of model size. Replaying the production trace, we estimate they would reclaim 79% of GPU-hours lost to recovery.

</details>


### [19] [Adaptive and Cost-Efficient Joint Scheduling of UAV Routes and Analytics with Transit-Borne Fog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25479)
*Suman Raj,Arindam Khanda,Gagana M D,Yogesh Simmhan,Sajal K. Das*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将公共巴士视为"移动雾节点"：无人机在停靠时把数据交给巴士，巴士沿固定线路行驶直到进入蜂窝覆盖区再回传。针对该模型提出任务调度问题并设计 Divide and Assign (DA) 启发式，在真实蜂窝与公交数据上的 36 种负载配置中显著提升效用、降低成本并保持高完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农村地区蜂窝基站稀疏，无人机在执行有截止时间约束的分析任务时无法可靠卸载负载；而巴士具有固定路线与时刻表，可作为可预测的移动中继，弥补通信空白。

Method: 将公共巴士建模为移动雾节点，无人机在停靠时移交数据。形式化定义 Mission Scheduling Problem，联合调度无人机路线与每个分析任务的放置位置（无人机边缘、固定雾节点或巴士），并满足截止时间、能量与成本约束。提出 Divide and Assign (DA) 启发式，选择仍能满足任务截止时间的最低成本停靠点；并给出应对交通波动的自适应变体。

Result: 基于真实蜂窝与公交数据的农村区域 36 种负载配置中，DA 比最强启发式效用高最多 20%，比最强改编先验调度器高最多 41%，且总成本最低。公交层承担最多 71% 的数据交付，任务完成率提升至 100%，充电循环减少最多 31%。存在交通波动时，自适应变体可挽回 93% 因延误损失的效用，完成率保持 97% 以上。

Conclusion: 以公共巴士作为移动雾节点可有效扩展农村无人机分析的通信与计算能力，DA 及其自适应变体在效用、成本、完成率与能耗方面均优于现有方案，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) performing deadline-bound analytics over large rural areas cannot reliably offload workloads to sparse cellular base stations. We propose an approach that uses scheduled public buses as \textit{mobile fogs}: a UAV hands off data to a bus during a halt, and the bus carries it until its route enters cellular coverage. Because a public bus follows a fixed route and timetable, a handover depends on \textit{where} and \textit{when} the bus next reaches a cellular zone, rather than how near the stop is. We formulate a Mission Scheduling Problem over this model, co-scheduling UAV routes with the placement of each analytics task on the UAV edge, a stationary fog, or a bus, under deadline, energy, and cost constraints. Our \textit{Divide and Assign} (DA) heuristic selects the cheapest halt that still meets a task's deadline. Across 36 workload configurations in a rural region, derived from real cellular and transit data, DA achieves up to 20\% higher utility than the strongest heuristic and up to 41\% higher utility than the strongest adapted-prior scheduler, while incurring the lowest aggregate cost. The transit tier handles up to 71\% of drop-offs, raises task completion to 100\%, and reduces recharging cycles by up to 31\%. Finally, in the presence of traffic variability, the adaptive variant recovers 93\% of the utility the delays cost and maintains completion rates above 97\%.

</details>


### [20] [Co-Fabric: Breaking Host-Domain Boundaries for Unified xPU Interconnection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25560)
*Zhen Peng,Jiaming Huang,Chaofan Chen,Zhao Zhang,An Wu,Baoyang Liu,Xinglong Wang,Tanlong Ci,Jinfeng Li,Xueke Duan,Hao Wang,Xi Chen,Shunshun Zhang,Zhiyuan Su,Zhu Cao,Zhichong Dou,Shaohua Wu,Lu Jing,Yue Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Co-Fabric——一种基于总线的跨主机域统一xPU互连架构，通过精简四层协议栈、跨域P2P路由和统一地址空间，显著提升大规模AI集群的通信效率并降低功耗与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型参数量已超出单xPU容量，需跨多主机域分散部署，导致xPU间通信成为系统效率瓶颈。现有scale-up互连方案存在不足：基于以太网的RoCE方案引入特定消息语义与协议栈开销，且依赖碎片化的按主机寻址；传统基于主机的总线则局限于单一主机域，缺乏跨主机统一寻址能力。

Method: Co-Fabric是一种突破主机域边界的总线式互连，包含三项贡献：（1）精简的四层协议栈，实现纳秒级处理延迟与原生可靠性；（2）跨域扩展与P2P机制，利用包头内嵌端口标识进行路由；（3）基于影子设备自动枚举构建的统一地址空间。

Result: 在64-xPU 3D-Mesh系统上，Co-Fabric相较RoCE将节点间通信延迟降低50%以上，带宽提升2-5倍，DeepSeek R1推理加速30%-80%；互连成本降低最高80%，功耗降低5%。

Conclusion: Co-Fabric凭借精简协议栈和更高的数据通信效率，相较以太网RoCE大幅减少协议与处理开销，验证了其面向AI计算中心的规模扩展优势。

Abstract: Large-model parameters have grown beyond the capacity of a single xPU, dispersing across multiple xPUs spanning distinct host domains, where xPU-to-xPU communication dominates overall system efficiency. Existing scale-up interconnect remains inadequate: network-based solutions built on Ethernet--such as RoCE (RDMA over Converged Ethernet)--introduce specific message-semantics and protocol-stack characteristics, and rely on fragmented per-host addressing, while conventional host-based fabrics are confined to a single host domain and lack cross-host unified addressing. This paper presents Co-Fabric, a bus-based interconnect that, unlike conventional bus designs, breaks host-domain boundaries to deliver unified xPU interconnection for scale-up superpods. Co-Fabric makes three contributions: a streamlined four-layer protocol stack achieving nanosecond-scale processing latency with native reliability; a cross-domain scaling and P2P mechanism that routes using port identifiers embedded in the packet header; and a unified address space built on shadow-device auto-enumeration. On a 64-xPU 3D-Mesh system, Co-Fabric cuts inter-node communication latency by over 50% and improves bandwidth by 2-5x over RoCE, accelerating DeepSeek R1 inference by 30%-80%. Moreover, since its streamlined four-layer protocol stack and higher data-communication efficiency reduce protocol and processing overhead relative to the Ethernet-based RoCE stack, Co-Fabric cuts the cost and power of the interconnect itself by up to 80% and 5%, respectively. These results demonstrate Co-Fabric's advantage for AI computing centers.

</details>


### [21] [Hydrozoan: Latency-Adaptive DAG Consensus under Mixed Byzantine and Crash Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25918)
*Qianyu Yu,Lefteris Kokoris-Kogias,Alberto Sonnino*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Hydrozoan 是首个在混合故障模型下具备双提交路径的 DAG 共识协议，可在两或三个消息延迟内提交，并在同一 DAG 上通过分级间接规则统一两条路径，兼顾高吞吐与低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DAG 共识协议虽能实现高吞吐和最优三消息延迟，但两延迟协议要么降低容错能力（需 5f+1 类委员会），要么依赖回退机制而牺牲 DAG 的高吞吐。因此需要一种既能保持高吞吐、又能在地理分布式环境下灵活实现两延迟提交的方案。

Method: 提出 Hydrozoan 协议，在 f 个拜占庭节点与 c 个崩溃节点的混合故障模型下，基于 n = 3f+c+2p+1 个验证者，采用双提交路径：当故障节点不超过 p 时两个消息延迟提交，否则三个延迟提交，无需额外消息、视图切换，且每轮可有多个领导者。两条路径在同一 DAG 上评估，使用新颖的分级间接规则协调，确保所有诚实验证者达成一致决策。此外提出 Optimal-Hydrozoan 变体，在快速路径上多容忍一个故障，首次匹配已知下界。

Result: 在地理分布式评估中，Hydrozoan 的吞吐量与 Mysticeti 相当；当快速仲裁集位于快速区域时可快约 25% 提交；当快速仲裁集不匹配或超过 p 个故障时，回退到三个消息延迟提交，而现有两延迟协议在此情况下会停滞。两条路径的安全性与活性均已在 Lean 4 中机器验证。

Conclusion: Hydrozoan 通过 (f, c, p) 参数将快速仲裁集部署到所需位置，实现两消息延迟提交，并在配置不当或故障超限时仍能以三消息延迟提交，兼具灵活性与鲁棒性。其 Optimal-Hydrozoan 变体首次达到已知下界，为 DAG 共识在延迟与容错之间提供了更优的权衡。

Abstract: DAG-based consensus protocols can achieve great throughput and the optimal three-message-delay limit for n = 3f+1 consensus. While two-delay protocols exist, they pay with reduced resilience (requiring 5f+1-style committees) or rely on fallbacks that sacrifice the DAG's high throughput. This paper introduces Hydrozoan, the first DAG protocol with a dual commit path under a hybrid fault model of f Byzantine and c crashed validators, on n = 3f+c+2p+1 validators. Leaders commit in two message delays whenever at most p validators are faulty, and in three otherwise, with no extra messages, no view changes, and multiple leaders per round. Both paths are evaluated on the same DAG, using a novel graded indirect rule to reconcile them so that every honest validator reaches the same decision. We show that under geo-distributed conditions, which path is faster is a property of geography rather than the protocol, as rounds reaching a remote region cost far more than those that do not. The (f, c, p) knobs place the fast quorum where the deployment requires it, allowing a commit in two message delays. If misconfigured, Hydrozoan can still commit in three message delays: Hydrozoan commits on whichever path fires first. We also present Optimal-Hydrozoan, a variant that tolerates one more fault on the fast path, the first construction to match the known lower bound. The safety and liveness of both protocols are machine-checked in Lean 4. Our geo-distributed evaluation shows that Hydrozoan matches Mysticeti's throughput, commits ~25% faster when the fast quorum fits fast regions, and falls back to three message delays when it does not or past p faults, where existing two-delay protocols stall.

</details>


### [22] [Distributed Near-Equitable Coloring in the LOCAL Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26190)
*Amit Nir,David Peleg*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究LOCAL模型中近似均衡染色问题，证明确定性算法下精确平衡本质上是全局的（在环$C_n$上需$\Omega(n)$轮），而粗略平衡可在与直径无关的时间内本地实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知的均衡$(\Delta+1)$-染色松弛算法沿生成结构的聚合代价随直径$D$缩放。作者希望理解精确均衡与近似均衡在分布式LOCAL模型中的根本复杂度差异，尤其是精确性是否必须是全局性质。

Method: 在LOCAL模型中研究环$C_n$及扭曲纤维积（twisted fiber products）图族上的均衡自由$3$-染色；通过下界论证（$\Omega(n/g)$轮）与确定性算法（$O((n/g)\log^* n)$轮）进行匹配，并考察标识符全集阈值$N=n+1$及ruling-set锚点限制。进一步构造实现各种直径尺度的显式图族。对上界侧，分析常数乘性均衡及$(1\pm\eta)$平衡的局部算法。

Result: 环$C_n$上精确平衡自由$3$-染色需$\Omega(n)$轮；加性失衡$g$需$\Omega(n/g)$轮，与确定性$O((n/g)\log^* n)$算法匹配至$\log^* n$因子，并实现$\tilde\Theta(n^\alpha)$, $\alpha\in(0,1)$的稠密中间复杂度族。阈值精确为$N=n+1$，$\log^* n$间隙无法通过更快的ruling-set锚点消除。该界扩展到扭曲纤维积，实现从Moore界$\Theta(\log n/\log\Delta)$到$\Theta(n/\Delta)$的每个直径尺度；精确均衡$(\Delta+1)$-染色在该族上需$\Theta(D)$轮。相反，粗略平衡可局部实现：环上常数乘性均衡需$\Theta(\log^* n)$轮；一般图上类大小保持在$(1\pm\eta)$均值内的时间与直径无关，调色板$\Delta+1$（当$\Delta\le 2^{\sqrt{\log n}/C}$）或$(1+\eta)(\Delta+1)$（当$\Delta\le n^{1-o(1)}$），后者需$O(\log n(\log\log n)^2)$轮。

Conclusion: 精确均衡染色在确定性LOCAL模型中本质上是全局问题（即使在低直径的有界度图上也需$\Theta(D)$时间），而粗略均衡可完全本地化、与直径无关地实现。这揭示了精确性与近似性在分布式复杂度上的根本分离。

Abstract: For an $n$-vertex graph of maximum degree $Δ$, an equitable $(Δ+1)$-coloring is a proper coloring all of whose color classes have size $σ= n/(Δ+1)$ up to rounding. Known algorithms for relaxations of this target aggregate along a spanning structure, at cost scaling with the diameter $D$. We study near-equitable coloring in the LOCAL model and prove that, for deterministic algorithms, exact balance is inherently global: already on the cycle $C_n$, exact balanced free $3$-coloring requires $Ω(n)$ rounds. More generally, additive imbalance $g$ requires $Ω(n/g)$ rounds, matching a deterministic $O((n/g)\log^* n)$-round algorithm up to the $\log^* n$ factor, and these problems realize a dense family of intermediate deterministic LOCAL complexities $\tildeΘ(n^α)$, $α\in (0,1)$, on cycles. The exact bound is sharp in two further senses: the identifier-universe threshold is exactly $N=n+1$, and the $\log^* n$ gap cannot be closed via faster ruling-set anchors. The bound then extends to twisted fiber products, an explicit family realizing, for every $Δ\ge 3$, every diameter scale from the Moore bound $Θ(\log n/\logΔ)$ up to $Θ(n/Δ)$. Exact equitable $(Δ+1)$-coloring requires $Θ(D)$ rounds deterministically on this family, so exactness costs diameter time already on bounded-degree graphs of logarithmic diameter.
  In contrast, coarse balance admits local algorithms. On cycles, constant multiplicative equity costs $Θ(\log^* n)$ rounds. On general graphs with large color classes, all class sizes can be kept within $(1\pmη)$ times the average in time independent of the diameter: with palette exactly $Δ+1$ for $Δ\le 2^{\sqrt{\log n}/C}$, and with palette $(1+η)(Δ+1)$ for every $Δ\le n^{1-o(1)}$, the latter in $O(\log n\,(\log\log n)^2)$ rounds.

</details>


### [23] [PatchKV: Efficient KV Cache Recovery for Dynamically Edited LLM Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26219)
*Guotao Yang,Rui Guo,Siwei He,Sheng Chen,Yitao Hu,Keqiu Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PatchKV 是一种面向"保留后缀的上下文修改"场景的 KV 缓存恢复系统，通过脏区预测与稀疏非局部块选择构建固定修复集，其余后缀块从 CPU 内存低精度恢复并做 RoPE 校正，实现更快且精度相当的长上下文恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时运行的 LLM 智能体工作流常修改中间上下文片段而保留长后缀。尽管后缀 token 不变，但因果历史与旋转位置的变化使已卸载的 KV 状态无法精确复用：全量重算浪费 prefill 计算，而无差别复用会传播过期状态，全精度还原又带来额外数据搬运。因此需要一种既能避免重算、又能控制状态陈旧与数据移动的恢复方案。

Method: PatchKV 将相邻版本上下文分解为精确前缀、更新片段与对齐后缀。利用离线长度条件漂移模型预测编辑局部脏区，并结合从存储注意力中选出的稀疏非局部块，将二者并集按块对齐为固定修复集。剩余后缀块从 CPU 内存按冻结的逐块精度标签恢复，并通过融合路径完成反量化、RoPE 校正与 KV 页放置。

Result: 在 3 个模型与 3 个长上下文问答任务上，PatchKV 的平均恢复首 token 时间相比全后缀重算加速 2.51–3.85 倍，相比 CacheBlend 加速 1.26–2.06 倍；在 9 个设置中有 6 个 F1 与 CacheBlend 持平或更高，其余设置差距在 1.36 分以内。

Conclusion: 通过脏区预测、稀疏非局部补丁与低精度融合恢复的组合，PatchKV 在保持或提升答案质量的同时显著降低后缀保留修改场景下的恢复延迟，为长上下文智能体工作流的 KV 复用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agent workflows often revise interior context spans while retaining long suffixes. Although suffix tokens remain unchanged, altered causal histories and rotary positions prevent exact reuse of their offloaded key-value (KV) states. Full suffix recomputation wastes prefill work, while indiscriminate reuse propagates stale states and full-precision restoration adds data movement. We present PatchKV, a profile-guided recovery system for suffix-preserving revisions. PatchKV decomposes adjacent context versions into an exact prefix, an updated span, and an aligned suffix. It predicts an edit-local dirty region using an offline length-conditioned drift model, augments this region with sparse nonlocal blocks selected from stored attention, and block-rounds their union into a fixed repair set. The remaining suffix blocks are restored from CPU memory using frozen per-block precision tags and a fused path for dequantization, RoPE correction, and KV-page placement. Across three models and three long-context question-answering workloads, PatchKV achieves a $2.51$-$3.85\times$ speedup in mean resume time-to-first-token over full suffix recomputation and a $1.26$-$2.06\times$ speedup over CacheBlend, while matching or exceeding CacheBlend's F1 score in six of nine settings and remaining within 1.36 points in the others.

</details>


### [24] [DHSched: Stateless Control for Stateful Real-Time Avatar Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26363)
*Xin Wang,Haitong Zhang,Xianghong Li,Shumin Lin,Xianzheng Song,Lin Wang,Zhenyu Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 DHSched：一种通过无状态对等 Dispatch 副本管理长时有状态会话的控制平面，将会话所有权外部化为 (WorkerID, epoch) 生成号并进行条件提交与再校验，从而消除重分配过程中旧运行时仍持执行权导致的“双所有者/陈旧执行”问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时虚拟形象服务运行长时的 GPU 会话，需将连续文本流转换为语音、面部动作、渲染视频与 RTC 媒体。弹性伸缩会反复在 Worker 间重分配这些会话：重分配时共享放置状态被更新，而旧 GPU 运行时与 RTC 连接仍然存活，造成控制面分配与执行权威之间的空窗。传统有状态控制器通过保留每会话权威来串行化所有权，却把该状态放在了控制器的扩缩容与故障转移关键路径上。

Method: DHSched 以无状态对等 Dispatch 副本管理长时有状态会话：将会话所有权外部化为由 (WorkerID, epoch) 标识的生成号，Dispatch 对每个生成号进行条件提交，Infer-Controller 在释放推进状态的输入前声明并再校验同一生成号，使陈旧运行时即使仍存活也失去执行权威；候选排名基于周期性刷新的负载视图，容量准入保持权威；恢复通过源 epoch 守卫与源无关重建沿同一生成路径推进。

Result: DHSched 部署于生产虚拟形象服务栈，达到 49,987 并发在线会话；2026 年 6 月 10 日完成 9,927 次会话迁移，未观察到双所有者冲突或容量超卖。生产峰值下 CreateLive 延迟 P99 为 98.3 ms，PushText 延迟 P99 为 16.5 ms。在 100,000 次随机化所有权运行中，“仅分配时”“仅声明”“仅 WorkerID”协议均允许陈旧执行，而 DHSched 未记录到双所有者或陈旧生成号释放。

Conclusion: DHSched 通过将所有权外部化为生成号并由无状态对等副本条件提交与再校验，在不引入控制器有状态关键路径的前提下实现了长时 GPU 会话的安全重分配，并在生产规模与随机化验证中均优于其他协议，杜绝了陈旧执行与双所有者问题。

Abstract: Real-time avatar services run long-lived, GPU-backed sessions that transform a continuous text stream into speech, facial motion, rendered video, and RTC media. Elastic operation repeatedly reassigns these sessions across Workers. A reassignment updates shared placement state while the previous GPU runtime and RTC connection remain alive, opening a gap between control-plane assignment and execution authority. A stateful controller serializes ownership by retaining per-session authority, placing that state on the controller's scaling and failover path.
  We present DHSched, a control plane that manages long-lived stateful sessions through stateless peer Dispatch replicas. DHSched externalizes session ownership as a generation identified by (WorkerID, epoch). Dispatch conditionally commits each generation. Infer-Controller claims and revalidates the same generation before releasing state-advancing input. A stale runtime loses execution authority even if it remains alive. DHSched ranks candidates from periodically refreshed load views while keeping capacity admission authoritative. Recovery advances the same generation path via source-epoch guards and source-independent reconstruction.
  DHSched runs in our production avatar-serving stack, which reached 49,987 concurrent live sessions. On 10 June 2026, it completed 9,927 session migrations with no observed dual-owner conflicts or capacity overshoots. At the production peak, CreateLive latency was 98.3 ms at P99 and PushText latency was 16.5 ms at P99. Across 100,000 randomized ownership runs, assignment-time, claim-only, and WorkerID-only protocols permit stale execution, while DHSched records no dual-owner or stale-generation release.

</details>


### [25] [Don't let your Memory defy you: Fragmentation-Aware Serverless Allocation with Elastic Memory Locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26476)
*Achilleas Tzenetopoulos,Dimosthenis Masouros,Sotirios Xydis,Francky Catthoor,Dimitrios Soudris*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Memoryless是一个碎片感知的Serverless资源管理器，将内存局部性作为控制平面原语，通过联合选择和放置不同计算分配与内存局部性比例的函数变体，减少碎片化与资源浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serverless平台通常采用捆绑的、以内存为中心的配置（CPU容量随内存大小变化），资源解耦虽能减少浪费，却会产生多样化的CPU-内存形状，导致外部碎片化，使残余容量被闲置在节点上。内存解聚与分层可将这些资源空洞转化为可用容量。

Method: 提出Memoryless，一个碎片感知的资源管理器，将内存局部性暴露为Serverless控制平面原语。它联合选择并放置具有不同计算分配和内存局部性比例的函数变体，使调度器能将实例形状匹配到碎片化的节点容量，同时保持SLO。基于Knative实现，并用trace驱动的工作负载进行评估。

Result: 与最先进的Serverless放置框架相比，Memoryless在稳态下将节点使用量降低最多40%，峰值时降低46%，同时将SLO违规控制在3%以内；并将CPU搁浅降低44%。

Conclusion: 弹性内存局部性可将碎片化的节点容量转化为可用资源，Memoryless证明了碎片感知调度在Serverless场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Serverless platforms commonly rely on bundled, memory-centric configurations, where CPU capacity follows the specified memory size. Resource decoupling reduces this waste, but can create external fragmentation by producing diverse CPU-memory shapes that leave residual capacity stranded across nodes. Memory disaggregation and tiering can turn these resource holes into usable capacity by enabling elastic memory locality, where instances use different mixes of local and remote memory. In this paper, we present Memoryless, a fragmentation-aware resource manager that exposes memory locality as a serverless control-plane primitive. Memoryless jointly selects and places function variants that differ in their compute allocation and memory-locality ratio. This allows the scheduler to match instance shapes to fragmented node capacity while preserving SLOs. We implement Memoryless on top of Knative and evaluate it with trace-driven workloads. Compared to state-of-the-art serverless placement frameworks, Memoryless reduces node usage by up to 40% in steady state and 46% at peak, while keeping SLO violations within 3%. It also reduces CPU stranding by 44%, showing that elastic memory locality can convert fragmented node capacity into usable resources.

</details>


### [26] [A Configurable Heuristic for the MLCS Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26602)
*Farhana Akter Tumpa,Rebin Silva Valan Arasu,Rajiv Gupta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 ARP 启发式算法，通过添加 Δ、替换公共子序列和字符组优先三类操作求解多序列最长公共子序列（MLCS）问题，在速度与解质量间实现可调权衡，实验证明其在合成与生物序列上优于经典启发式 BNMAS，并在接近超启发式 UB-HH 解质的同时快 1.1–1.7 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLCS 对任意数量序列是 NP 难问题。现有精确算法（基于动态规划或 MLCS-DAG 剪枝）随序列长度和集合规模增长会迅速耗尽内存；启发式方法牺牲解质量，超启发式方法则需要大量参数调优，因此需要一种兼顾解质量与运行效率、且调参负担轻的方法。

Method: 提出 ARP 启发式算法，针对给定主序列执行三类关键操作：(i) Adding-Δs：通过逐步添加主序列的子序列 Δ 来增量式构建解；(ii) Replacing-Subsequences：对全部解共有的子序列进行定向替换，以增强解集多样性；(iii) Prioritizing：优先选择主序列中可能共同出现在更长公共子序列中的字符组。用户可配置替换与优先操作的强度，以实现质量—运行时间权衡。 

Result: 在合成与生物序列集上的实验表明：ARP 的最快配置 AOnly 找到的公共子序列显著长于经典启发式 BNMAS；其激进配置的解质量可与当前最优超启发式 UB-HH 相媲美，同时运行速度快 1.1×–1.7×。

Conclusion: ARP 通过可调的三类操作在解质量与运行时间之间提供灵活权衡，能够在大规模序列上超越经典启发式并接近超启发式的解质量，同时保持更快的运行速度，是一种有效的 MLCS 求解启发式。

Abstract: Motivation: The Multiple Longest Common Subsequence (MLCS) problem for an arbitrary number of sequences is an NP-hard problem in sequence analysis. Existing exact algorithms based on dynamic programming or MLCS-DAG pruning rapidly exhaust memory as sequence lengths and set sizes grow, while heuristic and hyper-heuristic approaches compromise solution quality and require heavy parameter tuning, respectively. Results: This paper presents the ARP heuristic that, for a given primary sequence, performs three key actions: (i) Adding-$Δ$s, which incrementally builds a solution by adding subsequences, called $Δ$s, of the primary sequence to an initial solution; (ii) Replacing-Subsequences, which enhances the diversity of the solution set via targeted replacement of subsequences common to all solutions; and (iii) Prioritizing groups of characters from the primary sequence that are likely to appear together in longer common subsequences. ARP allows the user to configure the degree of Replacements and Prioritization actions for carrying out quality-runtime tradeoff. Empirical evaluations on both synthetic and biological sequence sets demonstrate that ARP's fastest configuration AOnly finds significantly longer common subsequences than the BNMAS classical heuristic and its aggressive configuration attains solution quality comparable to state-of-the-art hyper-heuristic (UB-HH) while running 1.1$\times$-1.7$\times$ faster.

</details>


### [27] [Seeking Cost-Optimal Infrastructure Size for Distributed Filesystems: A Ceph Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26616)
*Niccolo Tosato,Isac Pasianotto,Ruggero Lot,Stefano Cozzini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实证方法，以Ceph为案例研究硬件配置选择对分布式文件系统（DFS）I/O性能的影响，利用Linux热插拔接口和Cgroups控制CPU核心数、内存量和磁盘带宽三个硬件变量，避免额外软件开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式文件系统是现代计算环境的关键组件，但其I/O性能难以仅凭存储系统硬件预测。论文旨在量化评估硬件配置选择对DFS性能的影响，为设计高性价比的分布式存储系统提供依据。

Method: 以Ceph为案例，研究CPU核心数、内存量和磁盘带宽三个硬件参数的影响。为控制这些变量，采用Linux热插拔接口与Cgroups，避免引入额外软件开销。

Result: 结果表明，在所分析的工作负载下，减少硬件资源并不总是带来成比例的性能损失。该方法为设计高性价比的分布式存储系统提供了实践见解，并具有足够的通用性，可应用于其他文件系统。

Conclusion: 硬件资源与DFS性能之间并非简单的线性关系，通过该实证方法可更经济地指导分布式存储系统的硬件配置设计，且方法可推广至其他文件系统。

Abstract: Distributed Filesystems (DFS) are a crucial component of modern computing environments, and their performance is critical to the success of all the facilities that rely on them. However, predicting the DFS I/O performance solely based on the storage system hardware is not trivial. In this paper, we address this challenge by presenting an empirical method that tries to quantitatively assess how hardware configuration choices influence the performance of a DFS using Ceph as a case study. We investigate the influence of three hardware parameters-number of CPU cores, amount of RAM, and disk bandwidth. To control these variables, we relied on the Linux hotplug interface and Cgroups, avoiding additional software overhead. Our results reveal that for the analyzed workloads, decreasing hardware resources does not always yield proportional performance losses. This method offers practical insights for designing cost-effective distributed storage systems, remaining general enough to be applied to other filesystems.

</details>

### [28] ["As a Language Model...": Chat Template Switches LLM Self-Referential Voice and Activation Steering Reproduces It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25021)
*Jędrzej Maczan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，聊天模板像开关一样控制大语言模型自我描述时"免责声明语气"与"体验语气"的强弱，且模型中存在可操控该行为的激活方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在被问及自身相关问题时倾向加上"我只是个AI"等免责声明，这些自我报告被用于AI安全与模型自我知识的讨论，但其成因尚不清楚。作者希望探明：模型是在描述自身，还是在反映其部署方式。

Method: 对比8个主流开源指令模型（最大9B参数）在有无聊天模板时的表现；在3个模型的激活空间中寻找控制该行为的方向，通过移除或添加该方向进行干预，并与同尺寸随机方向作对照。

Result: 聊天模板存在时，免责声明语气增强、"我感觉"等体验语气减弱；不存在时则相反。移除该方向会降低免责声明语气，添加则增强，而随机方向影响很小。将免责声明方向加入无模板的指令模型，可使其表现得如同存在模板。

Conclusion: 聊天模板控制着LLM的免责声明语气，研究模型自我报告或内省时需控制这一混淆因素，并可用该方向进行操控。更广泛地讲，模型关于自身的表述并非其固有事实，而是部分由聊天模板设定，因此不应被字面理解。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) tend to add disclaimers like "I'm just an AI" when asked about something related to themselves. The self-reports from such responses are used in debates about AI safety or self-knowledge of the models, yet what drives them is not well understood. Are the models telling us about themselves or rather how they are deployed? In this work, we show that the chat template works like a switch - when present, it turns this disclaimer voice up and experiential voice like "I feel" down, across 8 popular open-source instruct models up to 9B parameters in size. And conversely when the chat template is not present, it turns the disclaimer voice down and experiential voice up. Inside the activations of 3 models, we find a direction that steers this behavior. Removing the direction in the model's activation space turns disclaimer voice down and adding it turns it up, while a random direction of the same size has little effect. We find that instruct models without chat template, when we add the disclaimer direction to them, disclaim like the template was there. Since the chat template controls the disclaimer voice of LLMs, then researchers studying self-reports or introspection of models might have a confound they need to control for. Our results show that there is a direction they can use to steer this voice. More broadly, our work shows that what models say about themselves is not a fact about them. What they say doesn't come only from weights, but it is partially set by the chat template, and because of that a model's self-description shouldn't be treated literally.

</details>


### [29] [Federating Quantum and Classical Computing: A Privacy-Preserving Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25082)
*Carlos Cano,Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Diego Sal,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估联邦学习（FL）在混合量子-经典模型中的应用，采用Sherpa.ai盲式垂直联邦学习（SBVFL）协议避免原始数据集中化，并大幅降低通信开销。作者构建了分割乘性周期奇偶（SMPP）基准任务，实验表明SBVFL将准确率从0.7227提升至0.8757，接近非隐私集中式训练效果，且混合量子-经典模型的参数量显著少于经典神经网络和随机森林。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习（QML）被视为量子计算近期最有前景的应用方向之一，混合量子-经典模型已在化学模拟、金融建模和图像分类等领域应用。但此类模型在隐私敏感的多方场景中面临两大限制：一是需避免原始数据集中化，二是现代量子电路需保持参数高效性以保证大规模可训练。

Method: 采用联邦学习（FL）结合一个混合量子-经典主动方与一个经典被动方，使用Sherpa.ai的盲式垂直联邦学习（SBVFL）协议避免原始数据集中化并大幅减少通信量；同时按照QML常见设计实践构建了分割乘性周期奇偶（SMPP）基准任务进行仿真评估。

Result: 在SMPP任务上，SBVFL相比本地训练将准确率从0.7227提升至0.8757，接近非隐私集中式训练的准确率；同时混合量子-经典模型仅需远少于经典神经网络和随机森林的可训练参数量即可达到该效果。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够在不集中原始数据的前提下，实现高性能、隐私保护的量子-经典协同训练，为隐私敏感多方场景下的混合量子-经典模型部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) is increasingly recognized as one of the most promising near-term applications of quantum computing, viewed as a next-frontier candidate beyond purely classical approaches. Hybrid quantum-classical models operationalize this potential by embedding a parameterized quantum circuit within a model where all other components remain classical-a design already applied to chemistry simulation, financial modeling, and image classification. However, their deployment in privacy-sensitive, multi-party settings is constrained by the need to avoid centralizing raw data and by the requirement that modern quantum circuits remain parameter-efficient to stay trainable at scale.
  In this paper, we address these constraints by evaluating federated learning (FL) as a means of combining a hybrid quantum-classical active party with a classical passive party, using Sherpa.ai's Blind Vertical FL (SBVFL) protocol to avoid centralizing raw data, while drastically reducing communication. We construct the split multiplicative periodic parity (SMPP) benchmark, following common QML design practice. On this task, our simulations show that SBVFL raises accuracy from 0.7227 to 0.8757 compared to local training, closely approaching non-private centralized accuracy, and that the hybrid quantum-classical model achieves this with substantially fewer trainable parameters than the classical neural networks and random forest alternatives. These results show that FL enables high-performing, privacy-preserving quantum-classical collaboration without centralizing raw data.

</details>


### [30] [Entropy Can Flow, or It Can Guide. Be Entropy. LEDFlow: Introducing Entropy-guided Generation Order into Uniform Discrete Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25131)
*Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Yidong Ouyang,Yingjia Wan,Ying Nian Wu,Zhijiang Guo,Oscar Leong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LEDFlow，一种免训练采样器，通过局部熵自适应确定吸收顺序，将生成顺序引入均匀离散流，减少正确中间预测被后续错误覆盖的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀离散流允许每个生成位置反复更新，虽能纠正错误token，但也会使原本正确的中间预测被后续错误覆盖。Sudoku实验显示9.4%的生成单元在中间步骤正确却在最终输出中出错。

Method: 提出选择性吸收机制，在固定所选预测的同时保持活跃位置上的均匀流速度；提出Low-Entropy Discrete Flow（LEDFlow）免训练采样器，按局部熵自适应排序吸收。理论分析将吸收误差分解为联合依赖项与条件预测项，证明在固定吸收预算下选择最低熵位置可最小化条件项上界，并说明在不完美去噪器下全局前瞻的决策误差界随窗口增大而增长。

Result: 在推理基准上，LEDFlow在Nikoli Sudoku达到0.845求解准确率，在强约束任务上增益最大；在文本到图像生成上取得最佳总体得分；在多模态理解上于全部六个基准上优于原生采样器，推理成本与标准流采样相当。

Conclusion: 通过熵引导的选择性吸收引入生成顺序，可有效缓解均匀离散流中的中间正确预测被覆盖问题，在推理、图像生成与多模态理解任务上均取得性能提升且成本可控。

Abstract: Uniform discrete flow permits repeated updates at every generation position. While continued revision supports correction of wrong tokens, it also exposes correct intermediate predictions to later errors. An experiment on Sudoku puzzles shows that 9.4% of generated cells are correct at an intermediate step but incorrect in the final output. We introduce generation order into uniform discrete flow through selective absorption, which fixes chosen predictions while preserving the uniform-flow velocity at active positions. To prevent absorbing incorrect predictions, we propose Low-Entropy Discrete Flow (LEDFlow), a training-free sampler that adaptively orders absorption by local entropy. By decomposing absorption error into joint dependence and conditional prediction terms, we show that selecting the lowest-entropy positions under a fixed absorption budget minimizes an upper bound on the conditional term. We further support the choice of local entropy by showing that the decision-error bound of global lookahead grows with the lookahead window under an imperfect denoiser. Across reasoning benchmarks, LEDFlow attains 0.845 Nikoli Sudoku solve accuracy, with the largest gains on strongly constrained tasks. On text-to-image generation it attains the best overall score, and on multimodal understanding it improves over the native sampler on all six benchmarks, at an inference cost comparable to standard flow sampling.

</details>

*Adnan Aboulalaâ*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从概率论视角统一阐述大语言模型（LLM）与扩散模型：LLM被刻画为token序列上的概率测度、通过自回归条件分布指定，训练为最大似然估计、生成即随机过程的序贯模拟；扩散模型则基于得分函数，将生成视为逆向时间随机过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中相关工具通常被分散、割裂地处理，作者希望在一个自洽的框架内将它们整合起来，提供统一的认识视角。

Method: 以概率测度刻画LLM（由自回归条件分布指定）；将训练表述为最大似然估计并用随机梯度方法求解；将文本生成视为该随机过程的序贯模拟；分析KL散度不对称性在生成中的作用；并借助得分函数介绍扩散模型，在离散与连续时间下将生成视为把噪声变换为数据的逆向时间随机过程模拟。

Result: 揭示了KL散度不对称性与幻觉现象、以及统计合理性与真实性之间区别的关联，并说明扩散模型可与LLM纳入同一概率视角下理解。

Conclusion: LLM与扩散模型可在统一的概率框架下得到自洽描述，前者侧重自回归条件分布与序贯模拟，后者侧重得分函数与逆向时间过程，二者共享最大似然与随机过程模拟的核心思想。

Abstract: This paper presents a probabilistic perspective on large language models (LLMs), developed with the aim of bringing together, in a single self-contained account, tools that are usually treated separately across the literature. LLMs are described through probability measures on the set of sequences of tokens, specified via their autoregressive conditional distributions. Training is formulated as a maximum-likelihood estimation problem, addressed by stochastic gradient methods, while text generation is viewed as the sequential simulation of the resulting stochastic process. The role of the asymmetry of the Kullback--Leibler divergence in text generation is examined in relation with characteristic phenomena such as hallucination and the distinction between statistical plausibility and truth. As a complementary illustration of the same viewpoint, we also discuss diffusion models, built around the score function, which cast generation not as sequential token prediction but as the simulation of a reverse-time stochastic process transforming noise into data both in discrete and continuous time.

</details>


### [32] [Stable Unsupervised Continual Chunking with Sheaf SyncMap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25143)
*Xueyuan Li,Danilo Vasconcellos Vargas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用 sheaf 正则化（径向 sheaf 结构）改进 Decentralized SyncMap，通过惩罚变量间距离相关的径向运动来减少局部不一致，从而稳定无监督持续分块动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督持续分块的核心挑战是既要形成准确的分块，又要在时间上保持稳定。现有 SyncMap 类自组织系统在分块动力学上存在局部不一致问题。

Method: 在 Decentralized SyncMap 上引入 sheaf 正则化，设计径向 sheaf 结构，对成对变量之间距离依赖的径向运动施加惩罚，以降低局部不一致并稳定分块过程。

Result: 在 18 个概率 CGCP 图中，两状态记忆下有 12 个、动态记忆下有 17 个达到所评估 SyncMap 变体中最高的 NMI。顺序适应实验中，输入分布发生偏移后仍保持高 NMI。

Conclusion: Sheaf SyncMap 能稳定分块并提升准确性，还可在分布偏移后适应新知识，同时避免神经网络等方法常见的负迁移。

Abstract: Unsupervised Continual chunking is a fundamental problem in machine learning and neuroscience, where the goal is to identify groups of states that frequently co-occur in temporal sequences. A key challenge is to form accurate chunks while maintaining their stability over time. In this work, we propose sheaf regularization to reduce local inconsistencies in Decentralized SyncMap, a self-organizing system, and thereby stabilize its chunking dynamics. We introduce a radial sheaf structure that penalizes distance-dependent radial motion between pairs of variables. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves the highest normalized mutual information (NMI) among the evaluated SyncMap variants on 12 of 18 probabilistic Continual General Chunking Problem (CGCP) graphs with two-state memory and on 17 of 18 graphs with dynamic memory. In the sequential adaptation experiment, Sheaf SyncMap also achieves high NMI after shifts in the input distribution, indicating that it can adapt to new knowledge while avoiding the negative transfer commonly observed in modern machine learning systems such as neural networks.

</details>


### [33] [Brain-Inspired Hierarchical Modularity for General Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25146)
*Hongwei Yan,Kanglei Zhou,Qi Cheng,Weiyi Dong,Chunyan Lan,Guanglong Sun,Jun Zhou,Qian Li,Yi Zhong,Liyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受果蝇学习记忆系统启发的层次模块化原则，通过专家特化与集成整合实现持续学习，在在线不确定数据流下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习多基于离线、任务明确边界的训练，与在线、不确定、演化的数据流下的通用持续学习存在较大差距。该场景要求系统既能分离冲突经验以减少干扰，又能整合兼容经验以促进泛化。

Method: 受果蝇学习与记忆系统组织方式启发，提出层次模块化原则，协调专家特化与集成整合两种功能。将其实现为预训练基础模型的轻量模块化适配，结合脑启发的随机扩展进行专家路由，以及跨空间与时间尺度的多样化模块集成。

Result: 在视觉识别、视觉-语言理解、自我-他人视频理解以及具身视觉-语言-动作学习等任务上，方法在在线且不确定的数据流下持续提升学习效果，在具身操作任务中相较无回放的替代方案提升超过50个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持层次模块化是一种具有生物学基础、适用于从动态经验中学习的路径。

Abstract: Continual learning, the ability to learn from sequential experience while retaining and adapting prior knowledge, is central to intelligent systems operating in changing environments. However, conventional continual learning is typically studied with offline task-wise training and clear task boundaries, leaving a substantial gap from general continual learning under online, uncertain, and evolving data streams. In this regime, intelligent systems must separate conflicting experience to reduce interference while integrating compatible experience to promote generalization. Inspired by the organization of the Drosophila learning and memory system, we identify a hierarchical modular principle that coordinates both functions through expert specialization and ensemble integration. We instantiate this principle as lightweight modular adaptation of pretrained foundation models, combining brain-inspired random expansion for expert routing and diversified modular integration across spatial and temporal scales. Across visual recognition, vision-language understanding, ego-exo video understanding, and embodied vision-language-action learning, our method consistently improves learning under online and uncertain data streams, with gains exceeding 50 percentage points over replay-free alternatives in embodied manipulation. These findings support hierarchical modularity as a biologically grounded path for learning from dynamic experience.

</details>
