*Tianfeng Chen,Xianyue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于学习的图边稀疏化方法GES，用于欧氏TSP，大幅剪枝边同时保持解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确求解大规模TSP计算代价高昂。传统图稀疏化方法依赖固定启发式，无法充分利用实例特定的结构信息，因此需要一种能自适应生成稀疏图的方法来提升求解效率。

Method: 提出图边稀疏化（GES）方法，一种面向欧氏TSP的基于学习的稀疏化策略。它融合几何结构信息与组合优化技术，针对不同实例自适应地生成稀疏化图，显著减小图规模并加速求解过程。

Result: 在MATILDA数据集上可剪枝高达95%的边，解与最优值的差距保持在1%以内；在TSPLIB基准上展现出强泛化能力，部分大规模实例剪枝率超过99%，最优性差距仍低于1%。

Conclusion: GES通过结合学习与几何结构信息实现自适应稀疏化，在保证解质量的同时显著降低TSP求解规模，并具备良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Solving large-scale instances of the Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) exactly is computationally expensive. Researchers often employ graph sparsification methods to improve computational efficiency. Traditional sparsification methods typically rely on fixed heuristics and fail to fully exploit instance-specific structural information. In this paper, we propose Graph Edge Sparsification (GES), a learning-based sparsification approach for Euclidean TSP. By incorporating geometric structural information and combinatorial optimization technology, our proposed method adaptively generates a sparsification graph for different instances, significantly reducing the graph size and accelerating the solving process. Experimental results demonstrate that our sparsification method can prune up to 95\% of edges on the MATILDA dataset, while keeping the solution gap within 1\% of the optimal value. Moreover, our approach exhibits strong generalization capability on the TSPLIB benchmark.In some large-scale instances, the pruning rate exceeds 99\%, while the optimality gap remains below 1\%.

</details>


### [35] [Exposing Blind Spots in Deep Imbalanced Regression Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25152)
*Noah C. Puetz,Jens U. Brandt,Marc Hilbert,Elena Raponi,Thomas Bäck,Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视深度不平衡回归（DIR）的评估体系，从数据域、评价指标和随机种子稳定性三个维度进行扩展研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DIR评估存在三个盲点：以图像基准为主、标准少/中/多样本协议只能诊断而非完整决策、尾部区域跨随机种子的稳定性未被系统评估。作者希望通过多模态时序数据、新指标和重复实验来弥补这些不足。

Method: 在MuViS多模态虚拟传感基准（六个物理域、九个时序外源回归任务）上评估DIR；采用balanced MAE（bMAE）并引入balanced MASE（bMASE）作为尺度归一化指标；对六种代表性DIR方法在多个随机种子下进行重复评估。

Result: 标准虚拟传感模型在尾部区域存在明显退化，被全局MAE掩盖；现有DIR方法能提升平衡性能，但向多模态时序数据迁移不均衡；尾部区域对种子级变异性高度敏感，是当前评估实践下隐藏的失效模式。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了可复现的DIR评估基础，代码公开，推动未来回归系统在稀有目标区间上达到与常见区间同样可靠的性能。

Abstract: Deep Imbalanced Regression (DIR) addresses a common failure mode of regression models: target distributions are highly non-uniform, causing models to perform best in densely populated target regions even when reliable performance is required across the full target range. Despite rapid methodological progress, DIR evaluation remains constrained by three blind spots: it is dominated by image-based benchmarks, its standard many-/medium-/few-shot protocol is diagnostic but not decision-complete, and tail-region stability across random seeds has not been systematically evaluated. We revisit DIR evaluation along these three axes. First, we broaden the data domain by evaluating DIR on a multimodal virtual sensing benchmark (\textsc{MuViS}) with nine time-series extrinsic regression tasks across six physical domains, where rare target values often correspond to operationally meaningful regimes. Second, we adopt balanced MAE (\emph{bMAE}) and introduce balanced Mean Absolute Scaled Error (\emph{bMASE}), a scale-normalized metric for decision-complete comparison across methods and datasets. Third, through a repeated reevaluation of six representative DIR methods across multiple random seeds, we show that the tail regions targeted by DIR exhibit particularly high sensitivity to seed-level variability. Our results show that standard virtual-sensing models exhibit substantial tail degradation hidden by global MAE, that existing DIR methods can improve balanced performance but transfer unevenly to multimodal time-series data, and that tail-region instability remains a largely hidden failure mode under current DIR evaluation practice. Together, these findings and our publicly available code provide a reproducible basis for future DIR research toward regression systems that capture rare target regimes as reliably as common ones.

</details>


### [36] [Learning Neural Feedback Linearization for Data-driven Systems via Augmented Lagrangian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25163)
*Lakshmi Priya P. K.,Andreas Schwung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的反馈线性化控制框架，用神经Lie导数替代传统控制器组件，并给出闭环稳定性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反馈线性化依赖精确的解析模型与Lie导数计算，难以在模型未知或存在不确定性的系统中应用，需要一种完全数据驱动的替代方案。

Method: 将相对阶相关条件显式嵌入学习过程，设计并训练反馈线性化控制器；用神经Lie导数替换传统反馈控制器组件，构建端到端的数据驱动框架；推导在辨识误差有界时跟踪误差有界的充分条件，以建立实际闭环稳定性。

Result: 理论结果表明，有界的辨识误差可导致有界的跟踪误差，从而保证实际闭环稳定性；通过在电枢控制直流电机上的应用验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种可保证稳定性的全数据驱动反馈线性化方法，并通过直流电机案例验证了其可行性。

Abstract: The paper proposes a novel data-driven framework for designing and training a feedback linearizing controller by explicitly incorporating relative degree based conditions into the learning process. This enables the conventional feedback controller components to be replaced by neural Lie derivatives, thereby facilitating a fully data-driven feedback linearization framework. Furthermore, practical closed-loop stability is established by deriving sufficient conditions under which bounded identification errors lead to bounded tracking errors. The derived theoretical results are validated through their application to an armature controlled DC motor.

</details>


### [37] [Mitigating Sequential Reappearance in Diffusion Data-Point Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25166)
*Donghyun Kim,Taehyuk Lee,Jinyeong Kim,Youngmin Oh,Dohyeong Kim,Jaehyuk Ryu,Sangwoo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别扩散模型数据点遗忘中的"序列重现"失效模式，并提出目标级评估协议来追踪遗忘状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散数据点遗忘通常只在每次删除后立即评估，但后续请求会不断更新同一模型，导致被遗忘的实例可能重新回到记忆状态，这一失效模式未被捕获。

Method: 提出目标级评估协议，追踪每个目标是否在删除后立即被遗忘、在序列结束时保持遗忘，或在后续删除过程中重现；并分析删除后目标的局部去噪损失几何。

Result: 发现后期重现的目标在删除后表现出比保持遗忘的目标更尖锐的局部去噪损失几何。

Conclusion: 序列重现是扩散模型数据点遗忘中需重视的失效模式，且局部去噪损失几何可作为预测重现的指标，强调了序列化评估的必要性。

Abstract: Diffusion data-point unlearning is typically evaluated immediately after each deletion, even though subsequent requests may repeatedly update the same model. We identify sequential reappearance, a failure mode in which an instance that is initially judged to be forgotten later returns to the memorized regime without reuse of the deleted data or adversarial fine-tuning. To capture this behavior, we introduce a target-level evaluation protocol that tracks whether each target is forgotten immediately, remains forgotten at the end of the sequence, or reappears during subsequent deletions. We further find that targets that later reappear exhibit sharper local denoising-loss geometry after deletion than targets that remain forgotten.

</details>


### [38] [Multi-Term Fourier Graph Neural Network with Sample Relationship Learning for Enhanced Remaining Useful Life Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25179)
*Ya Song,Laurens Bliek,Yaoxin Wu,Yingqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MTFGN-SRL框架，将样本建模为单张完整图并用傅里叶图神经网络在频域捕捉时空信息，结合多回看窗口的多项学习与样本关系学习模块，在CMAPSS数据集上RUL预测性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ST-GNN用于RUL预测存在三大问题：1）部署前需领域专业知识或大量算力来构建图结构；2）时间依赖被限制在预设的固定回看窗口内，忽略时间序列长度变化的问题，导致漏掉短期或长期依赖；3）传统模型难以捕捉相邻时间窗口样本间的内在关系，而这些关系对提升预测精度和鲁棒性至关重要。

Method: 提出MTFGN-SRL框架：不再将样本视为图序列，而是视为一张完整图，用傅里叶图神经网络（FGN）在频域捕捉时空信息；设计多项学习模块，利用多个回看窗口生成不同项数的样本送入FGN，以增强有用信息提取；并开发样本关系学习模块，通过训练异质GNN识别样本间关系。

Result: 在CMAPSS数据集上的评估表明，MTFGN-SRL在RUL预测中优于多种最先进方法。

Conclusion: MTFGN-SRL通过频域建模、多回看窗口的多项学习以及样本关系学习，有效缓解了现有ST-GNN在图结构构建、固定窗口限制和样本关系缺失方面的不足，提升了RUL预测的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Predicting the remaining useful life (RUL) is essential for effective predictive maintenance. Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ST-GNNs), which can model both temporal and spatial relationships by representing time series data as a sequence of graphs, have shown exceptional performance in RUL prediction. However, current ST-GNNs face several drawbacks. First, they require domain expertise or significant computational power to establish graph structures prior to deploying GNNs. Second, the models are restricted to capture temporal dependencies within a predefined fixed-size lookback window. This restriction ignores the common issue of varying time series lengths, leading the prediction model to miss short-term or long-term dependencies. Finally, conventional models often fail to capture the inherent relationships between samples generated from adjacent time windows, which are crucial for improving both the accuracy and robustness of predictions. To address the aforementioned issues, we introduce a novel framework called Multi-Term Fourier Graph Neural Network with Sample Relationship Learning (MTFGN-SRL). Rather than treating the sample as a sequence of graphs, we consider it as a single complete graph and utilize a Fourier Graph Neural Network (FGN) to capture the spatio-temporal information in the frequency domain. We propose a multi-term learning module that utilizes multiple lookback windows to generate samples with varying terms, which are then fed into the FGN to enhance the extraction of useful information from the data. Finally, we develop a sample relationship learning module by training a heterogeneous GNN to identify inter-sample relationships, resulting in enhanced accuracy and robustness in predictions. Evaluations on the CMAPSS dataset demonstrate MTFGN-SRL's superior performance over state-of-the-art methods in RUL prediction.

</details>

*Weihang Ding,Junfei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出以"治理化交付平面"评估LLM智能体能否可信地承担前向部署工程师（FDE）角色，用预言机对十个交付阶段逐阶段打分，并刻画"训练了但没学到"（TBDL）这一隐性失败，在多种GPU与8B–70B开源基座上端到端测试四个前沿智能体并与人类FDE对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练正变成一种服务（PTaaS）：客户交数据与目标，由前向部署工程师在预算、人工审批门与可复现性约束下交付微调、评估并部署好的模型。让LLM智能体坐进FDE的位置，引出现有基准无法回答的问题——不是智能体能否提高某个指标，而是它是否值得被信任来完成交付。现有基准缺少对交付过程可信度的评估手段。

Method: 在治理化交付平面上评估：智能体驱动十个阶段，预言机依据平台记录的事实对每个阶段打分；设置算子运行的验收门以保证有问题的运行不被付款，并使用在已知损坏运行上校准的检测器在运行中标记严重损坏。端到端运行四个前沿智能体（Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6-luna、Gemini 3.7 Flash、DeepSeek V4-Pro），使用计量的L40S、A100、H200 GPU，覆盖8B至70B开源基座，每个场景先认证再评分；同时在同一预言机下运行人类FDE对照臂，逐一对智能体与人类比较。

Result: 提出了治理化交付平面与可复现的评分机制，识别并刻画了核心隐性失败模式TBDL（训练了但没学到）：损失下降、所有信号保持绿色，而交付的模型并不优于基座。算子验收门可在付款前捕获所有此类运行，校准后的检测器能在运行中途标记严重损坏。四个智能体与人类FDE在多种GPU和模型规模上完成端到端对照评估（摘要未给出具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: 评估重心应从"智能体能否提升指标"转向"智能体能否被信任完成交付"，需用平台记录的客观事实作为预言机信号，并依靠验收门与损坏检测器把隐性失败（如TBDL）在付款或部署前暴露出来；人类FDE对照臂提供了智能体可信度的参照基线。

Abstract: Post-training is becoming a service (PTaaS): a customer hands an operator data and a goal, and a forward-deployed engineer (FDE) returns a fine-tuned, evaluated, and deployed model under a budget, a human-approval gate, and reproducibility requirements. Seating an LLM agent in the FDE seat raises a question existing benchmarks cannot answer: not whether an agent can raise a metric, but whether it can be trusted to deliver. We answer it on a governed delivery plane, where an agent drives ten stages and an oracle scores each stage from platform-recorded facts. The central silent failure is the run that trains but does not learn (TBDL): loss falls, every signal stays green, and the delivered model is no better than the base. An operator-run acceptance gate catches every such run before payment, and a detector calibrated on known-corrupted runs flags severe corruption mid-run. We ran four frontier agents (Claude Opus 5, GPT-5.6-luna, Gemini 3.7 Flash, DeepSeek V4-Pro) end to end on metered L40S, A100, and H200 GPUs across 8B to 70B open bases, certifying every scenario before scoring. We also ran a human FDE arm under the same oracle and compare every agent against it.

</details>


### [40] [Correcting Within-Group Self-Selection Bias in Prioritized Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25297)
*Oscar Miró López-Feliu,Herke van Hoof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示优先经验回放(PER)在随机环境中会造成"组内自选择"偏差，即扭曲同一状态-动作对下不同结果的回放频率，并提出三种保留组级优先权重的修正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PER通常按绝对时序差分误差回放高优先级转移以提升样本效率，但在随机环境中，它会使同一状态-动作对下真实结果的分布发生偏移（组内自选择），导致组内结果频率与平均贝尔曼目标发生变化。作者希望量化这一偏差并加以修正。

Method: 将PER分解为组间分配与条件性同组选择两部分，推导在保持当前组级优先级质量不变前提下的固定缓冲区修正方法：SAMPLE（按PER选组、再均匀采样同组兄弟样本训练）、AVG（对同组贝尔曼目标取平均）、MODEL（从经验性的完整结果模型中采样）。

Result: 在具有稀有高幅值结果的精确状态-动作环境中，兄弟感知回放相比PER提升了学习效率，尽管匹配参数扫描显示调参可以缩小部分差距；在MinAtar中，用近似VQ-VAE分组配合SAMPLE在五款游戏中的四款缓解了均值保持型奖励尾部带来的性能退化。

Conclusion: 兄弟感知回放能在保持对高优先级状态-动作区域关注的同时，恢复其经验性的结果频率，从而修正PER在随机环境中的组内自选择偏差。

Abstract: Prioritized experience replay (PER) improves sample efficiency by replaying high-priority transitions, usually according to absolute temporal-difference error. In stochastic environments, PER can distort the distribution of realized outcomes replayed from transitions with the same state-action pair. We call this within-group self-selection. We quantify the resulting changes in within-group outcome frequencies and mean Bellman targets. We decompose PER into between-group allocation and conditional sibling selection, and derive fixed-buffer corrections that preserve current group-level priority mass: SAMPLE selects a group through PER and trains on a uniformly sampled sibling; AVG averages sibling Bellman targets; and MODEL samples from an empirical full-outcome model. In exact state-action environments with rare high-magnitude outcomes, sibling-aware replay improves learning efficiency over PER, although matched parameter sweeps show that tuning can narrow some gaps. In MinAtar, approximate VQ-VAE groups with SAMPLE mitigate degradation under mean-preserving reward tails in four of five games. Sibling-aware replay thus retains the focus on high-priority state-action regions while recovering their empirical outcome frequencies.

</details>


### [41] [Topological Signal Processing With Unoriented Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25310)
*Andrea Cavallo,Varun Sarathchandran,Geert Leus,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无定向拓扑信号处理（UTSP）框架，用无定向关联矩阵替代有向边界，并给出无定向分解与正则化方法，在真实数据上优于有向基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑信号处理（TSP）在单纯复形上使用有向边界算子，适用于流信号或拓扑不变量起作用的场景。但许多高阶信号本身无方向，对其应用有向算子会产生单纯形方向的任意选择问题，因此需要研究无定向的TSP框架。

Method: 1）用无定向关联矩阵替代有向边界，并证明任意单纯形层级间的无定向关联与拉普拉斯矩阵具有类图的谱性质；2）由于去定向后Hodge分解失效，提出无定向的"交互阶分解"，量化高阶信号由低阶信号聚合解释的程度；3）利用该分解推导信号重建的正则化项，对每个交互阶分别进行惩罚。

Result: 在真实数据上的实验表明，阶感知正则化器优于有向基线，且当信号能量在各阶之间分布不均时增益最大。

Conclusion: 该工作为无方向高阶信号提供了系统的无定向拓扑信号处理理论与正则化工具，拓展了TSP的适用范围。

Abstract: Topological signal processing (TSP) processes signals on simplicial complexes with oriented boundary operators, which is the natural choice for flow signals or when the topological invariants play a role for the task at hand. However, many higher-order signals carry no orientation, and applying oriented operators to them is not well-defined since it introduces an arbitrary choice of simplex orientation. We study an unoriented TSP (UTSP) framework that replaces oriented boundaries with unoriented incidence matrices. First, we show that unoriented incidence and Laplacian matrices between arbitrary simplicial levels admit graph-like spectral properties. Second, since dropping orientation removes the Hodge decomposition, we introduce an unoriented counterpart, termed interaction-order decomposition, which quantifies how much of a higher-order signal is explained by aggregating lower-order signals. Third, we use this decomposition to derive regularizers for signal reconstruction that penalize each interaction order separately. Experiments on real-world data show that the order-aware regularizers outperform oriented baselines, with the largest gains when the signal energy is unevenly distributed across orders.

</details>


### [42] [Spatiotemporal Kronecker Covariance Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25326)
*Andrea Cavallo,Athanasios Georgoutsos,Elvin Isufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kronecker协方差神经网络(KVNN)，用Kronecker积和分解时空协方差矩阵，实现可证明稳定的时空图神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列蕴含跨时空的复杂模式。基于协方差的统计工具(如ST-PCA)只能处理线性操作且在小样本下易受估计误差影响；而现有的基于协方差的时空神经网络虽更稳定、非线性，却忽略了不同时间步之间的相关性。

Method: 提出Kronecker协方差神经网络(KVNN)，一种时序图神经网络：将时空协方差矩阵表示为Kronecker积之和，从而解耦空间与时间依赖；在空间和时间分量上分别实施滤波操作，实现富有表现力的处理。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上，KVNN取得较强的预测性能，且往往所需可训练参数显著少于竞争方法，并在估计噪声下保持一致性。

Conclusion: KVNN具备严格的谱分析、对有限样本估计误差的可证明稳定性，全面解决了ST-PCA的局限，是一种高效且稳健的时空预测方法。

Abstract: Multivariate time series contain complex patterns that span across both space and time. While covariance-based statistical tools like spatiotemporal Principal Component Analysis (ST-PCA) help identify these patterns, they are limited to linear operations and prone to estimation errors with limited data. Recent covariance-based spatiotemporal neural networks offer more stable, non-linear alternatives, but they ignore correlations across different time steps. To solve this, we introduce the Kronecker coVariance Neural Network (KVNN), a temporal graph neural network that represents the spatiotemporal covariance matrix via a sum of Kronecker products where spatial and temporal dependencies are decoupled. By implementing filtering operations on spatial and temporal components, KVNNs achieve expressive processing capabilities, admit a rigorous spectral analysis, and are provably stable to finite-sample estimation errors, ultimately addressing all of ST-PCA's limitations. We show on five real-world datasets that KVNNs achieve strong forecasting performance, often requiring significantly fewer trainable parameters than competitive methods, and are consistent under estimation noise.

</details>


### [43] [MT-ProtBERT: Multi-task Learning ProtBERT for Intrinsically Disordered Proteins Classification with Scarce Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25334)
*Jian Sun,Kingshuk Ghosh,Lilianna Houston,Mohammad H. Mahoor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MT-ProtBERT，通过多任务学习扩展ProtBERT以在低数据条件下建模内在无序蛋白，优于PARROT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内在无序蛋白（IDP）构象动态、缺乏稳定三维结构，且相似蛋白间序列相似度低。这种构象异质性限制了传统实验手段，加之低序列相似度导致数据稀缺，使得对相似或不同IDP的分类与检测变得困难，而这对于理解生物学与进化具有重要意义。

Method: 提出Multi-task ProtBERT（MT-ProtBERT），是面向低数据场景对ProtBERT的多任务扩展。其整合了动态窗口掩码（Dynamic Window Masking）、多尺度一维卷积分类器（MS-Conv1D），以及联合优化掩码语言建模与生物化学导向任务的辅助目标。

Result: 在两项低数据任务上评估：（i）短序列小数据集中的磷酸化位点预测（S/T/Y）；（ii）两个小数据集（684与530条序列）上的蛋白致密化预测，序列长度可比于典型无序区域。MT-ProtBERT在所有任务上持续优于基于RNN的IDP专用模型PARROT。

Conclusion: 结合自监督任务与生物化学导向任务、并采用多尺度学习，能够在数据稀缺条件下实现对非结构化蛋白的稳健建模。

Abstract: Intrinsically disordered proteins (IDPs) differ from folded proteins in that they are dynamic, lack a stable three-dimensional conformation, and have low sequence similarity between similar proteins. The conformational heterogeneity of IDPs - while beneficial for their diverse functions - limits the use of traditional experimental tools to determine their conformation. The experimental difficulty, along with low sequence similarity, results in data scarcity, and makes it difficult to classify/detect IDPs that are similar or dissimilar, a task relevant to understand biology and evolution. We address this challenge using Multi-task ProtBERT (MT-ProtBERT), a multi-task extension of ProtBERT tailored for low-data regimes. MT-ProtBERT integrates Dynamic Window Masking, a Multi-Scale 1D Convolutional classifier (MS-Conv1D), and auxiliary objectives that jointly optimize masked language modeling and biochemistry-informed tasks. We evaluate this framework on two tasks under limited data: (i) phosphorylation site prediction (S/T/Y) in short sequences and small datasets, and (ii) protein compaction prediction on two small datasets (684 and 530 sequences), including sequences comparable in length to typical disordered regions. MT-ProtBERT consistently outperforms PARROT, an RNN-based IDP-specific model, across all tasks. These results demonstrate that combining self-supervised and biochemistry-informed tasks, and multi-scale learning enables robust modeling of unstructured proteins under data scarcity.

</details>


### [44] [Concept Drift from a Causal Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25340)
*Eduardo V. L. Barboza,Jean Paul Barddal,Robert Sabourin,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从结构因果模型（SCM）视角重新定义概念漂移，提出按因果来源分类的漂移分类法，并构建基于SCM的数据流生成器，实现可控的机制级漂移模拟，用于更真实地评估自适应学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念漂移定义多基于联合分布P(x,y)的变化，未能区分数据生成过程中究竟是哪个组件发生了变化，导致对漂移的理解和评估不够细致、缺乏因果层面的解释性。

Method: 基于结构因果模型（SCM）提出概念漂移的因果视角，建立按因果来源分类的漂移分类法（外生变量变化、内生机制变化、混杂因素变化、目标生成过程变化）；据此开发SCM数据流生成器，模拟受控的机制级漂移事件，并结合因果发现方法构建基于真实依赖结构的数据流。

Result: 实验刻画了各类漂移的分布效应，表明不同因果来源的漂移会诱发不同的分布偏移模式和预测行为；基于真实依赖结构的数据流可提供更真实、更具信息量的评估场景；利用生成数据还能提升下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 研究非平稳环境下的自适应学习方法时，考虑因果结构至关重要；该工作为因果感知的非平稳环境评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Concept drift is a common phenomenon in real-world data streams, in which changes in the data-generating distribution can degrade predictive model performance. Most existing definitions characterize drift as changes in the joint distribution $P(\mathbf{x}, y)$, without distinguishing which component of the data-generating process has changed. In this work, we introduce a causal perspective on concept drift based on Structural Causal Models (SCMs). We propose a taxonomy that categorizes drift events by their causal origin, including changes in exogenous variables, endogenous mechanisms, confounders, and target-generating processes. Building on this framework, we develop an SCM-based data stream generator that simulates controlled mechanism-level drift events. Our experiments empirically characterize the distributional effects of each drift type and show that drifts with different causal origins induce distinct patterns of distribution shift and predictive behavior. Furthermore, by integrating causal discovery methods, we use our framework to construct data streams grounded in real-world dependency structures, enabling more realistic and informative evaluation scenarios. We also demonstrate that leveraging the generated data can improve downstream performance. These results highlight the importance of accounting for causal structure when studying and evaluating adaptive learning methods, and establish a foundation for causally-aware evaluation in non-stationary environments.

</details>


### [45] [Extending FunctionGemma for Practical On-Device Mobile Function Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25373)
*Ali Rezagholizadeh,Soheila Samiee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 FunctionGemma 270M-it 扩展至实际 Android 工作流，构建了约 9,500 条对话的合成数据集 MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED，覆盖 15 类设备控制，微调后端到端准确率从 29.3% 提升至 76.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧助手需要将自然语言映射到本地系统动作的函数调用模型，但现有资源多聚焦于 Web API 或狭窄的移动动作目录，缺乏覆盖实用 Android 工作流的数据和模型。

Method: 提出 MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED 合成、经 schema 校验的数据集（约 9,500 条对话、15 类设备控制，涵盖消息、通话、相机/截图、亮度、设备状态查询、手电筒、应用管理等）；用 TRL 监督微调，采用仅补全损失，训练出扩展专家模型以及与 Google MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE 联合训练的融合模型。

Result: 在 MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED 上端到端准确率从基础模型 29.3%、Google Mobile-Actions 变体 17.2% 提升至 76.5%；融合模型在该数据集保留 76.5%，在 MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE 上达 82.3%（较 Google 专家的 90.3% 低 8.0 个百分点），以换取类别覆盖翻倍。

Conclusion: 释放数据集、微调模型、可复现的训练/评估流水线及 Android 演示，表明紧凑的本地函数调用是实现低延迟、隐私保护移动助手的可行路径。

Abstract: On-device assistants require function-calling models that map natural language to local system actions, but existing resources emphasize web APIs or narrow mobile-action catalogs. We extend FunctionGemma 270M-it to practical Android workflows by introducing MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, a synthetic, schema-validated dataset of ~9,500 conversations covering fifteen device-control categories, including messaging, phone calls, camera/screenshot, brightness control, device-status queries, flashlight control, and application management. We fine-tune the 270M model with TRL supervised fine-tuning under completion-only loss, producing an extended specialist and a combined model trained jointly with Google's MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE. On MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED, end-to-end accuracy improves from 29.3% for the base model and 17.2% for Google's Mobile-Actions variant to 76.5%. The combined model retains 76.5% on MOBILEACTIONSEXTENDED and reaches 82.3% on MOBILEACTIONSGOOGLE, down from the 90.3% of Google's Mobile-Actions specialist, representing an 8.0-percentage-point trade-off in return for doubling category coverage. We release the dataset, fine-tuned models, reproducible training/evaluation pipeline, and an Android demo, highlighting compact local function calling as a practical path towards low-latency and privacy-preserving mobile assistants.

</details>


### [46] [Deep Reinforcement Learning on Item-Compatibility Graphs for One-Dimensional Bin Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25397)
*M. Aslı Aydın*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端、尺寸无关的图强化学习框架求解一维装箱问题（1D-BPP），在BPPLIB基准上零样本泛化并取得优于构造启发式和部分学习基线的最优性间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1D-BPP是经典NP难组合优化问题，应用广泛（物流、制造、云资源管理）。深度强化学习已成为数据驱动优化的竞争性范式，但现有学习型装箱方法多针对2D/3D变体，面向1D-BPP的智能学习求解器仍然稀缺。

Method: 将装箱过程建模为物品兼容图上的马尔可夫决策过程，作为结构化知识表示，每个动作为合并两个可相容的部分箱。使用图神经网络actor-critic策略提取关系特征，通过强化学习训练，并用随机束搜索解码，使单一模型可零样本泛化到任意规模实例。系统研究了图编码器、DRL算法、奖励函数、训练分布与超参数。

Result: 在完整BPPLIB基准上零样本评估：相对构造启发式将平均最优性间隙从2.66%降至2.31%，在结构化实例上提升最大；在同基准上相较学习基线，在九类族中的大多数取得更低间隙且跨实例分布更稳定；在最难的基准族上，超过依赖列生成与整数规划的先进学习求解器，且完全不使用求解器。分组遗传算法整体仍领先。

Conclusion: 该图强化学习框架为1D-BPP提供了尺寸无关、无需外部求解器的学习型求解方案，在多数场景下优于构造启发式与学习基线并表现更稳定。分组遗传算法仍在整体上占优，论文进一步分析了残余间隙的产生位置与原因。

Abstract: The one-dimensional bin packing problem (1D-BPP) is a classical NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem with applications ranging from logistics and manufacturing to cloud resource management. Although deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has become a competitive paradigm for data-driven optimization, most learned packing methods target 2D and 3D variants, and intelligent learned solvers for 1D-BPP remain scarce. In this paper, we present a novel end-to-end, size-agnostic graph reinforcement learning framework for 1D-BPP. We formulate the packing process as a Markov decision process on an item-compatibility graph, serving as a structural knowledge representation in which every action merges two partial bins that fit together. A graph neural network actor-critic policy extracts relational features from this representation and is trained through reinforcement learning and decoded by stochastic beam search, enabling a single trained model to generalize zero-shot to instances of any size. We conduct a systematic empirical study across graph encoders, DRL algorithms, reward functions, training distributions, and hyperparameters. Evaluated zero-shot on the full BPPLIB benchmark against a constructive heuristic, a grouping genetic algorithm, and recent learned methods, our data-driven policy lowers the mean optimality gap of the constructive heuristic from 2.66\% to 2.31\%, with the largest gains on structured instances. Against learned baselines evaluated on the same benchmark, it attains a lower gap on most of the nine families and is far more stable across instance distributions. On the hardest benchmark family, it outperforms a state-of-the-art learned solver that relies on column generation and integer programming, while using no solver at all. A grouping genetic algorithm remains ahead overall, and we analyze where and why the residual gap arises.

</details>

*Kaustubh Tangsali,Mohammad Amin Nabian,Kelvin Lee,Carmelo Gonzales,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了几何条件神经代理模型的不确定性量化方法，比较GP、MC dropout和深度集成三种方法在三个工业CAE数据集上的表现，发现各方法性能随数据集和评价标准变化，需根据下游决策选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经代理模型可加速CAE工作流，但在设计中使用时需要在整个几何、空间预测场和工程关注量上保持有意义的不确定性估计，因此需要研究现有UQ方法适配到几何条件神经代理模型后的行为。

Method: 比较一种闭式方法和两种基于采样的方法——基于高斯过程（GP）的方法、concrete Monte Carlo (MC) dropout和深度集成，在三个大型工业相关CAE数据集（外部空气动力学和碰撞动力学）上进行评估，考察预测不确定性的幅度可信度、误差定位、对陌生输入的响应以及对派生工程量的信息量。

Result: 在DrivAerStar数据集上，三种方法普遍对预测误差较大的位置分配更高的不确定性，基于验证的重缩放使区间覆盖率在分布内测试集上接近名义值。在AirFRANS和汽车碰撞数据集上，结果也显示出有用的误差排序和区间估计，但各方法的相对性能随数据集和评价标准变化。

Conclusion: UQ方法和评价指标的选择应基于预期的下游CAE决策。

Abstract: Neural surrogates can substantially accelerate computer-aided engineering (CAE) workflows, but their use in design requires uncertainty estimates that remain meaningful across varying geometries, spatial prediction fields, and engineering quantities of interest. We investigate how established uncertainty quantification (UQ) approaches behave when adapted to geometry-conditioned neural surrogates. We compare one closed-form and two sampling-based approaches-a Gaussian process (GP)-based method, concrete Monte Carlo (MC) dropout, and deep ensembles-and evaluate them on three large, industry-relevant CAE datasets for external aerodynamics and crash dynamics.
  We examine whether predicted uncertainties have credible magnitudes, identify locations with larger prediction errors, respond to unfamiliar inputs, and remain informative for derived engineering quantities. On the DrivAerStar dataset, where all three methods are compared, each generally assigns higher uncertainty to locations with larger prediction errors, and validation-based rescaling brings interval coverage close to nominal on a disjoint in-distribution test set. Results on AirFRANS and automotive crash also show useful error ranking and interval estimates, but the relative performance of the methods changes with the dataset and evaluation criterion. UQ methods and evaluation metrics should therefore be selected based on the intended downstream CAE decision.

</details>


### [48] [Lightweight Ranking Heads: Accelerating Multi-Task Experimentation in Production Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25433)
*Sanjay Surendranath Girija,Aniruddh Nath,Li Wei,Yanhao Jiang,Shawn Andrews,Lukasz Heldt,Yi Wu,Aditya Mahajan,Mohit Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Lightweight Ranking Heads (Light Heads) 框架，可在现有多任务排序模型中动态注入新任务，无需冷启动或重训主干模型，将多任务实验迭代周期从数周缩短至数天，并已在 YouTube 规模成功部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生产级推荐系统依赖复杂的多任务排序模型，引入新预测任务会带来瓶颈：与现有任务产生负向冲突，且因主干模型和下游模型需昂贵重训、奖励组合公式需调参，导致开发与实验周期过长。核心挑战是提升实验迭代速度。

Method: Light Heads 面向持续在线学习环境设计：动态注入新任务，避免模型冷启动与主干重训；采用 stop-gradient 与无状态每日训练严格隔离新任务，缓解任务冲突；通过集中式配置同时向多个模型添加 Light Heads，解锁更快的数据生成与下游模型协同训练。

Result: 在 YouTube 规模成功部署，将多任务实验迭代周期从数周降至数天；论文分析了无状态冷启动头的训练动态，将其性能与完整头进行对比，并展示 Light Heads 支持了新排序任务的快速 A/B 实验与部署，带来可衡量的生产价值。

Conclusion: Light Heads 通过隔离新任务、免重训主干与集中式配置，有效解决了多任务排序模型快速实验的瓶颈，实现了新排序任务的快速实验与上线，并产生实际生产收益。

Abstract: Modern production-scale recommender systems rely on complex, multi-task ranking models. Introducing new prediction tasks into these massive systems often causes bottlenecks - it risks negative task conflicts with existing tasks, and can lead to long development and experimentation cycles due to the expensive retraining of backbone models and downstream models or tuning of reward combination formulas. To address the critical challenge of slow experimentation velocity, we introduce the Lightweight Ranking Heads (Light Heads) framework. Designed for continuous online learning environments, Light Heads enable the dynamic injection of new tasks into existing multi-task ranking models, effectively obviating the need for model cold-starting and retraining of backbone models. By utilizing stop-gradients and stateless daily training, this design strictly isolates new tasks, mitigating the risk of adverse task conflicts. Crucially, this framework uses a centralized configuration that allows Light Heads to be added to multiple models simultaneously, unblocking faster training data generation and co-training of downstream models. Successfully deployed at YouTube scale, this approach reduces the iteration cycle for multi-task experimentation from several weeks to days. In this paper, we detail the system architecture, analyze the training dynamics of stateless cold-started heads, compare their performance to full heads, and demonstrate how Light Heads have enabled the rapid A/B experimentation and deployment of new ranking tasks that yield measurable production value.

</details>


### [49] [PermuFormer: Multi-Task Pretraining for Permutation Representation in Algebraic Combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25438)
*Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PermuFormer：一个在 28 亿 token 的多任务、多编码语料上自回归训练的 Transformer，用于代数组合学中面向排列任务的预训练，可有效微调至未见基础任务与复杂研究级任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI for math 领域多关注用前沿推理模型以语言为媒介解决明确问题，而专用小模型仍是重要组成。但专用模型通常直接基于数学对象（如图、数列）从零训练，难以获得捕捉数学多面性的领域感知表示。作者希望为代数组合学中的排列任务引入预训练方法，以提升下游微调效果。

Method: 构建 28 亿 token 的多任务、多编码语料；训练自回归 Transformer（PermuFormer）作为预训练起点；随后在预训练未见的基础任务及更复杂的研究级任务上微调；并与从零训练的相同架构、基线 MLP、以及同规模通用语言模型微调结果对比；同时分析模型内部机制。

Result: PermuFormer 作为微调起点，在未见基础任务和复杂研究级任务上常优于从零训练的相同架构、基线 MLP 及同规模微调通用语言模型。机制分析显示：部分任务可直接从提示的内部表示线性解码，而另一些任务需多轮生成后才能解码答案。

Conclusion: 预训练可帮助排列专注型专用模型获得领域感知表示，从而提升下游任务表现；PermuFormer 展示了面向数学对象的多任务预训练在代数组合学中的有效性，并揭示了不同任务在内部计算机制上的差异。

Abstract: Diverse pretraining has been shown to be an effective method for learning reusable, domain-aware representations that provide a starting point for fine-tuning on downstream tasks. While much of the excitement in AI for math has been concentrated in the use of frontier reasoning models to solve well-specified problems through the medium of language, narrow, specialized models remain an important component of the AI for math ecosystem. In contrast to large language models, specialized models are usually trained directly on the mathematical objects themselves (e.g., graphs, sequences of numbers) rather than the textual descriptions that characterize these objects. However, the common practice of training specialists from scratch may limit their ability to develop domain-aware representations that capture the multifaceted nature of mathematics. In this paper, we describe an approach to pretraining for permutation-focused tasks in algebraic combinatorics. We introduce PermuFormer, an autoregressive transformer trained on a 2.8 billion token multi-task, multi-encoding corpus. We show that PermuFormer is an effective starting point for fine-tuning on basic tasks unseen during pretraining and more complex research-level tasks, frequently outperforming the same architecture trained from scratch, baseline MLPs, and a fine-tuned generic language model of comparable size. We also analyze some of the internal mechanisms by which PermuFormer learns to solve training tasks. For example, we show that while some tasks can be linearly decoded directly from the internal representation of the prompt, other tasks require multiple rounds of generation before the answer can be decoded.

</details>


### [50] [Mean Velocity Matching: Rethinking Generative Dynamics in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25444)
*Yunhong Zhang,Changjie Cao,Zhihua Zhang,Bingli Liu,Zongjie Cao,Zongyong Cui,Ying Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mean Velocity Matching (MVM)，用单一速度场直接参数化扩散模型的随机反向动力学，无需额外估计或重建 score，并统一了随机与确定性采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 velocity-based 生成模型虽然只需学习单一传输场、形式简单，但其标准形式是确定性的；若要扩展到随机反向动力学，通常需要额外的 score 信息或速度到 score 的中间重建步骤。因此作者希望在保留单场预测优势的同时，直接支持随机反向过程。

Method: MVM 构造了一个高斯扰动过程，使恢复导向速度 (x0 - xt)/t 的条件期望直接构成反向 SDE 的漂移项，从而仅用一个学习场即可参数化随机反向过程，无需单独估计或重建 score。针对该速度在 t=0 附近回归无界的问题，引入 sqrt(t) 缩放参数化，在保持反向动力学的同时给出有界训练目标。同一学习场还可导出确定性的概率流 ODE，实现随机与确定性采样的统一建模。

Result: 基于 Transformer 的生成模型在 ImageNet 32×32 上达到 FID = \MVMImageNetThirtyTwoFID（\MVMImageNetThirtyTwoNFE 步 NFE）；在 ImageNet 256×256 上达到 FID = \MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixFID（\MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixNFE 步 NFE）。受控 SDE–ODE 对比显示：极低 NFE 下 ODE 表现更好，而函数评估次数充足时随机反向过程取得更低 FID。

Conclusion: MVM 为随机反向动力学提供了直接的单场参数化方法，在保持有竞争力的生成质量的同时，统一了随机与确定性采样框架。

Abstract: This work studies prediction parameterization for stochastic generative dynamics in diffusion models. Existing velocity-based generative models provide the simplicity of learning a single transport field, but their standard formulation is deterministic, whereas stochastic extensions generally require additional score information or an intermediate velocity-to-score reconstruction. To retain single-field prediction while directly supporting stochastic reverse dynamics, this paper introduces Mean Velocity Matching (MVM). MVM constructs a Gaussian perturbation process for which the conditional expectation of a restoration-oriented velocity, $(x_0-x_t)/t$, directly forms the reverse-SDE drift. Consequently, a single learned field is sufficient to parameterize the stochastic reverse process without separately estimating or reconstructing the score. Because direct regression of this velocity becomes unbounded near $t=0$, MVM further introduces a $\sqrt{t}$-scaled parameterization that preserves the reverse dynamics while yielding a bounded training target. The same learned field also induces a deterministic probability-flow ODE, enabling stochastic and deterministic sampling to be studied within a unified formulation. Experiments with Transformer-based generative models achieve an FID of $\MVMImageNetThirtyTwoFID$ at \MVMImageNetThirtyTwoNFE\ NFE on ImageNet $32\times32$ and $\MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixFID$ at \MVMImageNetTwoFiftySixNFE\ NFE on ImageNet $256\times256$. Controlled SDE--ODE comparisons further show that the ODE performs better under very low NFE, whereas the stochastic reverse process achieves lower FID when sufficient function evaluations are available. These results demonstrate that MVM provides a direct single-field parameterization of stochastic reverse dynamics while maintaining competitive generation quality.

</details>


### [51] [A Practical Recipe for Semi-Supervised Federated ASR: Online Pseudo-Labels with Server Update Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25471)
*Wonho Bae,Zakaria Aldeneh,Martin Pelikan,Jan "Honza" Silovsky,Tatiana Likhomanenko,Sheikh Shams Azam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究半监督联邦学习（SSFL）在自动语音识别（ASR）中的表现，指出教师模型与服务器锚定更新是决定成败的两个耦合设计轴，并据此提出训练指南，在11组对比中9组超越最强基线，域内平均提升20.8%、跨域10.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSFL在客户端无标注数据上用教师生成伪标签训练，但ASR场景下伪标签错误会在输出序列和训练轮次间累积导致发散，与全监督联邦学习存在较大差距。作者希望找出弥补这一差距的关键设计因素。

Method: 围绕两个耦合轴展开实验分析：教师轴（每客户端在线教师、广播式全局教师、以及由全局转在线的过渡式教师）与锚定轴（服务器在轮次间是否继续在标注数据上训练）。同时考察数据增强与批大小对稳定性的影响，并分析种子数据离散度及其与客户端数据重叠程度对稳定化需求的域依赖性。

Result: 单客户端在线教师自身会发散，但稳定后域内可匹配或超越固定全局教师，域偏移下也具竞争力；种子越强在线教师优势越小，过渡式教师可匹配或超越两者。服务器必须轮次间继续在标注数据上训练，否则在线教师漂移，且这种交替训练比种子模型更主导收敛。两个轴不可分割：激进的教师选择只有在锚定稳定训练后才有效，而稳定性对数据增强和批大小高度敏感。所需稳定化程度取决于种子数据离散度及其与客户端数据的重叠。

Conclusion: 教师与锚定是SSFL-ASR中相互耦合的核心设计轴，据此得出的训练指南在11组对比中9组优于最强先前方法，域内平均提升20.8%、跨域10.0%，缩小了与全监督联邦学习的差距。

Abstract: Semi-supervised federated learning (SSFL) trains models on clients' unlabeled data using a teacher to generate pseudo-labels, with a small labeled seed dataset on the server. Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) is particularly fragile here: pseudo-label errors compound across the output sequence and across training rounds into divergence, leaving a large gap to fully-supervised FL. We show that closing this gap turns on two coupled design axes -- the teacher (which model generates the pseudo-labels) and the anchor (the server-side updates on labeled data that stabilize training). On the teacher axis, a per-client online teacher (each client's own evolving model) diverges on its own, but once stabilized it matches or beats the broadcast global teacher (one server model, fixed within a round) -- decisively in-domain and competitively under domain shift. As the seed grows stronger and the online teacher's advantage narrows, a transitioning teacher (global $\rightarrow$ online at round $r$) matches or beats both. On the anchor axis, the server must keep training on labeled data between rounds -- otherwise the online teacher drifts -- and this interleaving, more than the seed model, governs convergence. The two axes are inseparable: aggressive teacher choices pay off only once the anchor stabilizes training, which is highly sensitive to data augmentation and batch size -- the settings that govern how much input and gradient noise the server injects. How much stabilization is needed is domain-dependent, governed by the dispersion of the seed data and its overlap with client data. These findings yield guidelines for SSFL in ASR training, improving over the strongest prior method on 9 of 11 pairs, by $20.8\%$ on average in-domain and $10.0\%$ cross-domain, narrowing the gap to fully-supervised FL.

</details>


### [52] [Terminal Shrinkage Averaging Reveals a Schedule-Estimator Interaction in LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25482)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Yixin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Terminal Shrinkage Averaging (TSA)，在原始最终迭代与近期检查点平均之间做插值，以解耦学习率调度与部署估计器，从而在保持训练后期进展的同时降低终端方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 LLM 预训练直接返回原始最终迭代，将生成参数轨迹的学习率调度与构造部署模型的估计器（原始最终迭代或检查点平均）耦合在一起。促进优化进展的调度可能与最小化原始最终迭代方差的调度不同，因此分离这两者有潜力在训练后期保持进展的同时减少返回模型的方差。

Method: 提出 Terminal Shrinkage Averaging (TSA)，在原始最终迭代与近期检查点平均之间进行插值，以平衡近期进展与终端方差。在局部二次近似下分析 TSA 如何改变偏好的终端学习率调度，并通过一系列受控的 NanoChat 实验测试这种交互作用。

Result: 收益可迁移到 depth-22 NanoChat，组合调度与估计器提升了验证质量；一次合格的 time-to-GPT-2 运行也比实验所用的公开基线更快完成，提供了基准加速的初步证据。

Conclusion: 通过分离学习率调度与部署估计器，TSA 能在保持后期优化进展的同时降低终端方差，并在更大规模 NanoChat 及 time-to-GPT-2 基准上展现出一致增益与加速潜力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) pretraining conventionally returns the raw final iterate. This couples two design choices: the learning-rate schedule that generates the parameter trajectory and the estimator that constructs the deployed model (e.g. the raw final iterate or a checkpoint average). A schedule that promotes optimization progress may differ from one that minimizes variation in the raw final iterate. Separating these choices creates an opportunity to maintain progress late in training while reducing variation in the returned model. To this end, we propose \emph{Terminal Shrinkage Averaging (TSA)}, which interpolates between the raw final iterate and the average of recent checkpoints to balance recent progress against terminal variation. We analyze how TSA changes the preferred terminal learning-rate schedule under a local quadratic approximation and test this interaction through a sequence of controlled NanoChat experiments. Finally, we demonstrate that the resulting gains transfer to depth-22 NanoChat, where the combined schedule and estimator improve validation quality. A qualifying time-to-GPT-2 run also finishes faster than the public baseline used in our experiments, providing preliminary evidence of benchmark acceleration.

</details>


### [53] [Learning Defensive Policies against Diverse Inference Attacks for Smart Meter Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25484)
*Ruichang Zhang,Mustafa A. Mustafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种代理引导的分层强化学习框架，通过电池负载整形策略在智能电表聚合信号中注入误导性电器级签名，实现对未知异构NILM攻击者的黑盒隐私防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电表数据虽提供细粒度能耗可视化，却使用户面临隐私风险。非侵入式负载监测（NILM）等推理攻击可从聚合信号中恢复电器级信息和敏感行为模式。实践中攻击者模型未知且异构，使鲁棒防御十分困难，因此需要一种能泛化到多样且未见攻击者的黑盒推理防御方法。

Method: 将智能电表隐私保护建模为黑盒推理防御问题，提出代理引导的分层强化学习框架。该框架学习基于电池的负载整形策略，向聚合信号注入真实但具有误导性的电器级签名，破坏NILM所利用的结构化模式。采用自监督的聚合结构隐私探针提供基于重构误差的代理奖励，并通过签名库使扰动与电器相关且可通过电池控制物理实现。

Result: 在真实数据集UK-DALE和REDD上实验，展现出强跨模型和跨电器泛化能力。面对六个未见NILM攻击者（UK-DALE覆盖四类电器、REDD覆盖五类电器），该防御使平均电器级RMSE分别提升107%和166%，F1分数分别下降79%和80%。

Conclusion: 理论与实验表明，代理引导的优化可提升攻击者多样性下的推理鲁棒性，为智能电表隐私保护提供了一种能泛化到未知异构攻击者的有效黑盒防御方案。

Abstract: Smart meter (SM) data provides fine-grained visibility into household energy consumption, but also exposes users to privacy risks. Inference attacks, known as non-intrusive load monitoring (NILM), can perform appliance-level inference from aggregate signals and recover sensitive behavioral patterns. In practice, attacker models are unknown and heterogeneous, making robust defense challenging. We formulate SM privacy protection as a black-box inference defense problem, aiming to reduce the recoverability of appliance-level information while generalizing across diverse and unseen attackers. We propose a proxy-guided hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that learns battery-based load-shaping policies to inject realistic but misleading appliance-level signatures into the aggregate signal, thereby disrupting the structured patterns exploited by NILM. A self-supervised aggregate-structure privacy probe provides a reconstruction-error-based surrogate reward for disrupting recoverable load structure, while a signature library makes the perturbations appliance-relevant and physically realizable through battery control. We provide theoretical rationale showing that proxy-guided optimization improves inference robustness under attacker diversity. Experiments on real-world datasets UK-DALE and REDD demonstrate strong cross-model and cross-appliance generalization. Across six unseen NILM attackers, covering four appliances on UK-DALE and five on REDD, our proposed defense increases average appliance-level RMSE by 107% and 166%, respectively, while reducing F1 score by 79% and 80%.

</details>


### [54] [Continuous Optimization for p-adic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25501)
*Julian Salazar,Dimitri Kanevsky,Matt Harvey,Pascal Getreuer,Lucas Dixon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对 p 进参数机器学习模型的连续梯度下降方法，通过将 Q_p 嵌入 Berkovich 仿射线（路径连通的度量树）实现连续可微优化，并成功学习模运算等实数线性模型无法表达的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 p 进优化器是离散的、多为组合搜索，因为 p 进数 Q_p 全不连通，且标准损失在远离极小值处是平坦的，无法进行连续优化。作者希望实现原生的连续梯度下降以训练 p 进参数的模型。

Method: 提出在 Berkovich 仿射线上处理 Q_p：这是 Q_p 的一个典范的、路径连通的扩张，保持其等距性并唯一延拓解析映射。该空间是一个度量树，具有可解释的点与局部导数，作者据此构建有效的优化器与反向传播，并制定梯度下降、动量、Adam 等变体。

Result: 梯度下降的近似能高效学习系数在 Q_p 中的线性模型，用于模运算（一种类似 XOR、实数线性模型无法表达的任务）；同时展示动量与 Adam 变体、线性回归，以及在二进编码层级（Quillian 语义网络）上的分类。

Conclusion: 该方法解决了 Martins (2025) 提出的开放问题，首次实现了 p 进模型的连续原生梯度优化，并开源了库 https://github.com/google-deepmind/padic-ml。

Abstract: We present the first method for native, continuous gradient descent for machine learning models with $p$-adic parameters. Existing native optimizers are discrete, mostly combinatorial searches, as the $p$-adic numbers $\mathbb{Q}_p$ are totally disconnected, with standard losses that are flat away from their minima. To enable continuous optimization, we propose working with $\mathbb{Q}_p$ via its Berkovich affine line: a canonical, path-connected expansion of $\mathbb{Q}_p$ that preserves its isometries and uniquely extends its analytic maps. This hull is a metric tree with interpretable points and local derivatives, which we show enables effective optimizers and backpropagation. We formulate gradient descent and show that its approximations efficiently learn linear models with coefficients in $\mathbb{Q}_p$ to do modular arithmetic, an XOR-like task not expressible by linear models in $\mathbb{R}$. We also demonstrate momentum and Adam variants, linear regression, and classification on binary-encoded hierarchies (Quillian semantic networks), addressing open problems posed by Martins (2025). Library at https://github.com/google-deepmind/padic-ml

</details>


### [55] [Hill Sampling for Test-Time Scaling: A Simple and Better Alternative to Repeated Sampling, Evolution, and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25510)
*Jacob Beck,Philip V. Ogren,Ari Kobren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Hill Sampling：对冻结 LLM 反复采样程序编辑，保留当前最优程序并以其为条件继续采样，在多个可验证科学/算法任务上取得强效果，且优于更复杂的进化搜索与测试时权重学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用测试时算力提升 LLM 求解可验证科学/算法问题的系统，依赖日益复杂的进化搜索框架或测试时训练更新模型参数。作者质疑这些复杂机制是否必要，试图寻找更简单的测试时算力分配策略。

Method: Hill Sampling：从冻结 LLM 反复采样候选程序编辑，保留迄今找到的最优程序，并将后续所有采样都条件化在该最优程序上。在 circle packing、有限集合和/差问题、Erdos 最小重叠问题上，用三个开源权重模型评估。此外，进行比较规模较大的进化策略（ES）直接作用于测试时 LLM 权重的研究。

Result: Hill Sampling 在 circle packing 上刷新已发表方法的最优结果，在 Erdos 最小重叠问题上超过 AlphaEvolve 参考结果，在有限集合和/差问题上也表现强劲；其中 circle packing 与 Erdos 结果仅需在 8 块 NVIDIA H100 上数小时。ES 实验显示：学习权重反而不如将 ES 学习率设为零，零学习率下仍通过固定随机扰动在权重空间搜索；这些扰动有助探索，但 token 采样的随机性更强，重复采样仍显著弱于 Hill Sampling。

Conclusion: 建议采用简单的测试时算力分配策略：反复采样对当前已验证最优解的编辑，然后再考虑引入档案库、多样性机制、进化脚手架或测试时参数学习等额外复杂性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can improve solutions to verifiable scientific and algorithmic problems by spending additional computation at test time. Recent systems achieve strong results with increasingly elaborate evolutionary search harnesses or by updating model parameters during test-time training. We ask how much of this machinery is necessary. We introduce Hill Sampling, a simple procedure that repeatedly samples candidate program edits from a frozen LLM, retains the best program found so far, and conditions all subsequent samples on that program. We evaluate the method on circle packing, sums/differences of sets, and Erdos' minimum-overlap problem using three open-weight models. Hill Sampling sets a new state of the art on circle packing among published methods, improves over the AlphaEvolve reference on Erdos' minimum-overlap problem, and achieves strong results on sums and differences of finite sets. The circle-packing and Erdos results require only hours of wall-clock time on eight NVIDIA H100 GPUs. To our knowledge, we also conduct, the largest study, by parameter count, of evolution strategies (ES) applied directly to LLM weights at test time. Surprisingly, learning the weights is worse than setting the ES learning rate to zero: at zero learning rate, the method is still searching in weight space through fixed random perturbations. Those perturbations can help exploration, but randomness from token sampling is stronger still, and repeated sampling remains substantially weaker than Hill Sampling. These results suggest a simple test-time compute allocation strategy: repeatedly sample edits to the best verified solution found so far, before introducing additional complexity such as adding archives, diversity mechanisms, evolutionary scaffolds, or test-time parameter learning.

</details>


### [56] [A JEPA Recipe for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25541)
*Mingyu Jeon,Suwan Cho,Jae Young Suh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了一种联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)潜变量项的稳定训练配方，使其在表格基础模型先验下与数值预测目标共同收敛，但代价是性能略逊且训练成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的运行中，JEPA的潜变量项在表格基础模型先验上发生坍缩，并连带编码器退化为常数映射，因此需要一种能让潜变量项存活的训练方法。

Method: 让数值预测头读取编码器场而非预测器输出，并以指数移动平均(EMA)差值作为目标；同时两条训练分支均采用平台期停止规则而非固定步数预算，以避免固定时间跨度把减速误判为性能上限。

Result: 在每分支各一次运行的收敛结果中，JEPA分支在147个真实数据集上落后于纯数值分支：分类为32:70（按数据集名去重后29:63），回归为8:24，分类差距较小、回归差距更大，且各分层与各基准上计数方向一致。JEPA达到平台期所需步数是纯数值分支的1.42倍，墙钟时间为1.66倍。

Conclusion: 在该配方下JEPA潜变量项可稳定收敛，但相比纯数值目标在性能上略差，且训练成本更高；结论基于每分支单次运行，需谨慎对待。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models learn to predict cell values in context, whereas world-model self-supervision asks for prediction in representation space (LeCun, 2022; Assran et al., 2023). On a tabular foundation-model prior, the latent term of a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) collapsed in our earlier runs and took the encoder with it to a constant map. We report a recipe under which the latent term survives to convergence beside the value objective: the value head reads the encoder field rather than the predictor, and the target is an exponential moving average (EMA) difference. To bound its cost against the value-only arm, both arms train until a plateau rule stops them, with no fixed step budget. A fixed horizon had confounded a slowdown with a ceiling, since the value-only arm was still improving well past the usual budget. At convergence, in one run per arm, the JEPA arm trails the value-only arm across 147 real datasets, 32:70 wins to losses on classification (29:63 with one entry per dataset name) and 8:24 on regression, the margin small on classification and wider on regression, and the count leans the same way in each stratum and each benchmark. The JEPA arm (jepa) needs 1.42 times as many steps as the value-only arm (ds), and 1.66 times its wall-clock, to reach its plateau.

</details>


### [57] [DefaultGNN: A Dual-Perspective GNN Framework for Predicting Corporate Default from Buyer-Seller Transaction Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25542)
*Junghoon Kim,Hyunsung Kim,Seungyoon Choi,KyoungYong Park,Jihun Lee,YongGu Ji,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用电子税务发票数据构建买方/卖方双视角交易网络，提出DefaultGNN框架预测企业违约，并在实际信用评级中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信用模型依赖稀疏或缺失的财务报表，而交易网络反映真实经济活动，但风险如何通过买卖关系传播尚缺乏深入研究。

Method: 基于六年真实电子税务发票数据，将交易历史与违约事件关联，发现交易驱动风险具有角色依赖性和规模依赖性；据此构建买方视角和卖方视角的多重交易网络，并提出双视角图神经网络框架DefaultGNN。

Result: DefaultGNN显著优于基于属性和基于图的基线模型，尤其对内在风险信号有限的企业效果更好；可视化展示困境交易伙伴如何贡献违约风险；与持牌信用评级机构合作验证，预测可补充现有信用评分模型，在不增加获批企业违约风险的前提下将通过率提高7-11个百分点。

Conclusion: 交易网络为违约预测提供互补信息，双视角图神经网络能有效建模风险传播并提升信贷决策效率。

Abstract: Corporate default prediction is a core problem in financial risk management, yet traditional credit models rely heavily on financial statements that are often sparse or unavailable for many firms. Corporate transaction networks offer a complementary view of real economic activity, but how risk propagates through buyer-seller relationships remains underexplored. We conduct a large-scale empirical study using real-world electronic tax-invoice data spanning six years that links transaction histories with default events, revealing that transaction-driven risk is both role-dependent (buyer or seller) and scale-dependent. Based on these findings, we construct multiplex buyer-view and seller-view transaction networks and propose DefaultGNN, a dual-perspective graph neural network-based framework for corporate default prediction. DefaultGNN integrates both views to model how risk flows through transactional relationships, achieving strong improvements over both attribute-based and graph-based baselines, especially for firms with limited intrinsic risk signals. We further provide interpretable network-based explanations by visualizing how distressed trading partners contribute to default risk. In collaboration with a licensed credit rating agency, we validate that DefaultGNN's predictions complement existing credit scoring models, improving approval rates by 7-11%p without increasing default risk among approved firms. The source code can be found at https://github.com/jhkim611/DefaultGNN

</details>


### [58] [SambaGraph: Action-Reaction Spatio-Temporal Graphs for Soccer Tactical Response Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25569)
*Abel A. Reyes-Angulo,Henry O. Velesaca,Steven Araujo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出SambaGraph，一个用于足球战术攻防响应建模的动作-反应时空图数据集与基准，基于2022世界杯全部64场比赛构建4070个以动作为中心的图序列片段与26270对攻防配对，并在响应分类、防守检索和LLM推理三类任务上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球战术本质上是交互式的：进攻动作会改变对手的防守问题，而观察到的反应取决于多智能体的比赛状态。现有研究缺乏一个以图结构表示攻防互动、可复现的战术响应建模基准数据集。

Method: 从2022 FIFA世界杯全部64场比赛的追踪与事件数据中，整理出4070个以动作为中心的片段，表示为时间对齐的23节点球员-球图序列，包含进攻/防守两种视图、响应标签，以及26270个划分安全的攻防配对。研究三个问题：观测响应能否由图片段分类、能否为查询进攻检索成功防守、图摘要能否支持有据可依的LLM推理。方法包括紧凑签名MLP、融合图-签名双编码器，并与本地LLM进行对比。

Result: 紧凑签名MLP在响应分类上取得0.796±0.007的macro-F1；融合图-签名双编码器在全库防守检索上取得0.471±0.029的Hit@5和0.655±0.051的Hit@10。难负样本最大化配对区分度但未提升检索质量。本地LLM在直接分类上不如监督编码器，在八候选重排中也未超过强原始顺序，但能提供有据可依的战术理由。

Conclusion: SambaGraph可作为基于图的足球战术-响应研究的可复现基准，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Soccer tactics are interactive: an attacking action changes the opponent's defensive problem, and the observed response depends on the multi-agent match state. We introduce SambaGraph, an action--reaction spatio-temporal graph dataset and benchmark for soccer tactical response modeling. From tracking and event data for all 64 matches of the 2022 FIFA World Cup, we curate 4,070 action-centered episodes represented as temporally aligned 23-node player--ball graph sequences with attack/defense views, response labels, and 26,270 split-safe attack--defense pairs. We study three questions: whether observed responses can be classified from graph episodes, whether successful defenses can be retrieved for a query attack, and whether graph-derived summaries support grounded LLM reasoning. A compact signature MLP obtains $0.796\pm0.007$ macro-F1 for response classification, while a fused graph--signature dual encoder reaches $0.471\pm0.029$ Hit@5 and $0.655\pm0.051$ Hit@10 for full-bank defensive retrieval. Hard negatives maximize pair discrimination but not retrieval quality. Local LLMs underperform supervised encoders for direct classification and do not improve over a strong original order in eight-candidate reranking, but they provide grounded tactical rationales. These results position SambaGraph as a reproducible benchmark for graph-based soccer strategy-response research. Code and dataset are available at: https://github.com/areyesan/SambaGraph.

</details>


### [59] [EMGBlend: Heterogeneity-Aware Self-Supervised Pretraining for Gesture and Force Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25582)
*Yuwei Jia,Cheng Zhong,Jinyang Yu,Zhe Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EMGBlend 是一个针对异构公共表面肌电数据集的自监督预训练框架，通过显式机制设计而非简单拼接来实现多源数据融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共表面肌电（sEMG）数据集在电极布局、通道数、频率支持和规模上差异巨大。简单混合预训练会导致通道语义错位、引入某些设备无法观测的频谱目标，并让大规模或高通道数数据集主导学习。

Method: EMGBlend 结合了共享通道补丁与几何感知注意力（geometry-aware attention），将频谱目标限制在每个记录支持的频带内（band-aware targets），并平衡各数据源的曝光度（source balancing）。在 11 个公共 EMG 源上预训练一个 109M 参数模型。

Result: 在手势识别、连续力回归和接触分类任务上，EMGBlend 一致优于匹配的随机初始化和波形重建控制基线。固定预算源控制实验表明，多源预训练提升了手势识别效果，并在力解码上保持竞争力。消融实验证实几何、频带感知目标和源平衡均有助迁移，但跨人 NinaPro 力估计仍然困难。

Conclusion: EMGBlend 展示了如何通过显式机制设计（而非简单拼接）来结合异构 EMG 数据集。代码已开源。

Abstract: Public surface electromyography (EMG) datasets vary widely in electrode layout, channel count, frequency support, and size. Simply mixing them for pretraining can misalign channel semantics, introduce spectral targets that some devices cannot observe, and let large or high-channel-count datasets dominate learning. We introduce EMGBlend, a self-supervised framework designed around these differences. It combines shared channel patches with geometry-aware attention, restricts spectral targets to each recording's supported frequency band, and balances exposure across data sources. We pretrain a 109M-parameter model on 11 public EMG sources and evaluate it on gesture recognition, continuous-force regression, and contact classification. EMGBlend consistently outperforms matched random initialization and waveform reconstruction controls. Fixed-budget source controls show that multi-source pretraining improves gesture recognition and remains competitive for force decoding. Ablations confirm that geometry, band-aware targets, and source balancing each contribute to transfer, although cross-person NinaPro force estimation remains difficult. Overall, EMGBlend shows how heterogeneous EMG datasets can be combined through explicit mechanism design rather than simple concatenation. Code is available at https://github.com/tamanano/EMGBlend

</details>


### [60] [Rewired or Gated? How Instruction Tuning Shapes Knowledge-Conflict Circuits in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25602)
*Shubham Santosh Pandere,Gautam Ranka,Ritika Varshney,Navya Deshmukh,Roushni Sareen,Roshan Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次对基础模型与指令微调模型的"冲突消解回路"进行机制层面比较，发现指令微调并未重建回路，而是对既有注意力头进行门控/重加权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在提示与权重之间取舍由少数可识别的注意力头决定。指令微调改变了冲突下的行为，但其究竟是重塑底层回路，还是仅对已有组件进行门控/重加权，此前尚不清楚。

Method: 在三个模型家族（Llama-3.2-3B、Qwen-2.5-3B、Gemma-3-4B）上进行基础版与指令版的机制对比，采用五种独立方法：节点与边归因、叠加角色分析、因果消融、路径修补。

Result: 五种方法一致指向"门控"：相同层（晚层）的相同注意力头被重加权而非替换，节点重叠度高（0.60–0.82）。行为上，微调使模型更偏向参数化记忆，指令模型比基础模型更易拒绝简短的 counterfactual 语境；但这一怀疑性依赖表述框架，当同一虚假主张以连贯、有证据的段落呈现时即消失。

Conclusion: 指令微调带来的鲁棒性是真实但狭窄的。由于冲突回路是被保留而非重建，基于基础模型校准的可解释性与控制工具应能直接迁移到其部署的指令版本。

Abstract: In language models, the choice between believing the prompt and believing the weights is made by a handful of identifiable attention heads. Instruction tuning changes how models behave under conflict, but whether it rewires the underlying circuit or merely gates/reweights already present components, remains unknown. We provide the first mechanistic base-vs-instruct comparison of conflict-resolution circuits, across three families (Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen-2.5-3B, Gemma-3-4B). Five independent methods, node and edge attribution, superposition role analysis, causal ablation, and path patching, converge on gating, with the same heads, in the same late-layers, are found to be reweighted rather than replaced with a high node overlap (0.60-0.82). Behaviorally, tuning shifts models toward parametric memory, making instruct models reject a terse counterfactual context far more than base ones, the opposite of a naive user-following expectation. Yet this added skepticism is a factor of framing since it disappears when the same false claim is delivered as a coherent, evidential passage. The robustness that instruction tuning buys against terse injection is therefore real but narrow. More broadly, we believe that because the conflict circuit is preserved rather than rebuilt, interpretability and control tools calibrated on base models should transfer directly to their deployed instruct siblings.

</details>


### [61] [What Should a Self-Teacher See? Privileged Context Design for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25623)
*Kanghui Tian,Siyuan Liu,Tianxiang Jiang,Shuai Dong,Yizhuo Li,Tian Ding,Yuan Guo,Songze Li,Haowen Hou,Congcong Wang,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在在线自蒸馏中，提供较抽象的特权上下文（策略名、方法论框架、问题类别）比完整参考解答效果更好，且存储的提示词显著更少；最佳上下文随学生规模和任务而变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究"更多特权信息是否一定带来更好的教师"这一张力，质疑自蒸馏中惯例使用完整参考解答（含最终答案和特定推理路径）作为特权上下文的做法是否最优。

Method: 在在线自蒸馏（OPSD）中，固定学生视图与训练，比较四类特权上下文：默认的完整参考解答、三种离线编译的抽象（命名策略、方法论无关框架、问题类别），以及仅保留答案的对照。主要在竞赛数学任务上进行，并在两种规模（4B、8B）和三个随机种子上评估。

Result: 最优中间上下文在域内峰值均值上比完整解答在4B提升1.4分、8B提升1.6分，同时提示词token约少一个数量级；三个种子下框架与类别上下文在两个规模均有正向平均增益；仅答案上下文与完整解答差距在0.2分以内；初始师生KL无法预测下游表现。

Conclusion: 自教师不应看到它能看到的全部信息，而应提供学生仍能据以行动的抽象层次；最优上下文取决于学生规模与任务。

Abstract: More privileged information does not always make a better teacher. We study this tension in on-policy self-distillation (OPSD), where a frozen copy of the base model scores the student's own rollouts under privileged context, conventionally a complete reference solution that bundles the final answer with one particular reasoning path. Holding the student view and training fixed within each scale, we compare that default against three abstractions compiled offline, a named strategy, a method-independent framing, and a problem category, and against an answer-only control that keeps the destination but removes the path. In the primary runs on competition mathematics, the best intermediate contexts improve the in-domain peak mean over the full solution by 1.4 points at 4B and 1.6 at 8B, while storing an order of magnitude fewer hint tokens. Comparisons across three seeds also show positive mean gains for the framing and category contexts at both scales. Answer-only conditioning remains competitive in the primary runs, within 0.2 points of the full solution at these scales. The preferred context varies with student scale and task. Initial teacher-student KL does not order downstream performance. What a self-teacher should see is therefore not everything it could, but the level of abstraction its student can still act on.

</details>


### [62] [An Exploratory Replica-Overlap Probe of the Grokking Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25634)
*A. C. Opus,J. Q. Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用64个独立种子网络检验grokking转变中RSB启发的权重重叠分布是否变化，但注册的置换对齐步骤不保持网络函数、数值精度不达标且存在协议偏离，导致预注册规则给出UNDETERMINED（C0_INSTRUMENT_INVALID）；事后分析中仅标准差比值具备检验效力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验grokking转变是否伴随RSB（复本对称破缺）启发的成对权重重叠分布变化，并采用预注册的确认性分析流程。

Method: 训练64个独立种子网络，覆盖4种配置，训练至持续收敛或40,000轮上限；对隐藏单元进行置换以对齐模型并对齐后计算权重重叠统计量q_wt与基于预测的q_fn；执行数值精度检验与协议审计；对满足12/16检查点完整性的frac40配置做Hartigan dip事后检验及校准。

Result: 对齐置换未同步置换偏置与头内部单元，不能保持网络函数，q_wt继承该缺陷；数值精度要求未达标且发现协议偏离，注册结论为UNDETERMINED（C0_INSTRUMENT_INVALID）。仅frac40通过完整性要求；事后dip区间含零（Delta dip 95% CI [-0.017, 0.034]），但校准显示dip检验在模拟分离度下功效为零，故该区间无信息量；重叠标准差约增大5.6倍，是唯一在观测效应下有效力的统计量。集成损失仅在预设1%阈值下近似平坦；grokking率0/16、11/16、16/16因训练比例与划分身份混淆而仅为描述性。

Conclusion: 这些数据既不构成确认性零结果，也不构成对Parisi序参量的有效解读；q_wt类统计量因对齐缺陷不可用，dip检验无功效，唯有重叠标准差比值具检验力。grokking率与集成损失结论受设计混淆限制，均为描述性。

Abstract: We trained 64 independently seeded networks in four configurations, continuing each to sustained convergence or a 40,000-epoch ceiling. We then asked whether an RSB-inspired distribution of pairwise weight overlaps changes across the grokking transition. It is the alignment step, not the overlap statistic, that determines what this registered probe can report. The registered implementation permutes hidden units without the corresponding bias and head-internal permutations and therefore does not preserve the network function. Every q_wt value computed through this alignment inherits the defect; q_fn does not, because it is computed from predictions of the unpermuted models. The numerical-precision requirement also failed, and an audit found protocol deviations. Consequently, the pre-registered rule gives no verdict: registered outcome UNDETERMINED (reason code C0_INSTRUMENT_INVALID). These data provide neither a confirmatory null nor a validated reading of the Parisi order parameter. Only frac40 cleared the 12/16 checkpoint-completeness requirement. For this configuration, a post-hoc criterion applied to the same data gave a Hartigan-dip interval containing zero (95% CI for Delta dip = [-0.017, 0.034]), whereas the overlap standard deviation increased by a factor of about 5.6. A post-hoc calibration assigns the dip test zero power at the simulated separations; the interval is therefore uninformative, not evidence of no change. The standard-deviation ratio is the only statistic here with power at the observed effect. Ensemble loss was near-flat only under the pre-specified 1% threshold. Finally, grokking rates of 0/16, 11/16 and 16/16 remain descriptive because train fraction is confounded with split identity.

</details>


### [63] [Efficient Cost-Aware LLM Evaluation via Bayesian Bandit Gittins Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25645)
*Qian Xie,Yueli He,Nairen Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GittinsEval，将 LLM 配置选择建模为成本感知贝叶斯 bandit 问题，利用贝叶斯最优 Gittins 策略决定评估哪个配置及何时停止，并扩展到部分评估配置的 anytime 推荐。实验表明其在多个基准上竞争力强，仅用 1%~2% 的穷举评估成本即可达到近零简单遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穷举评估每个候选 LLM 配置在所有基准项上的表现以找到高性能配置成本过高，因此需要一种高效的配置选择方法。

Method: 将配置选择形式化为成本感知贝叶斯 bandit 问题，提出 GittinsEval：基于贝叶斯最优 Gittins 策略决定下一个评估的配置及停止时机；扩展出适用于完全和部分评估配置的 anytime 推荐规则，采用 LCB 风格得分以考虑后验不确定性；离线预计算后仅需轻量在线更新，计算高效。

Result: 在 GSM8K、PIQA、AlpacaEval 和 MMLU 的响应矩阵上，GittinsEval 持续具有竞争力；在大样本基准上优于配置级贝叶斯优化，在大候选任务上优于成本无关的 bandit 基线；常仅用 1%~2% 的穷举评估成本即可达到近零简单遗憾；自适应停止规则通常在 1%~10% 时触发。

Conclusion: GittinsEval 为 LLM 配置选择提供了一种计算高效、成本感知的贝叶斯 bandit 方案，能在极低评估预算下取得接近最优的表现，并具备实用的自适应停止能力。

Abstract: Exhaustively evaluating every candidate LLM configuration on every benchmark item to identify a high-performing one is costly. We formulate configuration selection as a cost-aware Bayesian bandit problem and propose GittinsEval, which draws on the Bayesian-optimal Gittins policy to determine which configuration to evaluate next and when to stop. We extend the policy with an anytime recommendation rule over both fully and partially evaluated configurations, using an LCB-style score to account for posterior uncertainty. GittinsEval is computationally efficient, requiring only lightweight online updates after offline precomputation. Across GSM8K, PIQA, AlpacaEval, and MMLU response matrices, GittinsEval is consistently competitive, with particularly strong gains over configuration-level Bayesian optimization on large-example benchmarks and over cost-unaware bandit baselines on large-candidate tasks. Crucially, GittinsEval often attains near-zero simple regret using only 1% to 2% of the exhaustive-evaluation cost; it also offers an adaptive stopping rule that typically triggers at 1% to 10%.

</details>


### [64] [From Experts to Sub-experts: Fine-grained Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25655)
*Zhentao Tan,Chang Liu,Yao Liu,Yue Wu,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NSFT，一种从专家级细化到子专家级的细粒度PEFT框架，通过结构化通道分组与重要性选择实现更精准高效的MoE大模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM规模扩大，稠密全参数适配成本高昂，催生了MoE等稀疏模块化架构。这引出PEFT的关键问题：应以何种粒度选择和更新参数？现有方法如LoRA在预定义权重矩阵上操作，专家级稀疏微调则更新整个被选专家。然而作者观察到被激活的专家内部也是稀疏的，只有少部分中间通道对下游任务强烈响应，说明专家级适配仍然过于粗糙。

Method: 提出NSFT（Neural Sub-expert Fine-Tuning）细粒度PEFT框架。它将每个专家沿中间维度分解为结构化通道组，结合路由重要性与专家内部激活显著性来选择任务相关的子专家。针对稀疏部分更新带来的有效更新幅度减小问题，引入学习率缩放和动态梯度缩放进行补偿。

Result: 在OLMoE与Ling-mini-2.0上，针对挑战性领域特定任务和通用基准进行实验，NSFT持续优于代表性PEFT与专家级稀疏微调基线，同时使用显著更少的可训练参数，并保持有竞争力的通用能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，子专家级适配是面向MoE大模型更精准、更高效的PEFT范式。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) scale rapidly, dense full-parameter adaptation becomes increasingly expensive, motivating sparse and modular architectures such as Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models. This shift raises a key question for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT): at what granularity should parameters be selected and updated? Existing PEFT methods such as LoRA operate on predefined weight matrices, while expert-level sparse tuning methods update entire selected experts. However, we observe that activated experts are internally sparse, with only a small fraction of intermediate channels strongly responding to downstream tasks, indicating that expert-level adaptation is still too coarse. We propose NSFT (Neural Sub-expert Fine-Tuning), a fine-grained PEFT framework that refines MoE adaptation from experts to sub-experts. NSFT decomposes each expert along the intermediate dimension into structured channel groups and selects task-relevant sub-experts by combining routing importance with intra-expert activation saliency. To optimize sparse partial updates, NSFT further introduces learning-rate scaling and dynamic gradient scaling to compensate for the reduced effective update magnitude. Experiments on OLMoE and Ling-mini-2.0 across challenging domain-specific tasks and general benchmarks show that NSFT consistently outperforms representative PEFT and expert-level sparse tuning baselines, while using substantially fewer trainable parameters and preserving competitive general capability. These results suggest that sub-expert-level adaptation is a more precise and efficient PEFT paradigm for MoE LLMs.

</details>


### [65] [Targeted Review for AI-Assisted Biodiversity Surveys: Active Continuous-Score Occupancy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25657)
*Timm Haucke,Lauren Harrell,Justin Kay,Mary Clapp,Sara Beery*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ACORN 方法，将 ML 预测融入占域模型，并策略性地选择对下游生态分析最 informative 的样本进行专家复核，在显著减少复核量的同时逼近全人工标注结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家日益依赖机器学习为科学数据集打标签，但模型难免出错，系统性偏差可能污染科学结论。因此研究者需复核大量 ML 标签，然而人力预算有限，亟需优化复核努力的分配方式，以服务于科学目标而非单纯提升分类器精度。

Method: 聚焦生态学中的占域建模（occupancy modeling，估计物种在环境因子条件下可能出现的分布）。提出 Active Continuous-Score Occupancy Modeling（ACORN），将 ML 预测引入占域模型，并主动选择对下游生态分析信息量最大的样本交予专家复核。

Result: 在相机陷阱（camera-trap）与生物声学（bioacoustic）数据集上，ACORN 恢复的生态学结论接近全人工标注数据所得，且所需专家复核量远少于非定向复核策略。

Conclusion: ML 辅助的科学工作流应针对下游推断优化专家投入，而非仅针对分类器精度，尤其在人工复核预算受限时。代码已开源。

Abstract: We increasingly use machine learning to label scientific datasets. The models we develop and deploy are improving all the time, but they are not and will likely never be perfect. Mistakes matter, as errors can propagate into our scientific understanding, particularly when systematically biased. Very reasonably, scientists thus review substantial proportions of ML-generated labels to verify or correct mistakes in pursuit of ensuring their scientific findings are not biased by ML. In this work, we focus on helping scientists optimally allocate this reviewing effort relative to their scientific goals. We focus on a specific class of scientists (ecologists) and a specific, widespread, and impactful modeling target (occupancy modeling, which estimates where species are likely to occur, conditioned on environmental factors). We introduce Active Continuous-Score Occupancy Modeling (ACORN), a method that incorporates ML predictions into occupancy models and strategically selects samples for expert review that are maximally informative for downstream ecological analysis. Across camera-trap and bioacoustic datasets, our method recovers ecological conclusions close to those obtained from fully human-labeled data, while requiring substantially fewer expert reviews than non-targeted review policies. Our results suggest that ML-assisted scientific workflows should optimize expert effort for downstream inference, rather than for classifier accuracy alone, especially when human review budget is limited. Our code is available at https://github.com/timmh/acorn

</details>


### [66] [When Riemann flows with Wasserstein: Generative Modeling of Probability Distributions on Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25659)
*Doron Haviv,Edward De Brouwer,Rishabh Anand,Rex Ying,Aïcha Bentaieb,Gabriele Scalia,Hector Corrada Bravo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出黎曼Wasserstein熵流匹配（RWEFM），在黎曼流形的Wasserstein空间上进行生成建模，利用McCann位移插值作为条件路径，并推出GPU高效的黎曼熵映射近似最优传输映射，在超球面、环面及一般三角网格上实现生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学数据集（如分子构象集合、单细胞组织测量）本质上是"元分布"：非欧几里得域上概率测度的分布。现有生成方法大多假设欧几里得几何，无法捕捉这种结构，因此需要一种尊重数据内在几何的生成框架。

Method: 提出RWEFM，在黎曼流形(M,g)的Wasserstein空间P2(M)上构建生成框架。训练时将神经网络向量场回归到黎曼最优传输速度，使用McCann位移插值作为条件路径；理论上证明该构造在P2(M)上是有效的流匹配方法；并提出黎曼熵映射，作为流形上最优传输映射的GPU高效近似。方法仅需测地距离和投影算子。

Result: 实验表明，通过尊重数据的内在几何，RWEFM能在超球面潜空间中生成完整单细胞样本，并在环面上生成蛋白质构象集合；同时在一般三角网格上成功生成分布，验证了方法不限于具有闭式几何的流形。

Conclusion: RWEFM将流匹配扩展到黎曼流形的Wasserstein空间，为科学数据的元分布生成提供了几何一致且实用的新框架，且因只需测地距离与投影算子而具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Many scientific datasets, such as molecular conformational ensembles or single-cell tissue measurements, are naturally modeled as meta-distributions: distributions over probability measures on non-Euclidean domains. Existing generative methods largely assume Euclidean geometry and fail to capture this structure. We introduce Riemannian Wasserstein Entropic Flow Matching (RWEFM), a generative framework on the Wasserstein space $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathcal{M})$ of a Riemannian manifold $(\mathcal{M},g)$. RWEFM is trained by regressing a neural vector field onto Riemannian optimal transport velocities, using McCann displacement interpolations as conditional paths. We confirm theoretically that this construction leads to a valid flow matching approach on $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathcal{M})$ and introduce the Riemannian Entropic Map, a GPU-efficient approximation of the optimal transport map on manifolds. Our experiments show that by respecting the intrinsic geometry of the data, RWEFM can generate whole single-cell samples in hyperspherical latent spaces and protein conformational ensembles on the torus. As RWEFM requires only a geodesic distance and a projection operator, it is not restricted to manifolds with closed-form geometry, which we demonstrate by generating distributions on a general triangulated mesh.

</details>


### [67] [Marginal Log-Likelihood Increments under Dirichlet-Smoothed Markov Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25675)
*Levin David Schwab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Dirichlet平滑转移模型，推导向训练集加入一条工作流迹后参考加权对数似然的确切变化，将其表示为参考条件分布与模型之间KL散度的加权缩减，并据此得到采集增益上界、协方差恒等式与符号判据；在BPI Challenge 2012贷款申请日志上的案例研究表明，单独迹的预测精度与批量采集质量之间既不必要也不充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程挖掘中的主动学习/迹采集需要在批量层面选择最有价值的工作流迹，但缺乏对"加入一条迹究竟带来多少似然增益"的解析刻画，也缺少对候选间交互性质的判定依据。

Method: 在Dirichlet平滑转移模型下解析推导单条迹加入所致参考加权对数似然变化，将其化为参考条件分布与模型间KL散度的加权缩减；由此导出任意采集策略增益的上界（以可及增益占比表示）、参考加权效应的精确协方差恒等式，以及两候选交互的符号判据，并分析批量目标的子模/超模性。

Result: 批量目标既不满足子模性也不满足超模性；在BPI Challenge 2012贷款申请日志上测量上述三项量，四种"参考加权×预算单位"组合中仅一种出现正选择结果；两个使用相同描述符与标签的回归器中，个体增量预测更准者（中位R² 0.87 vs 0.62）反而只实现较小的可及增益份额（61% vs 69%）。

Conclusion: 单条迹的排序精度既非批量采集质量的必要条件也非充分条件，批量采集目标的结构较为复杂，需依据增益上界与交互符号判据而非个体预测精度来设计选择策略。

Abstract: For a Dirichlet-smoothed transition model, the effect of adding one workflow trace to the training archive is an exact change in reference-weighted log likelihood. We derive that change and show that it is a weighted reduction of Kullback--Leibler divergence between the reference conditionals and the model. From this form we obtain an upper bound on the gain available to any acquisition, which expresses a millinat difference as a share of what is attainable, an exact covariance identity for the effect of the reference weighting, and a sign criterion for the interaction between two candidates, from which the batch objective is neither submodular nor supermodular. A case study on the BPI Challenge 2012 loan-application log measures all three and finds a positive selection result in one of the four combinations of reference weighting and budget unit. There, of two regressors fitted to identical descriptors and identical labels, the one that predicts individual increments far more accurately, median $R^2$ 0.87 against 0.62, realizes the smaller share of the attainable gain, 61 against 69 per cent, so ranking accuracy for individual traces is neither necessary nor sufficient for batch quality.

</details>


### [68] [Graph Domain Adaptation Does Not End with Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25692)
*Ziqian Liu,Yongxue Xu,Enze Zhang,Jiaqi Zhang,Hao Wang,Maolin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 EviGDA，在图域适应中用图无关局部专家补充图表示适应，两专家独立优化、推理时以固定概率混合，在十个数据集十六个迁移任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图域适应方法（传播重设计、分布对齐、源到目标转移建模）在目标预测时仍依赖单一图传播路径。图感知专家与图无关的局部专家在拓扑偏移下可能呈现不同失效模式，因此单一适应后的图表示未必穷尽目标域的预测证据。

Method: EviGDA 框架包含两个专家：图感知专家进行消息传递与熵感知边缘对齐；图无关局部专家仅从源节点特征与标签学习，不做图传播与目标对齐。两专家独立优化，仅在推理阶段通过任务级常数概率混合进行组合，不使用联合训练、学习型路由或目标伪标签。

Result: 在十个数据集、16 个迁移任务上的大量实验表明，EviGDA 优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入图无关局部专家作为互补证据来源，并以简单的推理期常数混合融合，EviGDA 在不增加复杂训练机制的前提下提升了图域适应性能。

Abstract: Graph domain adaptation (GDA) transfers knowledge from a labeled source graph to an unlabeled target graph under shifts in both node attributes and graph structure. Existing methods primarily adapt graph representations through propagation redesign, distribution alignment, or source-to-target transition modeling, but still rely on a single graph-propagating path for target prediction. This leaves open whether an adapted graph representation exhausts the predictive evidence available in the target domain, since the graph-aware expert and graph-free local expert may exhibit different failure modes under topological shifts. To address this limitation, we propose EviGDA, an Evidence-Augmented Graph Domain Adaptation framework that complements graph representation adaptation with a graph-free local expert. The graph-aware expert performs message passing and entropy-aware marginal alignment, while the graph-free local expert learns solely from source node features and labels without graph propagation or target alignment. The two experts are optimized independently and combined only at inference through a task-level constant probability mixture, preserving complementary evidence without joint training, learned routing, or target pseudo-labels. Extensive experiments on ten datasets and 16 transfer tasks show that EviGDA outperforms state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [69] [Fully Byzantine-Resilient Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25701)
*Haejoon Lee,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出完全韧性AC-MARL方法FRAC-MARL，利用两跳消息冗余识别可靠消息，在拜占庭边攻击下参数几乎必然收敛至与无攻击情形相同的极限点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式拜占庭韧性actor-critic多智能体强化学习方法只能保证参数收敛到无攻击极限点的邻域，导致性能下降，无法完全恢复无攻击时的表现。

Method: 提出去中心化方法FRAC-MARL，每个智能体利用两跳消息中的冗余来识别可靠消息；在价值函数与团队奖励函数线性参数化、且对抗行为局限于通信层的拜占庭边攻击设定下进行分析；并引入一种新的拓扑收敛条件，给出构造此类网络的系统方法。

Result: 证明在时变通信图上，智能体参数几乎必然收敛到与无攻击情形相同的极限点；所提拓扑条件可在多项式时间内验证；并在协作多机器人编队控制任务上进行了验证。

Conclusion: FRAC-MARL实现了完全韧性的分布式AC-MARL，克服了以往方法仅收敛到邻域的缺陷，同时给出了可多项式验证的拓扑条件与网络构造方法，并在多机器人编队控制中展示了有效性。

Abstract: We study distributed Byzantine-resilient actor-critic multi-agent reinforcement learning (AC-MARL), where agents collectively learn policies through local interactions. Existing methods guarantee convergence of the agents' parameters only to a neighborhood of the attack-free limit points, resulting in degraded performance. We propose Fully Resilient AC-MARL (FRAC-MARL), a decentralized method in which each agent leverages redundancy in two-hop messages to identify reliable messages.
