Result: 1) 49-100%的提示出现分歧，单个token翻转常级联为轨迹级分歧；2) 22层累积主体误差无法区分翻转与非翻转步骤，结果主要取决于LM头前二logit边距相对于前二候选间方向性扰动的大小；3) 分析给出的五项可检验预测全部被实验证实，包括"更广范围地使用FP32计算反而使一致性变差"；4) 选择性FP32 LM头重算在低批（batch≤4）单流推理下，A10G上提升确切一致率+22-36个百分点（L4与A100上+12-21个百分点），延迟开销低于4%；5) 该方法为部分缓解而非通用确定性保证，当主体误差主导时（如batch≥8及端到端FP8）收益消失。

Conclusion: 贪心解码的确定性是精度依赖的假象。输出翻转的可预测性主要由LM头处的logit边距决定，而非主体累积误差；据此设计的阈值触发式FP32 LM头重算可作为低开销的部分缓解方案，但其有效性存在明确的适用边界（低批、非FP8、主体误差不主导），并推测训练期精度稳定性是决定因素之一。

Abstract: Greedy decoding from large language models is commonly treated as deterministic. We show it is not precision-invariant: the same model, prompt, and decoding algorithm produce different outputs in BF16 versus FP16 on identical hardware. Across our evaluations of six models (1.1B-7B parameters, four families; divergence additionally characterised at 12B) and three benchmarks, 49-100\% of prompts diverge; a single token flip often cascades into trajectory-level divergence. We develop an empirical error-propagation analysis and find that 22 layers of accumulated body error do not distinguish flipping from non-flipping steps; the outcome depends primarily on the top-two logit margin at the LM head relative to the directional perturbation between the top-two candidates. The analysis makes five testable predictions about intervention outcomes, including that applying more FP32 compute (broader scope) makes agreement worse. The experiments match all five predictions. The best-performing low-overhead intervention we evaluate, selective FP32 LM head recomputation, triggered only when the margin falls below a threshold, delivers +22-36 pp exact agreement on A10G (+12-21 pp on L4 and A100) at less than 4\% latency overhead in low-batch (batch size <=4) single-stream inference. We map the applicability boundary across six models and four batch sizes, and hypothesise that training-time precision stability is a determining factor. The method is a partial mitigation rather than a universal determinism guarantee: its benefit vanishes when body-originated error dominates, including at batch size >=8 and under end-to-end FP8 in our tests.

</details>


### [140] [Label-Efficient Learning for Ground-Based Sky-Image Classification: A Benchmark of Transfer Learning, Active Learning, and Pseudo-Labeling on GCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26631)
*Esther Bou Dagher,Viktoriya Bu-Dager,Boguslaw Zegarlinski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究地面云分类的标签效率，基于GCD数据集在有限标注预算下比较三种策略：监督迁移学习、基于不确定性的主动学习和高置信度伪标签，发现强监督迁移学习已高度标签高效，主动与半监督方法仅带来有限增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的地面云分类对大气监测、太阳能预测、航空气象评估和气候观测系统都很重要，但天空图像标注耗时，尤其当云类型视觉相似或混合时，因此需要研究在有限标注预算下的标签效率问题。

Method: 不提出新架构，而是在GCD数据集上基准测试三种策略：监督迁移学习、基于不确定性的主动学习、高置信度伪标签。以ImageNet预训练的ResNet50作为公共冻结骨干，在1%到100%的训练标签预算下用五个随机种子重复实验，并进行诊断分析。

Result: 监督迁移学习已高度标签高效：1%标签时测试准确率0.635±0.018，40%标签时0.730±0.002，接近全标签的0.735±0.003。主动学习和伪标签与监督采样相当，在某些指标和预算下有小的提升，但整体无大或一致的增益。诊断显示被接受的伪标签准确率0.946到0.977，但偏向较容易的高置信度云型；不确定性采样更倾向查询视觉困难的组，包括Mixed及易混淆的Stratocumulus和Cumulonimbus组，但仅带来适度增益。

Conclusion: 总体而言，迁移学习显著降低了GCD的标注需求，而简单的主动与半监督策略相较强监督基线仅提供有限额外收益。

Abstract: Accurate ground-based cloud classification is important for atmospheric monitoring, solar-energy forecasting, aviation weather assessment, and climate observation systems. However, reliable sky-image annotation is time-consuming, especially when cloud types are visually similar or mixed. We study the label efficiency of deep learning for ground-based cloud classification using the Ground-based Cloud Dataset (GCD). Rather than proposing a new architecture, we benchmark three practical strategies under limited annotation budgets: supervised transfer learning, uncertainty-based active learning, and high-confidence pseudo-labeling. An ImageNet-pretrained ResNet50 is used as a common frozen backbone, with experiments repeated over five random seeds for label budgets from $1\%$ to $100\%$ of the training labels. Supervised transfer learning is already highly label-efficient: test accuracy increases from $0.635 \pm 0.018$ with $1\%$ labels to $0.730 \pm 0.002$ with $40\%$ labels, approaching the full-label result of $0.735 \pm 0.003$. Active learning and pseudo-labeling are competitive with supervised sampling and provide small improvements for some metrics and budgets, but neither gives a large or consistent aggregate gain. Diagnostic analyses show that accepted pseudo-labels are reliable, with accuracy from $0.946$ to $0.977$, but biased toward easier high-confidence sky-type groups. In contrast, uncertainty sampling preferentially queries visually challenging groups, including Mixed and the confusable Stratocumulus and Cumulonimbus groups, but these targeted acquisitions yield only modest gains. Overall, transfer learning substantially reduces annotation requirements for GCD, while simple active and semi-supervised strategies provide limited additional benefit over a strong supervised baseline.

</details>


### [141] [MAGIC: Mixed-Granularity Agent Graphs via Incremental Construction with Dense-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26667)
*Kairui Yang,Ziheng Yi,Xunkai Li,Minghao An,Zhanke Liu,Zekai Chen,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAGIC是一个密集奖励强化学习框架，通过为每个功能角色在局部选择粒度（单个智能体或可复用群体）来生成混合粒度的多智能体协作图，在八个基准上超越SOTA并展现高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作拓扑同时影响LLM多智能体系统的性能与执行成本。任务在复杂度和所需能力上存在差异，因此需要生成任务特定的协作图来指定智能体参与和信息流。然而，现有代表性拓扑生成器要么在全局使用单个智能体，要么在全局使用预定义群体，忽略了不同子任务之间协作需求的差异。作者的关键洞见是：为每个功能角色在局部选择粒度，从而在一个组织内将细粒度控制与可复用协作模式结合起来。学习此类组织需要在组合构造空间中探索，而最终答案奖励提供的中间反馈有限，这构成了核心挑战。

Method: MAGIC构建混合粒度智能体图的方式是：顺序选择一个功能角色，将其实例化为单个智能体或可复用群体，并将其连接到已有单元。框架直接使用当前策略采样轨迹的回报来优化构造策略；同时采用基于势能的奖励塑形（potential-based reward shaping），利用探针式效用和结构信号提供中间反馈，同时保持累积任务奖励不变。

Result: MAGIC在八个基准上超越了当前最先进的基线方法，并在效率研究中展现出强大的推理效率。

Conclusion: 通过在局部为每个功能角色选择粒度，MAGIC实现了混合粒度的协作图生成，将细粒度控制与可复用模式统一于一个组织内。结合密集奖励强化学习与基于势能的奖励塑形，该方法有效解决了组合构造空间中中间反馈有限的问题，在性能与推理效率上均取得优异表现。

Abstract: Collaboration topology shapes both the performance and execution cost of LLM-based multi-agent systems. Because tasks differ in complexity and required capabilities, recent approaches generate task-specific collaboration graphs that specify agent participation and information flow. However, representative topology generators use either individual agents or predefined groups throughout an organization, overlooking differing collaboration needs across subtasks. Our key insight is to select granularity locally for each functional role, combining fine-grained control with reusable collaboration patterns within one organization. Learning such organizations requires exploring a combinatorial construction space with limited intermediate feedback from final-answer rewards. Therefore, we propose MAGIC, a dense-reward reinforcement learning framework for mixed-granularity graph generation. Specifically, MAGIC constructs a mixed-granularity agent graph by sequentially selecting a functional role, instantiating it as a single agent or reusable group, and connecting it to existing units. We directly optimize the construction policy using returns from trajectories sampled under the current policy and use potential-based reward shaping to provide intermediate feedback from probe-based utility and structural signals while preserving the cumulative task reward. MAGIC outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across eight benchmarks and demonstrates strong inference efficiency in our efficiency study.

</details>


### [142] [A Spectral Theory of Grokking: Weight Decay induces Feature Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26679)
*Lenz Pracher,Pascal de Jong,Oskar Lieshaus,Alan Jeffares,Steffen Rulands*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个定量理论，解释了神经网络从“懒惰学习”（lazy learning）向“丰富学习”（rich learning）转变时如何导致延迟泛化（grokking）现象，并通过模块加法任务实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在grokking现象中，模型早期拟合训练数据后，泛化性能会延迟很久才出现。这一延迟期间训练可能从固定NTK机制转向任务相关核特征方向持续演化的机制。作者希望定量解释这种从懒惰到丰富学习的转变如何产生延迟泛化。

Method: 针对使用平方损失和L2权重衰减的齐次网络，作者证明记忆后存在有限残差，且残差比例在较小NTK特征值对应的目标分量中更大。这些残差反馈到NTK自身的动力学中，将动力学投影到任务相关谱方向后得到一个约化系统，其中残差驱动的核增长与权重衰减相竞争。理论预测grokking时间尺度由学习率与权重衰减的乘积控制。作者在模块加法任务上测试这些预测。

Result: 在齐次MLP中，训练准确率饱和后，任务对齐的傅里叶结构仍在NTK中持续涌现。84×90网格的训练网络在不同学习率和权重衰减下，恢复了预测的相位几何以及泛化时间与学习率、权重衰减的逆积缩放关系。单块Transformer在42×45网格中显示出相似的宏观相位结构及相同的转变时间缩放，尽管它不严格满足齐次性。

Conclusion: 这些结果提供了一个机制性推导，将拟合后的特征学习与泛化的出现及其在学习率-权重衰减平面中的相位结构联系起来。

Abstract: In grokking an early fit to the training data separates from a much later improvement in generalization. During this delay, training can move from a fixed neural tangent kernel (NTK) regime to one in which task-relevant kernel eigendirections continue to evolve. We provide a quantitative theory for how this transition from lazy to rich learning can produce delayed generalization. For homogeneous networks trained with squared loss and $L_2$ weight decay, we show that a finite residual remains after memorization, with larger residual fractions in target components associated with smaller NTK eigenvalues. These residuals feed back into the dynamics of the NTK itself, and projecting the resulting dynamics onto task-relevant spectral directions yields a reduced system in which residual-driven kernel growth competes with weight decay. This system predicts that the grokking timescale is controlled by the product of learning rate and weight decay, that feature learning slows logarithmically near a critical decay above which task-aligned NTK structure can no longer support generalization, and that stronger decay can prevent fitting altogether. We test these predictions in modular addition. In a homogeneous MLP, task-aligned Fourier structure continues to emerge in the NTK after training accuracy has saturated, and an 84$\times$90-grid of trained networks across varying learning rate and weight decay recovers the predicted phase geometry and inverse-product scaling of the generalization time with learning rate and weight decay. A one-block Transformer shows similar macroscopic phase structure in a 42$\times$45-grid, as well as the same transition-time scaling despite violating exact homogeneity. Together, these results provide a mechanistic derivation connecting post-fit feature learning to both the onset of generalization and its phase structure in the learning rate and weight decay plane.

</details>


### [143] [Train Where the Quantized Model Goes: On-Policy Distillation for Low-Bit Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26708)
*Yuanteng Chen,Zhilei Liu,Peisong Wang,Yuantian Shao,Chuangyi Li,Weining Wang,Shuang Qiu,Gang Li,Jing Liu,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对子3比特量化后数学与代码推理严重退化的问题，提出在量化感知蒸馏（QAD）之后加入在线策略蒸馏（OPD）阶段，让量化模型在自己的自回归轨迹上接受全精度教师监督，从而恢复长文本推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAD虽能恢复子3比特量化下的短问答性能，但数学与代码推理仍严重受损，长生成易退化为重复循环并耗尽解码预算。作者将其归因于量化放大的暴露偏差：QAD在固定语料前缀上训练，而量化引入的偏差会沿模型自身的自回归轨迹不断累积。

Method: 提出在线策略蒸馏（OPD）阶段。从QAD检查点出发，学生模型通过部署时使用的量化前向路径生成，并在自身前缀上接受冻结的全精度教师反馈，结合稠密的词元级指导与任务验证器奖励。

Result: 在四个模型、2.79与1.88有效比特设置下，OPD将MATH-500上的平均BF16性能保持率从35%提升至70%，HumanEval从66%提升至91%，同时保持短问答性能；在等预算比较中，推理增益显著超过继续使用教师强制式QAD。

Conclusion: 通过将QAD稳定的低比特初始化与OPD的在线策略推理恢复相结合，该框架提供了一个全面的子3比特方案，在保留广泛能力的同时恢复长文本推理。

Abstract: Quantization-aware distillation (QAD) restores much of the short-form question-answering performance lost to sub-3-bit quantization, yet leaves mathematical and code reasoning substantially impaired. Long generations often degenerate into repetitive loops, exhausting the decoding budget without completing a solution. We trace this gap to quantization-amplified exposure bias: QAD trains on fixed corpus prefixes, while quantization-induced deviations compound along the model's own autoregressive trajectories. To address this mismatch, we introduce an on-policy distillation (OPD) stage that places teacher supervision where the quantized model actually goes. Starting from a QAD checkpoint, the student generates through the quantized forward path used at deployment and receives feedback from a frozen full-precision teacher on its own prefixes, combining dense token-level guidance with task-verifier rewards. Across four models at 2.79 and 1.88 effective bits, OPD raises average BF16 performance retention from 35% to 70% on MATH-500 and from 66% to 91% on HumanEval while preserving short-form performance, with reasoning gains substantially exceeding those of continued teacher-forced QAD in matched-budget comparisons. By coupling QAD's stable low-bit initialization with OPD's on-policy reasoning recovery, our framework provides a comprehensive sub-3-bit solution that preserves broad capabilities while restoring long-form reasoning.

</details>


### [144] [The Sirens' Song: When Proximal Background Context Overshadows Distant Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26718)
*Xiaoyu Yang,Jie Lu,Wei Duan,En Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"邻近陷阱"（Proximity Trap）概念，指长上下文LLM对远端证据关注不足的主因是大量任务无关的近端背景累积干扰，而非单纯距离。为此提出LYRA机制（t分布方向匹配）重塑上下文检索分布，在多基准上取得稳定提升，并发布ProxBench评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文LLM研究主要聚焦于"克服距离"本身，但作者认为远端证据被忽视的真正原因更多来自近端大量任务无关背景的累积竞争，即"邻近陷阱"，这一现象此前未被充分识别与研究。

Method: 提出LYRA（Long-context heavY-tailed Relevance Alignment），一种基于t分布的方向匹配机制，通过重塑上下文检索分布，将更多注意力质量分配给任务相关证据，同时保留模型编码的相对位置信息。

Result: 在LongBench-v2、RULER与LongBench上的大量实验表明，该方法在不同上下文长度和任务类别上均取得一致性提升；同时提出ProxBench，一个多层级细粒度基准，用于评估在近端背景干扰不断增强时的远端证据利用能力。

Conclusion: 远端证据利用不足的核心症结在于近端背景干扰的累积竞争，而非距离本身；LYRA通过重分布注意力有效缓解该问题，ProxBench为相关研究提供了更细粒度的评测工具。

Abstract: Long-context LLMs focus on retrieving distant evidence from extensive context, yet existing work has largely focused on overcoming distance alone. In this work, we identify the Proximity Trap, insufficient attention to distant evidence often arises less from distance itself than from cumulative competition with abundant, task-irrelevant proximal background. To address the Proximity Trap, we introduce LYRA (Long-context heavY-tailed Relevance Alignment), a t-distributed directional matching mechanism that reshapes the context retrieval distribution, directing more attention mass toward task-relevant evidence, while preserving the relative positional information encoded. Extensive experiments on LongBench-v2, RULER, and LongBench demonstrate consistent improvements across context lengths and task categories. We further introduce ProxBench, a multi-level fine-grained benchmark for evaluating distant evidence utilization under increasing proximal background interference. Project page: https://xiaoyuyoung.github.io/LYRA/

</details>


### [145] [EquivSVA: A Formally Verified Dataset of Behavioral Assertions Across Equivalent RTL Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26751)
*FNU Aditi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EquivSVA，一个基于"行为族"组织的形式化验证数据集，用于研究LLM生成的SystemVerilog断言是否捕获外部可观察行为而非某一RTL实现的偶然细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集多支持大规模训练、形式化评估、规约到断言生成和变异测试等目标，但缺少对"生成的断言是否捕捉外部可观察行为、是否依赖某一RTL实现的偶然细节"这一问题的研究，即断言生成的鲁棒性缺乏受控研究手段。

Method: 以行为族（behavior family）为核心组织数据：每个族包含同一外部行为的4个结构不同的RTL实现、共享的接口级gold属性、3个受控变异体及形式验证证据。数据集含12类共120个行为族、480个参考RTL实现、914个gold属性、360个变异体；每个族需通过固定的17项验证套件（覆盖RTL等价、gold属性证明、属性可达性、变异体可区分性及变异体上的gold属性检查），并提供族安全（family-safe）的训练/开发/测试划分。最后用Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct在留出测试集上做案例分析。

Result: 在留出测试集上，293个仅基于接口生成的性质中有93个形式化可靠（sound）；在24个测试族中，有14个族的可靠属性数量在不同等价实现间存在变化，说明生成鲁棒性随实现结构而波动。

Conclusion: 行为族组织方式能够在不改变预期功能的前提下，支持对断言生成鲁棒性的受控研究；数据集、生成器、验证脚本与案例工件已公开。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to generate SystemVerilog Assertions from natural-language specifica- tions and register-transfer-level designs. Existing datasets and benchmarks support important goals such as large- scale training, formal evaluation, specification-to-assertion generation, and mutation-based testing. A complemen- tary need is to study whether a generated assertion cap- tures externally observable behavior or depends on inci- dental details of one RTL implementation. We present EquivSVA, a formally verified dataset organized around behavior families. Each family contains four structurally distinct RTL implementations of the same externally ob- servable behavior, shared interface-level gold properties, three controlled mutants, and formal-validation evidence. EquivSVA contains 120 behavior families across 12 cat- egories, 480 reference RTL implementations, 914 gold properties, and 360 mutants. Every final family passes a fixed 17-job validation suite covering RTL equivalence, gold-property proofs, property reachability, mutant dis- tinguishability, and gold-property checks on mutants. We also provide fixed family-safe train, development, and test splits. As a small demonstration of the analyses en- abled by the dataset, we evaluate the publicly released, Apache-2.0-licensed Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct model on the held-out test split. Of 293 interface-only generated properties, 93 are formally sound, and the number of sound properties varies across equivalent implementations for 14 of 24 test families. These results illustrate how behavior-family organization can support controlled stud- ies of assertion-generation robustness without requiring changes in intended functionality. The dataset, generators, validation scripts, and case-study artifacts are publicly released at https://github.com/aditigupta96/EquivSVA.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [TRACTOR Benchmark for Evaluating C to Rust Translators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25121)
*Hamed Okhravi,Brandt Ogden,Noah Luther,Ian McQuoid,Howard Mak,Nathan Burow*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 DARPA TRACTOR 项目的标准化 C-to-Rust 翻译基准，包含递进式测试集、里程碑项目及评估基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C/C++ 等内存不安全语言编写的关键软件长期面临内存安全漏洞风险。语言、程序分析与 AI 的进步使得通过自动翻译迁移到 Rust 成为可能，但缺乏可系统评估翻译工具的标准基准。

Method: MIT Lincoln Laboratory 作为 TRACTOR 项目的独立测试与评估机构，设计了标准化基准，包含难度递进的测试测试电池（test batteries）与更大规模的里程碑项目，并配套评估基础设施及针对正确性、安全性、地道性（idiomaticity）和性能的度量指标。

Result: 形成了可系统评估 C-to-Rust 翻译工具的标准基准及评估基础设施，并已公开发布。

Conclusion: 该基准与评估基础设施公开可用，支持 C-to-Rust 翻译技术的广泛开发与评估。

Abstract: Memory-safety vulnerabilities remain a persistent source of security risk in critical software, much of which is implemented in memory-unsafe languages such as C and C++. Recent advances in programming languages, program analysis, and artificial intelligence have created new opportunities to modernize these legacy systems through automated translation to memory-safe languages such as Rust. The DARPA Translating All C to Rust (TRACTOR) program seeks to develop scalable techniques for translating large C codebases into safe, performant, and maintainable Rust. MIT Lincoln Laboratory serves as the program's independent test and evaluation organization and has developed a standardized benchmark for systematically assessing C-to-Rust translation tools. This report describes the TRACTOR benchmark, including progressively challenging test batteries and larger milestone projects, as well as the supporting evaluation infrastructure and metrics for assessing correctness, safety, idiomaticity, and performance. The benchmark and associated evaluation infrastructure are publicly available to support the broader development and evaluation of C-to-Rust translation technologies.

</details>


### [147] [TAILOR: Template-Preserving Augmentation for Long-Tailed Log Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25261)
*Sepideh Hodaeian,Zhenhao Li,An Ran Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文研究日志解析中长期尾稀有日志组导致的评估偏差问题，并提出TAILOR框架，通过模板保持的增强策略提升稀有日志组的解析准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实日志数据集呈现高度不平衡的长尾分布，少数频繁模板占主导，而大量稀有模板仅出现几次。这种不平衡使基准指标被频繁模板主导，评估结果过于乐观，稀有但运维重要事件的解析性能被掩盖，问题严重性未被充分暴露。

Method: 作者首先定义稀有日志组（实例少于5个），并在Loghub-2.0基准上进行实证研究。随后提出TAILOR框架：在模板推断之前，对稀有日志组进行模板保持的日志消息增强，为其补充模板一致的日志消息，从而提供更多结构证据以区分静态token与动态变量。

Result: 实证研究发现稀有日志组占全部模板近20%，但仅占日志消息不到0.01%；所有被测解析器在这些组上性能显著下降。TAILOR在稀有日志组上的解析准确率相较最强基线提升19%，同时在完整数据集上保持有竞争力的性能；该增强策略可跨不同LLM骨干泛化，并在不改变核心架构的情况下持续改进现有LLM解析器。

Conclusion: 稀有日志组是日志解析评估中被忽视的重要问题，其长尾特性导致现有解析器性能严重下降。TAILOR通过模板保持增强有效缓解该问题，且方法具有良好的通用性，可即插即用地提升现有LLM解析器。

Abstract: Log parsing is essential for system log analysis because it supports tasks such as debugging, monitoring, and anomaly detection by transforming unstructured log messages into structured log templates. However, real-world log datasets exhibit highly imbalanced, long-tailed distributions, where a small number of frequent templates dominate while many rare templates appear only a few times. This imbalance causes evaluation results to be overly optimistic by allowing frequent templates to dominate benchmark metrics, while poor performance on rare yet operationally important events remains largely hidden. In this paper, we investigate the prevalence and impact of rare log groups, defined as log groups with fewer than five instances. Our empirical study on the widely used Loghub-2.0 benchmark shows that rare log groups account for nearly 20% of all templates but less than 0.01% of log messages. Because they contain only a handful of instances, all evaluated parsers experience substantial performance degradation on these groups. To address this challenge, we propose TAILOR, a log parsing framework that improves template inference for rare log groups through template-preserving augmentation. TAILOR enriches rare log groups with template- consistent log messages before template inference. The additional structural evidence helps distinguish static tokens from dynamic variables. Our experimental results show that TAILOR improves parsing accuracy on rare log groups by 19% over the strongest baseline while maintaining competitive performance on complete datasets. We further show that the proposed augmentation strategy generalizes across different LLM backbones and consistently improves existing LLM-based parsers without modifying their core architectures.

</details>


### [148] [The Vocabulary of Flaky Tests in Swift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25516)
*João Medeiros,Denini Silva,Breno Miranda*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次在 Swift 生态中评估基于词汇的机器学习 flaky 测试预测，随机森林在 91 个 flaky 与 22,349 个稳定测试上达到 F1=0.86、AUC=0.95，并揭示了 flakiness 与稳定性两类互补的词汇信号及其边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flaky 测试在不改代码的情况下产生非确定性结果，会削弱 CI 信心并延误交付。基于词汇的机器学习预测已在 Java 和 JavaScript 上被证明有效，但尚无研究评估其对 Swift 的适用性——而 Swift 的测试风格以 UI 和异步代码为主，词汇特征是否依然有效尚属未知，因此需要专门的实证研究。

Method: 从 15 个开源 Swift 项目中，通过重复执行（re-execution）与提交历史挖掘（commit-history mining）收集 91 个 flaky 测试与 22,349 个稳定测试。以 TF-IDF 一元+二元词（unigram+bigram）为特征，在分层 5 折交叉验证下训练五种分类器：随机森林、决策树、朴素贝叶斯、SVM 和 KNN。此外设置平凡基线与词汇阈值规则作为对照，并用信息增益分析识别关键信号词。

Result: 随机森林表现最佳（Precision = 0.92，F1 = 0.86，AUC = 0.95），显著优于所有平凡基线，包括对最具信息量词元施加的词汇阈值规则，确认存在真实的判别信号（MCC = 0.75，而最佳基线仅 0.08）。信息增益分析揭示两类互补信号：flakiness 标记主要出现在不稳定测试中，包括并发原语（async、await）、基于期望的同步（expectation、fulfill）、错误传播（throws）和显式时序依赖（timeout、wait、now）；稳定性标记主要是纯同步测试的断言词汇（xctassertequal），作为反对 flakiness 的证据。

Conclusion: 该研究将基于词汇的 flaky 检测扩展到 Swift 生态，并刻画了其有效性与边界。误差分析表明模型在两类情况下失败：flakiness 隐藏在测试体之外的共享基础设施中，或异步构造被用于确定性上下文，这暴露了纯词汇预测的固有局限。

Abstract: Flaky tests produce non-deterministic outcomes without code change, eroding CI confidence and delaying deliveries. While vocabulary-based machine learning prediction has proven effective for Java and JavaScript, no study has evaluated it for Swift, a language whose testing style is dominated by UI and asynchronous code. We collect 91 flaky and 22,349 stable tests from 15 open-source Swift projects via re-execution and commit-history mining, then train five classifiers (Random Forest, Decision Tree, Naive Bayes, SVM, KNN) on TF-IDF unigram+bigram features under stratified 5-fold cross-validation. Random Forest achieves the best performance (Precision = 0.92, F1 = 0.86, AUC = 0.95) and substantially outperforms trivial baselines, among them a vocabulary-threshold rule applied to the most informative tokens, confirming a genuine discriminative signal (MCC = 0.75 vs. 0.08 for the best baseline). Information-gain analysis reveals two complementary signal types. Flakiness markers appear predominantly in unstable tests and comprise concurrency primitives (async, await), expectation-based synchronisation (expectation, fulfill), error propagation (throws), and explicit timing dependence (timeout, wait, now). Stability markers, chiefly the assertion vocabulary of plainly synchronous tests (xctassertequal), count as evidence against flakiness. Error analysis shows that the model fails when flakiness is hidden in shared infrastructure outside the test body or when async constructs are used in a deterministic context, exposing the intrinsic limit of lexical prediction. These results extend vocabulary-based flakiness detection to the Swift ecosystem and characterise both its effectiveness and its boundaries.

</details>


### [149] [Dynamic Conformance Testing of WebGPU Through Specification-Driven Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25520)
*Mahya Samdaliri,Zhihao Yao,Kasthuri Jayarajah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 LANTERN，一个规范引导的动态一致性测试框架，通过对 WebGPU CTS 测试进行语法与语义变异，在 Chromium 上大规模运行并发现浏览器漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 官方 WebGPU 一致性测试套件（CTS）仅关注规范内的良构用法，无法覆盖语义边界情况与对抗性输入；而通用模糊测试器又因 WebGPU 复杂的图形栈与多进程架构而难以奏效。因此需要一种既能利用规范信息、又能主动探索异常情形的测试方法。

Method: LANTERN 从 WebIDL 定义中提取显式语法 API 规则，并从自然语言规范文本中恢复语义约束（如命令顺序、对象生命周期）。基于选定的规则，在 AST 定位下对 CTS 测试进行文本变换，生成既有效又故意无效的测试变体，并在带 AddressSanitizer 插桩的 Chromium 上大规模执行以发现缺陷。

Result: 在评估中发现了三个可复现的漏洞，其中包括一个堆破坏（heap corruption）问题，均存在于研究时点的 Chromium 版本中。

Conclusion: 结果表明，以语法为种子、具备语义感知的一致性测试变异方法，是在 WebGPU 测试中发现浏览器漏洞的有效途径。

Abstract: WebGPU is a low-level graphics and compute API that exposes modern GPU functionality to web applications. While the official WebGPU Conformance Test Suite (CTS) focuses on well-formed usage under the WebGPU specification, it is not designed to stress implementations with semantic edge cases or adversarial inputs. General-purpose fuzzers, in contrast, struggle with WebGPU because of its complex graphics stack and multi-process architecture. We introduce LANTERN, a specification-guided dynamic conformance testing framework that mutates CTS tests using constraints extracted from the WebGPU specification. LANTERN extracts explicit syntactic API rules from WebIDL definitions and recovers semantic constraints, such as command ordering and object lifetimes, from natural-language specification text. Selected rules guide AST-located textual transformations that generate both valid and intentionally invalid CTS variants. We execute the resulting tests at scale on AddressSanitizer-instrumented Chromium to discover bugs. Our evaluation discovers three reproducible bugs, including a heap corruption, in Chromium versions current at the time of study. These results demonstrate that syntactically seeded, semantics-aware mutation of conformance tests provides a way to uncover browser bugs during WebGPU testing.

</details>


### [150] [Evaluating Shaker for Flaky Test Detection in Python Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25528)
*Gabriela Leal,Denini Silva,Leopoldo Teixeira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次在 Python 上对 Shaker 进行实证评估：在与普通重执行（ReRun）预算匹配的配对设计中，Shaker 未展现统计学显著的检测优势（37.2% vs 35.8%，p=0.84），原因在于多数基准脆弱测试无法复现，且可复现者的脆弱性主要来自网络与随机性而非 Shaker 针对的并发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shaker 通过注入资源竞争（CPU、内存、I/O 压力）放大并发引起的非确定性，被报告在 Java/Android 基准上检出 95% 的脆弱测试，远高于普通重执行的 37.5%。但该工具尚未在 Python 上得到评估，其跨语言有效性未知，因此需要首次开展此类实证检验。

Method: 从 Gruber 等的真值数据集中选取非顺序依赖的脆弱测试，采用配对设计将 Shaker 与预算匹配的 ReRun 基线进行比较，给两者相同数量的执行次数：137 个测试各运行 100 次，并使用 McNemar 精确检验进行统计比较。

Result: 按 Java/Android 的配置，Shaker 相对普通重执行无统计学显著优势（37.2% vs 35.8%；McNemar 精确 p = 0.84）。原因有二：其一，不到一半的真值脆弱测试在独立硬件上能以任一技术复现为脆弱，且多数未复现的测试在 100 次运行中从未出现分歧；其二，可复现的测试主要由网络交互和随机性导致的脆弱性主导，而非 Shaker 所针对的并发。

Conclusion: Shaker 的并发压力注入策略在 Python 上未能复现其在 Java/Android 上的检出优势。研究进一步揭示了领域层面的隐患：跨执行环境复用脆弱测试真值集，会把真实的脆弱测试悄悄变成表观真阴性，从而压低任何工具测得的召回率。

Abstract: Flaky tests pass or fail non-deterministically on unchanged code, eroding trust in test suites and inflating the cost of every failure. Shaker detects them by injecting resource contention (CPU, memory, and I/O stress) to amplify non-determinism caused by concurrent execution, and was reported to detect 95% of the flaky tests in a Java and Android benchmark against 37.5% for plain re-execution (ReRun). We present the first empirical evaluation of Shaker for Python. Drawing non-order-dependent flaky tests from the ground-truth dataset of Gruber et al., we compare Shaker against a budget-matched ReRun baseline in a paired design, giving both techniques the same number of test executions: Each of 137 tests is run 100 times under each. As configured for Java and Android, Shaker provides no statistically significant detection advantage over plain re-execution (37.2% vs. 35.8%; McNemar exact p = 0.84). Two findings explain why. First, fewer than half of the ground-truth flaky tests reproduce as flaky at all on independent hardware under either technique, and most of the tests that fail to reproduce never diverge once across 100 runs. Second, the tests that do reproduce are dominated by flakiness from network interactions and randomness rather than the concurrency Shaker targets. Beyond the tool, this exposes a broader hazard for the field: reusing a flaky-test ground truth across execution environments silently converts genuine flaky tests into apparent true negatives, deflating any tool's measured recall.

</details>


### [151] [An Empirical Analysis of Cross-OS Portability Issues in Python Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25531)
*Denini Silva,MohamadAli Farahat,Marcelo d'Amorim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首个针对Python跨操作系统可移植性问题的大规模实证研究，通过跨OS测试复现与GitHub issue分析，构建了失效分类体系并评估了静态工具与大语言模型的检测/修复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python虽号称跨平台，但实际部署中常出现跨操作系统可移植性失效。此前缺乏对Python跨OS可移植性问题的系统性、大规模实证认识。

Method: 分析2,042个开源仓库，采用两种互补方法：(1)对500个项目进行系统性跨OS测试复现；(2)人工分析240个GitHub issue。据此构建包含7个主类别、24个子类、15种诊断特征与4种修复模式的分类体系，并评估静态分析工具与大模型的表现。

Result: 跨平台测试中11.2%的项目出现与OS相关的测试失败；确认102个真实可移植性问题，涉及另外95个项目。文件/目录操作、进程管理和库依赖最常见。静态分析工具支持极少；大模型识别准确率40-79%，在结构化指导下修复成功率达50-77%。提交33个PR（17个被合并，0个被拒）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了理解与解决Python跨OS可移植性问题的首个综合基线，为开发者、工具设计者及研究社区提供了可操作的洞见。

Abstract: While Python is designed as a cross-platform language, real-world applications encounter portability failures when deployed across different operating systems. We present the first large-scale empirical study of cross-OS portability issues in Python, analyzing 2,042 open-source repositories using two complementary approaches: systematic cross-OS test reexecution and manual analysis of GitHub issues. Our cross-platform testing of 500 projects reveals that 11.2% exhibit OS-dependent test failures. Through systematic analysis of 240 GitHub issues, we confirm 102 genuine portability problems spanning 95 additional projects. We develop a comprehensive taxonomy identifying 7 primary failure categories - with file/directory operations, process management, and library dependencies being most prevalent - along with 24 distinct sub-categories, 15 diagnostic signatures, and 4 systematic repair patterns. Our evaluation reveals that existing static analysis tools provide minimal support for portability detection, while large language models achieve 40-79% accuracy in identifying issues and 50-77% success in generating fixes when provided with structured guidance. Through 33 contributed pull requests, we demonstrate practical applicability and developer acceptance (17 merged, zero rejected) of our findings. This work establishes the first comprehensive baseline for understanding and addressing cross-OS portability issues in Python, providing actionable insights for developers, tool designers, and the broader research community.

</details>


### [152] [Understanding Maintenance and Support in a Community-Driven Scientific Workflow Ecosystem: A Cross-Space Study of Galaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25587)
*Khairul Alam,Kowsik Roy,Md Shamimur Rahman,Banani Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对Galaxy科学工作流系统开展大规模实证研究，分析11,762个GitHub issue、52,203个PR与6,235个论坛讨论，刻画其维护与支持图景及跨空间关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Galaxy作为社区驱动的科学工作流系统，其可持续性依赖软件、工具、工作流、基础设施、文档与用户支持生态的持续维护。但维护知识分散在开发与社区支持空间中，难以理解"维护什么"、"维护产物如何被解决"以及用户关切与仓库级开发的联系。

Method: 使用BERTopic建模对三类维护产物进行主题识别；刻画维护与支持关切；分析协调、诊断、贡献者、自动化、参与度等特征与解决结果及解决时间的关系；并考察开发空间与支持空间之间显式与潜在的关联（可追溯性）。

Result: 识别出9个issue主题、14个PR主题、14个论坛主题，维护图景涵盖工作流执行、数据管理、工具与依赖、基础设施、测试、科学资源、文档与用户支持。协调、诊断、贡献者、自动化与参与度特征与解决与否及解决速度呈现不同关联。跨空间显式可追溯性有限：16,426条已解决的显式关系中97.77%发生在GitHub内部，仅294条连接GitHub产物与社区论坛讨论，尽管存在额外的语义与技术相关性。

Conclusion: Galaxy的维护是一种分布式生态系统级过程；研究指出改进诊断报告、生命周期感知的分诊、跨空间可追溯性以及社区支持知识复用的机会。

Abstract: Galaxy is a widely used, community-driven scientific workflow system whose sustainability depends on continuous maintenance across its software, tools, workflows, infrastructure, documentation, and user-support ecosystem. However, maintenance knowledge in Galaxy is distributed across development and community-support spaces, making it difficult to understand what is maintained, how maintenance artifacts are resolved, and how user-facing concerns connect to repository-level development. We conduct a large-scale empirical study of Galaxy using 11,762 GitHub issues, 52,203 pull requests, and 6,235 Community Forum discussions. We characterize maintenance and support concerns, examine factors associated with resolution outcomes and resolution time, and investigate explicit and candidate connections among maintenance artifacts across these spaces.
  Using BERTopic modeling, we identify nine issue topics, 14 pull-request topics, and 14 forum topics, revealing a maintenance landscape spanning workflow execution, data management, tools and dependencies, infrastructure, testing, scientific resources, documentation, and user support. Resolution analyses show that coordination, diagnostic, contributor, automation, and engagement characteristics exhibit different associations with whether artifacts are resolved and how quickly resolution occurs. We further find limited explicit traceability between development and support spaces: 97.77\% of 16,426 resolved explicit relationships occur within GitHub, while only 294 connect GitHub artifacts with Community Forum discussions, despite additional semantic and technical relatedness across these spaces. Together, these findings characterize Galaxy maintenance as a distributed ecosystem-level process and identify opportunities to improve diagnostic reporting, lifecycle-aware triage, cross-space traceability, and the reuse of community-support knowledge.

</details>


### [153] [What Was Once Learned May Need to Be Unlearned: Machine Unlearning for Deprecated API Knowledge in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25786)
*Jin Liu,Yanzhong He,Guancheng Lin,Xiao Liu,Jacky Wai Keung,Xiao Yu,Xiaoxue Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究针对代码补全模型中过时API知识的机器遗忘，构建了行为驱动的基准MUDAPIBench，并发现梯度差分（GD）方法在遗忘过时API与保留其他能力之间取得最佳权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM因预训练语料包含历史版本库代码而生成已弃用API。现有推理时干预、模型编辑或机器遗忘方法因多种合理补全而受限，且少有研究验证模型是否真的表现出目标弃用行为，也缺乏对其他API意外变化的评估。

Method: 开展机器遗忘过时API知识的系统性实证研究，构建MUDAPIBench——一个行为驱动基准，包含来自8个Python库145个弃用到最新API映射的7000多个模型特定实例，仅保留原模型确实生成目标弃用API的实例。在3个代码LLM上评估8种代表性遗忘方法，考察弃用API遗忘、最新API生成、其他及无关API行为保留、通用代码生成能力与效率。

Result: 梯度差分（GD）提供最佳整体权衡，在抑制弃用API的同时保留其他能力，计算成本适中。分析显示不同库间差异显著，模型训练数据截止后才弃用的API更难遗忘；逐层分析表明GD以相对受控的内部变化实现有效遗忘。

Conclusion: 行为驱动的基准与多维评估揭示了机器遗忘过时API的可行性与挑战，GD是综合表现最佳的方法，未来需关注库间差异与训练截止时间的影响。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) for code completion may generate deprecated APIs because their pre-training corpora contain code from historical library versions. Existing approaches use inference-time intervention, model editing, or machine unlearning, but multiple plausible completions make predefined replacements restrictive. Moreover, existing studies rarely verify whether models exhibit the targeted deprecated behavior or evaluate unintended changes to other APIs.
  We conduct a systematic empirical study of machine unlearning for deprecated API knowledge and construct MUDAPIBench, a behavior-grounded benchmark with over 7,000 model-specific instances derived from 145 deprecated-to-up-to-date API mappings across eight Python libraries. Instances are retained only when the original model generates the target deprecated API. We evaluate eight representative unlearning methods across three code LLMs on deprecated API forgetting, up-to-date API generation, preservation of other and unrelated API behaviors, general code-generation capability, and efficiency.
  Results show that Gradient Difference (GD) provides the best overall trade-off, suppressing deprecated APIs while preserving other capabilities with moderate computational costs. Further analyses reveal substantial variation across libraries and show that APIs deprecated after the model's training-data cutoff are harder to forget. Layer-wise analyses indicate that GD achieves effective forgetting with comparatively controlled internal changes.

</details>


### [154] [Confidence-Guided Cross-Modal Knowledge Transfer for Multimodal Anomaly Detection in Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25856)
*Peipeng Wang,Xiuguo Zhang,Lihua Zhang,Zhiying Cao,Jingchun Zhou,Zheng Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 CMT-AD，一种置信度引导的跨模态知识迁移多模态异常检测方法，在统一深度聚类框架下融合指标与日志，通过置信度评估动态可靠性并利用门控中间模态缓解跨模态异质性，性能超过现有方法且F1高于0.9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统的可靠安全运行需要准确的异常检测。现有研究从单模态转向多模态交互与融合，但如何有效利用可靠的跨模态信息仍具挑战：一是各模态受负载波动等影响导致可靠性动态变化；二是多模态数据在结构和语义上存在异质性。

Method: CMT-AD 在统一深度聚类框架中联合建模指标与日志；通过软聚类分布评估模态可靠性，将聚类不确定性量化为置信度分数；在置信度引导下分析各模态在跨模态交互中的贡献，并用高置信模态补充低置信模态；引入门控中间模态，设计结构与语义一致性约束，使原始模态与中间模态对齐以保留相似性结构和语义分布；同时加入模态内与跨模态正则项以增强聚类紧凑性并缓解负迁移。

Result: 在三个大规模数据集上评估，CMT-AD 优于最先进方法，F1 分数高于 0.9。

Conclusion: CMT-AD 通过置信度引导的跨模态知识迁移及结构语义一致性约束，有效应对模态可靠性动态变化与跨模态异质性，实现了高精度的微服务系统多模态异常检测。

Abstract: Accurate anomaly detection is essential for reliable and secure operations of microservice systems. While an increasing number of studies have shifted from unimodal modeling to multimodal interaction and fusion, effectively leveraging reliable cross-modal information remains challenging. The challenge primarily stems from two aspects. Firstly, different modalities are influenced by factors like load fluctuations, leading to dynamically changing reliability. Secondly, multimodal data exhibit heterogeneity in both structure and semantics. Therefore, we propose a confidence-guided Cross-Modal knowledge Transfer method for multimodal Anomaly Detection (CMT-AD). It jointly models metrics and logs within a unified deep clustering framework and estimates modality reliability through the soft clustering distributions, where clustering uncertainty is quantified into confidence scores. Guided by these confidence scores, the model actively analyzes the contributions of each modality in cross-modal interactions and supplements low-confidence modalities with knowledge from high-confidence ones. To further mitigate cross-modal heterogeneity, we introduce a gated intermediate modality and design structural and semantic consistency constraints that align the original modalities with the intermediate modality to preserve similarity structures and semantic distributions across modalities. Furthermore, intra-modal and cross-modal regularization terms are incorporated to enhance cluster compactness and mitigate negative transfer. We evaluated CMT-AD on three large-scale datasets, and the results demonstrate that CMT-AD outperforms state-of-the-art approaches and achieves an F1-score higher than 0.9.

</details>


### [155] [When Should Dependency Updates Invoke Repair Agents? A Lightweight Routing Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25911)
*Liheng Fan,Jialun Yin,Yuzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 DepFixRouter：一个轻量路由器，在依赖更新 PR 进入昂贵的仓库级修复代理之前，基于创建时信号预测是否需要兼容性修复，实现预算感知的升级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖更新 PR 大多例行，但少数需要非平凡的兼容性修复。让仓库级编码代理处理每个更新会浪费模型调用、CI 时间、仓库上下文和审查精力，因此需要在诊断或修复前判断哪些 PR 应被升级。

Method: 将问题建模为“代理前路由”。使用 497 个带标注的 GitHub 依赖更新候选，仅用创建时的文本与元数据信号（PR 标题、bot/依赖标记）训练 LinearSVC 路由器；并额外评估回顾性全历史信号；最后进行 60 例诊断代理试点。

Result: 497 个候选中仅 72 个需要实质修复。创建时安全的 LinearSVC 达到 0.488 修复 F1，在前 20% 路由 PR 中捕获 51.4% 的修复，每次捕获修复的调用数从全路由/随机的 6.90 降至 2.68。回顾性信号将前 20% 召回提升至 65.3%，提示 PR 历史存在事后偏差。诊断代理试点中路由门控使 LLM 调用减少 66.7%、token 减少 66.1%。

Conclusion: DepFixRouter 可充当例行依赖更新自动化与昂贵仓库级代理之间的轻量升级层，实现预算感知维护，且不依赖回顾性修复证据进行部署时路由。

Abstract: Dependency-update pull requests are frequent and mostly routine, but a small subset requires non-trivial compatibility repair. Recent repository-level coding agents make such repair increasingly plausible, yet invoking them on every dependency update wastes model calls, CI time, repository context, and review attention. We frame this as a pre-agent routing problem: deciding which dependency-update pull requests should be escalated before downstream diagnosis or repair attempts. We introduce DepFixRouter, a lightweight router that ranks dependency updates by historical compatibility-repair likelihood using creation-time textual and metadata signals. On 497 labeled GitHub dependency-update candidates, only 72 require substantive repair. A creation-time-safe LinearSVC using only PR titles and bot/dependency flags reaches 0.488 repair F1 and captures 51.4% of repairs within the top 20% routed pull requests, improving calls per captured repair from 6.90 under route-all or random policies to 2.68. Retrospective full-history signals improve top-20% recall to 65.3%, revealing substantial hindsight leakage in pull-request histories rather than deployment-time routing utility. In a 60-case diagnosis-agent pilot, router-gated diagnosis reduces actual LLM calls by 66.7% and tokens by 66.1%, suggesting budgetaware escalation while measuring diagnosis rather than patch generation. DepFixRouter can serve as a lightweight escalation layer between routine dependency-update automation and expensive repository-level agents, enabling budget-aware maintenance without relying on retrospective repair evidence for deployment-time routing.

</details>


### [156] [Compiling Sufficient Governance Context from Declared Losses and Reachable States: Exact Observation-Contract Synthesis with Cardinality and Cost Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26016)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出并验证了"最小充分治理上下文"这一对象：在有限可达状态模型、确定性声明裁决与候选可观测属性下，计算充分观测集、区分必备属性与联合充分契约，并在基数或成本目标下选择契约，通过穷举或SAT/MaxSAT编码合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望形式化地刻画"需要观测哪些属性才能确定裁决"这一问题：既要识别哪些属性单独不可缺少，也要识别哪些属性集合联合起来才充分，并在给定目标下选出最优契约。动机在于治理/编译器场景下需要对充分性与最优化给出可计算、可验证的方案。

Method: 定义观测契约（属性集合使所有可达状态的声明裁决被确定）与权威契约（在目标与约束下被选中并绑定到门模式）。对可承受穷举的情形合成所有包含极小的充分契约；否则用SAT/MaxSAT编码合成最小基数或最小成本契约，并直接检验充分性。在构造的代码/云领域与另一更大构造领域上评估，并设计AuthorityBench对比四种基线。

Result: 在第一个构造域中，单独必备核心本身不构成充分观测契约，且存在两个不同的约简；预注册的成本模型能精确区分二者。在第二个更大的构造域中同一模式重现，但该域的成本模型无法区分替代方案，出现完全可解释的成本并列（如实报告）。可辨识族规模随规模增长，穷举在注册超时内被确认不可行，而SAT/MaxSAT合成在远低于一秒内求解；MaxSAT相较普通SAT未测得基数优势。AuthorityBench中仅声明基线在三个域上都不精确充分。

Conclusion: 每个被选契约都做了充分性检验：失败时给出反例，成功时给出检验摘要而非可移植证书，体现两范围表中以编译器为主的范围；独立指定的端到端案例研究被登记为后续工作，本文不作主张。

Abstract: We call the object this paper derives and certifies a minimal sufficient governance context: given a finite reachable-state model, a deterministic declared verdict, and candidate observable attributes, we compute sufficient observation sets, distinguish attributes that are individually indispensable from contracts that are jointly sufficient, and select among sufficient contracts under a cardinality or declared-cost objective. An observation contract is a set of candidate attributes whose values determine the declared verdict on every reachable state; an authority contract is one selected under an objective and bound to a gate schema. We synthesize every inclusion-minimal sufficient contract where exhaustive enumeration is affordable, and a minimum-cardinality or minimum-cost contract by SAT/MaxSAT encoding otherwise, checking sufficiency directly. On a constructed code/cloud domain, the individually-indispensable core is not sufficient as an observation contract and two distinct reducts exist; a preregistered cost model separates them exactly. On a second, larger, constructed domain, the same pattern recurs, but that domain's cost model does not separate the alternatives: a fully explained cost tie, reported as found. We measure discernibility-family scaling where exhaustive enumeration is confirmed infeasible within a registered timeout, while SAT/MaxSAT synthesis solves in well under a second; MaxSAT showed no measured cardinality advantage over plain SAT. AuthorityBench compares four baselines across three domains; the declared-only baseline is not exactly sufficient on any. Every selected contract is checked for sufficiency, with a counterexample on failure and a check summary, not a portable certificate, on success -- the compiler-focused scope of a two-scope table; an independently specified end-to-end case study is registered follow-up work, not claimed here.

</details>


### [157] [On Behavioral Alignment of Model-Code and Human-Code Understandability via Behavioral Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26101)
*Xiaokai Rong,Aashish Yadavally,Anh H. N. Nguyen,Hridya Dhulipala,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将代码可理解性视为读者与代码交互的关系属性，把"读者"扩展至人类与大模型，提出四种行为代理（BPMU）来度量模型代码可理解性，并检验其与人类判断的行为对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多从以人为中心或以代码为中心的视角研究代码可理解性，但可理解性本质上是读者与代码交互产生的关系属性。随着大模型在软件工程中的广泛应用，"读者"这一概念应扩展至包括人类和模型，因此需要研究人类与模型代码可理解性之间的行为对齐。

Method: 使用先前研究的人类可理解性评分数据集（涵盖不同参与者群体），评估多个开源与闭源LLM；提出四种模型-代码可理解性的行为代理（BPMU）：P0基于程序理解问答，P1–P3基于程序意图摘要（由作者提出的语义自一致性推导而来）。

Result: LLM与一般人类代码可理解性的行为对齐强于以往使用的浅层机器学习基线；分层分析显示模型代码可理解性与专业开发者对齐最紧密，与其它学生群体对齐显著较低；角色条件提示未能改善后者的对齐；语义自一致性是可靠且可扩展的行为代理度量。

Conclusion: 当前LLM无法准确模拟不同专业水平人类读者的视角；语义自一致性可作为量化模型代码可理解性的可靠、可扩展行为代理，对软件工程研究与实践具有广泛启示。

Abstract: Code understandability is a critical aspect of software quality. Prior research has largely focused on this attribute from a human-centric or code-centric perspective, while it should be viewed as a relational property arising from the interaction between a reader and the code. With the increasing adoption of large language models in software engineering, we posit that the notion of "reader" should be generalized to encompass both humans and models. Building on this relational perspective, we extend the concept of code understandability to distinguish between human and model code understandability, aiming to investigate the behavioral alignment between the two. To this end, we use a dataset from a prior study containing human-rated judgments of understandability across diverse participant groups, and evaluate multiple open and closed-source LLMs. To operationalize such behavioral alignment, we introduce four behavioral proxies of model-code understandability (BPMU): P0, based on program comprehension-focused question answering; and P1--P3, based on program intent summarization (derived from our proposed semantic self-consistency). Our findings show that LLMs exhibit stronger behavioral alignment with general human code understandability than previously used shallow machine learning baselines. Stratified analyses further reveal that model code understandability aligns most closely with professional developers, but significantly less with other student groups. Role-conditioned prompting does not improve the alignment for the latter, suggesting that current LLMs cannot accurately mimic the perspectives of human readers with varying expertise. Finally, we demonstrate that semantic self-consistency is a reliable and extensible measure to be used as a behavioral proxy for quantifying model code understandability, with broad implications in both software engineering research and practice.

</details>


### [158] [Post-Hoc Attention Steering of Large Language Models for Robust Code Understanding under Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26102)
*Xiaokai Rong,Aashish Yadavally,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CodeSteer方法，通过注意力引导让LLM关注语义相关的程序元素，显著提升其对混淆代码的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码混淆广泛用于软件系统和恶意软件中以隐藏程序逻辑，给人工和自动化分析带来挑战。尽管LLM在代码理解上表现强劲，但其对混淆的鲁棒性尚不清楚。初步研究表明，LLM在混淆代码上性能显著下降，说明其依赖表层词汇线索而非深层语义推理。

Method: 提出CodeSteer，一种注意力引导方法，将模型注意力重新分配到语义相关的程序元素上，包括用于输出预测的后向切片和用于执行推理的控制流路径。该方法将轻量级程序分析与推理时注意力引导相结合，引导LLM关注程序的核心输入到输出依赖关系。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上的实验表明，CodeSteer显著提升了混淆代码上的性能，通常可恢复到与未混淆程序相当的准确率。通过缓冲区溢出检测案例研究展示了其实际效用。

Conclusion: CodeSteer在恶意软件/漏洞分析和混淆代码逆向工程方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Code obfuscation is widely used in software systems and malware to conceal program logic and hinder analysis, posing significant challenges for both human developers and automated tools. While large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in code understanding, their robustness to obfuscation remains poorly understood. Our preliminary study shows that LLM performance significantly degrades on obfuscated code, suggesting a reliance on superficial lexical cues rather than deep semantic reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose CodeSteer, a novel attention steering approach that reallocates model attention toward semantically relevant program elements, including backward slices for output prediction and control-flow paths for execution reasoning. Our method integrates lightweight program analysis with inference-time attention steering to guide LLMs toward the core input-to-output dependencies of a program. Experiments across multiple models and datasets demonstrate that CodeSteer significantly improves performance on obfuscated code, often recovering comparable accuracy to the level of unobfuscated programs. We also show CodeSteer's practical utility through a case study on buffer overflow detection, highlighting its potential for malware/vulnerability analysis and reverse engineering of obfuscated code.

</details>


### [159] [Why Do LLMs Fail at OCL Generation? A Graph Reasoning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26122)
*Hamza Attarwala,Moataz Chouchen,Mohammad Hamdaqa,Omar Alam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过 PathOCL 数据集对六种先进 LLM 进行实证评估，揭示 LLM 在 OCL 约束生成中的失败主因是图推理能力不足，而非语言因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于提升 LLM 生成 OCL 约束的准确率，却较少深入探究其失败原因。本文旨在从 UML 类图上的图推理视角，系统分析 LLM 在 OCL 生成任务中失败的深层根源。

Method: 基于 PathOCL 数据集，对六种先进 LLM 开展实证评估，分析 UML 结构属性（如导航深度、模型复杂度）、词汇相似度、提示排序策略以及图感知提示对 OCL 正确性的影响。

Result: OCL 生成性能随导航深度和结构复杂度增加而显著下降；词汇相似度影响有限，而 UML 元素的文本排序会影响性能；基于图的提示能带来部分改善，但无法消除结构性推理错误。

Conclusion: OCL 生成主要受图推理能力限制，而非单纯语言因素，凸显结构推理是当前 LLM 在模型驱动工程任务中的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate Object Constraint Language (OCL) constraints from natural language specifications and UML class diagrams. However, existing work mainly focuses on improving accuracy, with limited understanding of why these models fail. Aims. This study investigates the underlying causes of LLM failures in OCL generation, framing the task as a graph reasoning problem over UML class diagrams. Method. We conduct an empirical evaluation using the PathOCL dataset across six state-of-the-art LLMs. We analyze the impact of UML structural properties (e.g., navigation depth and model complexity), lexical similarity, prompt ordering strategies, and graph-aware prompting on OCL correctness. Results. We find that OCL generation performance significantly degrades with increasing navigation depth and structural complexity. Lexical similarity has limited influence, while textual ordering of UML elements affects performance. Graph-based prompting yields partial improvements but does not eliminate structural reasoning errors. Conclusions. OCL generation is primarily constrained by graph reasoning limitations rather than purely linguistic factors. These results highlight structural reasoning as a key bottleneck for current LLMs in model-driven engineering tasks.

</details>


### [160] [A Large-Scale Longitudinal Study of Multi-CI Service Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26181)
*Taher A. Ghaleb,Rasha Bin-Thalab*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对135227个GitHub仓库（7种语言、8个CI服务）进行纵向研究，揭示多CI服务采用、演化与弃用的生命周期规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI采用已从单一实践演变为多服务并存的竞争格局，许多项目不再依赖单一CI服务，但多服务行为如何随项目生命周期演化尚不清楚。已有研究多聚焦单一服务、特定语言或开发者访谈，缺乏对多服务使用生命周期的系统探索。

Method: 开展大规模纵向研究，覆盖135227个GitHub仓库、7种编程语言、8个CI服务，通过定量与定性分析刻画CI服务的采用、演化与弃用过程。包括对CI配置提交理由的分析，以区分战略性与运维性动机。

Result: 约五分之一的仓库使用多个CI服务，但多为迁移过渡而非长期并行使用。CI服务选择是采用、复杂度、维护与参与度的主导因素，语言影响仅限特定情形。GitHub Actions的转向是同步的平台级迁移，2020年所有研究语言的首选采用率均突破50%；Travis CI因定价变化后的衰退是最强的弃用信号。行为多为被动反应，仅约11%的提交说明战略性理由。多CI采用主要与项目成熟度相关，而弃用则与服务身份而非仓库特征关系更强。

Conclusion: 研究为CI工具设计、迁移支持以及演化中的软件交付基础设施管理提供了实证依据，表明应更多关注平台级迁移与运维驱动的行为模式。

Abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) adoption has evolved from a single practice into a broad landscape of competing services, and many projects no longer rely on a single CI service. However, how this multi-service behavior evolves over a project's lifetime remains unknown. Prior work has examined CI adoption and migration through individual services, specific languages, or developer interviews, leaving the lifecycle of multi-service usage underexplored. In this paper, we conduct a large-scale longitudinal study of multi-CI service adoption covering 135227 GitHub repositories from seven programming languages and involving eight CI services. We characterize how CI services are adopted, evolved, and abandoned over time through quantitative and qualitative analyses. About one in five repositories use multiple CI services, often transitional during migration rather than for sustained parallel use. CI service choice is the dominant factor across adoption, complexity, maintenance, and engagement, with language effects limited to specific cases. Our results reveal that the shift to GitHub Actions was a synchronized platform-level transition, crossing 50\% of first adoptions in 2020 across all studied languages, while the decline of Travis CI following its pricing change became the strongest abandonment signal. Our analysis of CI configuration rationales shows that activity is mostly reactive: only about 11\% of commits state a strategic reason, indicating that commit messages capture operational maintenance patterns more reliably than strategic intent. Finally, we find that multi-CI adoption is primarily associated with project maturity, whereas abandonment is more strongly associated with service identity than repository characteristics. These findings provide empirical evidence for CI tooling, migration support, and future research on managing evolving software delivery infrastructure.

</details>


### [161] [WatchPoint: Executable User Feedback for Real-World Agentic Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26204)
*Guanqun Yang,Wei Yang,Xueqing Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出WatchPoint系统，模拟真实开发者行为，对运行中的应用生成并执行诊断脚本，产出结构化观察以指导编码模型的重试，在Web-Bench上恢复57.6%任务，接近人类测试者的54.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体的反馈机制多依赖截图、LLM评判打分或自然语言纠正，较少像开发者那样与运行中的应用交互，因此难以准确诊断Web开发中的失败原因。

Method: WatchPoint模拟用户系统，模仿真实开发者行为，针对运行中的应用生成并执行诊断脚本，产生结构化观察结果来引导编码模型的重新尝试；在包含50个多文件Web项目、1000个顺序依赖任务且由确定性端到端测试验证的Web-Bench上进行评估。

Result: WatchPoint恢复了其诊断任务中的57.6%；对照用户研究显示人类测试者恢复率为54.5%，验证了模拟的真实性；并识别出一类能力差距模式，决定模拟用户反馈何时有帮助、何时应被保留。

Conclusion: 自动化诊断脚本可在顺序Web开发任务中替代交互式人工测试，但模拟用户反馈的有效性受能力差距模式约束，并非始终适用。

Abstract: When a professional web developer's code fails a test, they do not simply re-read the stack trace. They open the application in a browser, click buttons, inspect computed styles, and run diagnostic commands to understand what went wrong. Existing feedback mechanisms for coding agents rely on screenshots, LLM-as-a-judge scoring, or natural-language corrections, but few interact with the live application the way a developer would. We introduce WatchPoint, a simulated-user system that mimics real developer behavior by generating and executing diagnostic scripts against the running application, producing structured observations that guide the coding model's retry. Unlike prior approaches that target single-file edits or evaluate using non-executable metrics, we operate on Web-Bench, a benchmark of 50 multi-file web projects comprising 1,000 sequentially dependent tasks, verified by deterministic end-to-end tests. WatchPoint recovers 57.6% of the tasks it diagnoses, and a controlled user study confirms the simulation's realism: human testers achieve a comparable recovery rate (54.5%), providing evidence that automated diagnostic scripts can substitute for interactive human testing on sequential web development tasks. We further identify a pattern of capability gaps that governs when simulated-user feedback is helpful and when it should be withheld.

</details>


### [162] [Reducing Hallucinations in Large Language Models Through Integrated Self-Verification and Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26229)
*Ashly Joseph*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CoVe-RAG+框架，将链式验证（CoVe）与检索增强生成（RAG）结合，用于缓解LLM在工程任务中的幻觉问题，实验显示事实准确率较基线提升28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM逐渐用于CAD文档、标准合规验证和知识检索等高级工程任务，但容易产生看似可信却无依据的幻觉输出，在追求精度与合规的高端工程应用中限制了其可信度。

Method: 提出CoVe-RAG+统一框架，将链式验证（CoVe）与检索增强生成（RAG）整合，支持LLM基于工程标准、CAD信息和仿真报告等权威外部来源进行验证，并采用迭代式自验证过程校验关键论断。在CAD模型文档、标准合规验证和历史设计数据复用等工程任务上进行评估。

Result: 相比基线CoVe和RAG方法，事实准确率提升28%；同时通过提供说明性验证报告与来源可追溯性增强用户信心。

Conclusion: CoVe-RAG+为在事实准确性至关重要的工程设计流程中部署LLM提供了一种可扩展且可靠的方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are progressively used for advanced engineering tasks, includes Computer-Aided Design (CAD) documentation, standards compliance verification, and knowledge retrieval. Still, they are prone to produce hallucinations, outputs that seem convincing but aren't based on context that limit their trustworthiness in high-end engineering applications where precision and compliance are crucial. The paper introduces CoVe-RAG+, a unified framework that integrates Chain-of-Verification (CoVe) with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to mitigate hallucinations in the results generated by large language models (LLMs). CoVe-RAG+ supports LLM verification in external sources of authority, such as engineering standards, CAD information, and simulation reports, while applying an iterative self-verification process to validate important claims. CoVe-RAG+ is assessed on engineering activities such as CAD model documentation, standards compliance verification, and the reutilization of historical design data. Experimental findings indicate a 28% improvement in factual accuracy relative to baseline CoVe and RAG methodologies. Moreover, CoVe-RAG+ strengthens user confidence by providing elucidative verification reports and source traceability. The findings indicate that CoVe-RAG+ provides a scalable and reliable option for implementing LLMs in engineering design processes where factual accuracy is critical.

</details>


### [163] [CANcept: Model-based CAN Traffic Generation and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26263)
*Lino Wertz,Junes Sheikhi,Florian Fehrle,Adrian Rupp,Tianhai Liu,Philipp Kern,Carsten Sinz,Bernhard Beckert*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一款开源、基于模型的CAN流量工具，用于指定、生成、回放和操纵CAN通信，支持定时相关安全属性测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于CAN总线的软件在测试定时相关安全属性时，需要精确控制发送数据和通信时序。现有开源工具通常用底层脚本编码这些场景，难以维护和演进。

Method: 提出开源模型化工具。通过流量调度模型（TSM）定义报文时序与内容变换，通过基于DBC的通信模型（DCM）定义报文级流量与CAN帧之间的编解码，并将两个模型结合在统一的执行机制中实现流量的生成、操纵与变换后轨迹的回放。

Result: 初步评估表明，该工具能够实现所指定的测试场景，时序偏差低，并在提高的流量速率下保持稳定执行。

Conclusion: 该工具为CAN定时相关安全属性测试提供了一种可维护、可扩展的模型化解决方案。

Abstract: Testing timing-related safety properties of Controller Area Network (CAN)-based software requires precise control over transmitted data and communication timing. Existing open-source tools typically encode such scenarios in low-level scripts, making them difficult to maintain and evolve. We present \toolName{}, an open-source model-based tool for specifying, generating, replaying, and manipulating CAN traffic. A traffic schedule model (TSM) defines message timing and content transformations, and a DBC-based communication model (DCM) defines encoding and decoding between message-level traffic and CAN frames. \toolName{} combines both models in one execution mechanism for generated and manipulated traffic, including replay of transformed traces. A preliminary evaluation indicates that \toolName{} realizes the specified scenarios, with low timing deviation and stable execution under elevated traffic rates.

</details>


### [164] [On the Lexical Superstition of Large Language Models for Code Comprehension: Re-evaluation on Code of Low Lexical Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26388)
*Xin Shen,San-Zhuo Xi,Yali Du,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出Face/Off语义保持的标识符重命名框架，发现当前LLM在代码理解任务中普遍过度依赖标识符名称的词汇线索，且该问题在提示与微调干预下依然顽固。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标识符名称在自然代码中具有统计信息量，但其信息并不总是可靠。作者想探究当程序结构保持不变、仅改变标识符命名时，LLM是否会不合理地过度赋予词汇线索以权重。

Method: 提出Face/Off——一个保持语义的标识符重命名框架，在多个模型和代码理解任务上评估渐进式命名条件（移除或误导标识符信息），并测试代表性提示与微调干预，另设类型推断对照实验。

Result: 词汇过度依赖在评估模型和主要任务中普遍存在：随标识符信息被移除或误导，性能普遍下降，且输出常被导向误导性名称所暗示的含义；提示与微调干预难以消除该模式；类型推断对照显示，当答案可局部恢复而无需目标名称时，命名效应较小。

Conclusion: 结果并非说明标识符无用，而是揭示当前LLM在权衡词汇线索与程序结构方面存在系统性脆弱性。研究呼吁构建既保留自然代码规律益处、又将结论建立在准确形式化代码语义之上的评估与建模方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have made them widely used for code-related tasks. Identifier names are statistically informative in naturally occurring code, but their information is not always reliable. We investigate whether current LLMs assign disproportionate weight to lexical cues when renaming preserves program structure. We introduce Face/Off, a semantics-preserving identifier-renaming framework, and evaluate progressive naming conditions across multiple models and code-comprehension tasks. Within this framework, lexical overemphasis is pervasive across the evaluated models and primary tasks: performance generally decreases as identifier information is removed or made misleading, and outputs are often directed toward the meanings suggested by misleading names. The pattern persists under representative prompt- and fine-tuning-based interventions, suggesting that lexical overemphasis is an entrenched problem. A type-inference control confirms a boundary: naming effects are smaller when the answer is locally recoverable without the target name. These results do not imply that identifiers are unhelpful; rather, they reveal a systematic vulnerability in how current LLMs balance lexical cues against program structure. Our findings motivate evaluations and modeling methods that preserve the benefits of natural code regularities while keeping conclusions grounded in accurate, formalized code semantics.

</details>


### [165] [FeatLens: Feature-Guided Dynamic Code Graph Construction and Retrieval for Repository-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26480)
*Xutian Li,Bo Xiong,Yifeng Zhu,Kunze Li,Xianlin Zhao,Runbang Yan,Yanzhen Zou,Lu Zhang,Bing Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FeatLens 提出一种特征引导的动态代码图构建与检索方法，用于仓库级代码生成，通过特征索引与个性化 PageRank 实现轻量、确定性的依赖检索，在 DevEval 和 EvoCodeBench 上取得最优的 DR@15 与 DIR@1，并显著降低图规模和 token 开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级代码生成需识别可复用依赖（如已有函数、API、跨文件定义）。现有检索方法（相似度搜索、持久化全仓库图、LLM 驱动图探索）存在图构建、推理与 token 成本高的问题；而特征导向方法虽贴合软件功能视角，但主要支持需求分解、规划或特征编辑，并未用于代码依赖检索。

Method: FeatLens 构建特征索引，将自然语言特征描述与函数级代码实体关联。给定生成任务后，从特征索引动态构建任务特定的种子图，并结合语义-结构图推理与个性化 PageRank 选择紧凑的推理图。该设计以确定性、轻量的依赖检索替代持久化的全仓库图维护和 LLM 探索。

Result: 在 DevEval 和 EvoCodeBench 上，FeatLens 在稀疏、稠密与图基线中取得最佳 DR@15（0.501 和 0.460）。DevEval 生成上取得最高 DIR@1，DeepSeek-V3.2 为 52.91%，GPT-5-mini 为 53.58%，同时保持有竞争力的 Pass@1 并生成更短代码。相比最强图基线，FeatLens 减少 61.0% 图节点、86.2% 边、45.9% 总 token 开销，且检索阶段不使用 LLM token。

Conclusion: FeatLens 通过特征引导的动态图构建与检索，在提升仓库级代码生成依赖检索效果的同时，大幅降低图规模与 token 成本，验证了确定性轻量检索替代全仓库图与 LLM 探索的可行性。

Abstract: Recent code generation research has moved from isolated function completion toward repository-level generation in existing codebases. To implement a target function correctly, an LLM must identify reusable repository dependencies such as existing functions, APIs, and cross-file definitions. Existing retrieval methods provide such context through code similarity search, persistent whole-repository graphs, or LLM-driven graph exploration, but often incur high graph construction, reasoning, and token costs. Feature-oriented methods offer a natural view of software functionality, yet they mainly support requirement decomposition, planning, or feature editing rather than code dependency retrieval. This paper presents \textbf{FeatLens}, a feature-guided dynamic code graph construction and retrieval approach for repository-level code generation. FeatLens builds a feature index that links natural-language feature descriptions to function-level code entities. Given a generation task, it dynamically constructs a task-specific seed graph from the feature index and applies semantic-structural graph reasoning with personalized PageRank to select a compact reasoning graph. This design replaces persistent whole-repository graph maintenance and LLM exploration with deterministic and lightweight dependency retrieval. Experiments on DevEval and EvoCodeBench show that FeatLens achieves the best DR@15 among sparse, dense, and graph-based baselines (0.501 and 0.460). On DevEval generation, it obtains the highest DIR@1, reaching 52.91\% with DeepSeek-V3.2 and 53.58\% with GPT-5-mini, while maintaining competitive Pass@1 and producing shorter code. Compared with the strongest graph-based baseline, FeatLens reduces graph nodes by 61.0\%, edges by 86.2\%, and total token overhead by 45.9\%, with no LLM tokens used during retrieval.

</details>


### [166] [From Approval to Execution: Reconstruction-Aware Repair Analysis for LLM-Agent Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26529)
*Junchi Zhu,Zhenguang Liu,Shaojing Fan,Jianhai Chen,Qinming He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"重建稳定授权"（ReSA）概念，用于分析LLM智能体审批机制中因对象重建导致的残余越权问题，并在工具APAS-Finder中实现，实验表明修复充分性取决于最终被执行的重建对象，而非审批记录或早期被检查的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体软件中审批机制成为高风险操作的主要防护手段，但用于审批展示的对象往往并非最终被执行的对象：工作流重载、transcript投影、参数重绑定、持久状态查找等都可能在执行前重建该对象。现有的字段流和检查覆盖分析只能确定期望字段被检查过，却无法保证被检查的对象版本真正到达执行点，也无法保证检查与使用之间没有替代对象介入。因此，即使局部完整的修复仍可能残留授权绕过。作者正是要解决这一"重建后残余越权"问题。

Method: 本文包含三项设计：（1）在表示转换、sink依赖、被消耗版本和授权范围之上形式化"重建稳定授权"（ReSA）；（2）推导出用于评判候选修复、并暴露残余sink后缀的义务（obligations）；（3）在APAS-Finder中实现该分析，并在独立的sink oracle观测执行之前冻结预测结果。

Result: 预测结果与全部28个受控结果一致。四个配对样本在字段流和检查覆盖相同的情况下需要做出相反判断；当提供对象流、支配性、干扰及相同授权契约时，一个CodeQL复合查询可恢复全部8个判断。两名分析者在全部四个模型验证判定上一致，在sink依赖上一致率达19/20。在已发布的消费者上，五个修复阻止了60/60的测试越界效应，而三个机制对照实验则暴露出预测的残余效应。

Conclusion: 修复的充分性取决于实际被消耗的重建动作，而不仅仅取决于审批记录或更早被检查的表示。因此，授权分析必须追踪对象在表示转换与重建后的最终消费版本。

Abstract: Approval mechanisms have become a primary safeguard for consequential actions in LLM-agent software. Yet the action shown for approval is often not the object ultimately consumed: workflow reload, transcript projection, argument rebinding, and durable-state lookup may reconstruct it before execution. Existing fieldflow and check-coverage analyses can establish that expected fields were inspected, but not that the inspected object version reaches the sink or that no replacement intervenes between check and use. Consequently, a locally complete repair may still leave a residual authorization bypass after reconstruction. We address this problem through three designs. (1) We formulate reconstruction-stable authorization (ReSA) over representation transitions, sink dependencies, consumed versions, and grant scope. (2) We derive obligations that judge candidate repairs and expose residual sink suffixes. (3) We implement the analysis in APAS-Finder and freeze predictions before an independent sink oracle observes execution. Predictions agree with all 28 controlled outcomes. Four matched pairs require opposite judgments despite identical field-flow and check coverage; a CodeQL composite recovers all eight when supplied object flow, dominance, interference, and the same grant contract. Two analysts agree on all four model-validation dispositions and 19/20 sink dependencies. On released consumers, five repairs prevent 60/60 tested out-of-scope effects, while three mechanism contrasts expose the predicted residual effects. Repair sufficiency therefore depends on the reconstructed action consumed, not merely on an approval record or earlier checked representation.

</details>


### [167] [TraceVIC: Causal Reasoning over Code Evolution for Identifying Vulnerability-Inducing Commits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26711)
*Fnu Tanish,Samiha Shimmi,Samikshya Chapagain,Hamed Okhravi,Mona Rahimi,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TraceVIC：基于时序图的漏洞引入提交识别方法，通过推理代码演化历史而非依赖git blame位置启发式，在C/C++项目上将F2提升至0.814。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件漏洞常在被引入很久后才被发现，难以定位引入漏洞的提交（VIC）。现有技术主要依赖git blame追踪易受攻击代码并用位置启发式（选取最早或最近的修改）判断，但真正的VIC可能出现在历史中的任意位置，且脆弱行为可能依赖跨多个修订演化的代码。因此，VIC识别需要推理漏洞相关代码如何演化，而非仅看候选提交在修订历史中的位置。

Method: 提出TraceVIC，一种基于时序图的方法。首先定位可能的根因代码行并追踪其跨修订的历史，构建图表示以捕获每个修订内的程序结构和漏洞相关代码在历史中的演化；在得到的修订历史上进行推理，使用时序边保留相邻修订间程序元素的对应关系，并根据候选提交对脆弱条件的贡献直接对其排序。

Result: 消融实验表明，建模完整修订历史将F2从0.637提升至0.814。TraceVIC相比最先进方法F2最高提升28.7%，并在四个未见过的C/C++项目中为79个漏洞中的78个识别出有效VIC。

Conclusion: 通过时序图对代码演化进行推理，TraceVIC显著优于依赖位置启发式的现有VIC识别方法，证明了建模完整修订历史与程序结构演化对于准确定位漏洞引入提交的重要性。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities are often discovered long after they are introduced, making it difficult to identify the vulnerability-inducing commit (VIC) responsible for introducing the underlying vulnerable condition. Existing VIC identification techniques largely rely on git blame to trace vulnerable code through revision history and use positional heuristics, such as selecting its earliest or most recent modification. However, the true VIC may occur anywhere within this history, and vulnerable behavior may depend on code that evolves across multiple revisions. We therefore argue that VIC identification requires reasoning about how vulnerability-relevant code evolves, rather than simply where a candidate commit appears in the revision history.
  We present TraceVIC, a temporal graph-based approach for identifying and ranking VICs by reasoning over code evolution. TraceVIC first localizes likely root-cause lines and traces their histories across revisions, constructing graph representations that capture program structure within each revision and the evolution of vulnerability-relevant code across the history. It reasons over the resulting revision history, using temporal edges to preserve correspondences between program elements across consecutive revisions, and directly ranks candidate commits according to their contribution to the vulnerable condition.
  Ablation results show that modeling the full revision history improves F2 from 0.637 to 0.814. TraceVIC improves F2 by up to 28.7% over state-of-the-art methods and identifies a valid VIC for 78 of 79 vulnerabilities across four unseen C/C++ projects.

</details>


### [168] [Metrics Failure in LLM-Based Code Vulnerability Repair: An Empirical Study and a Change-Aware Screen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26749)
*Om Nepal,Sushant Aryal,Oluseyi Olukola,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过五项受控实验论证：在单函数C/C++漏洞修复中，编译率（compile rate）作为评估指标在科学上不可靠，并建议采用变更感知、执行验证的评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多用于自动化修复C/C++安全漏洞，而编译率（生成的补丁是否能编译）被普遍当作进展的代理指标。作者认为该指标在单函数漏洞修复场景中并不科学可靠，需要系统地检验其有效性。

Method: 基于Big-Vul的203个漏洞函数、3个开源代码LLM（3.5亿至67亿参数）和3种提示策略，开展五项受控实验，并辅以人工检查；同时评估了两类替代/筛选指标：整体函数CodeBLEU与只对编辑区域打分的diff_F1。

Result: 编译率表现出五项缺陷：(i) 对能显著改善生成代码的干预几乎无响应；(ii) 主要受评估框架和数据集伪影支配而非模型质量，约64%的编译失败不可归因于模型，且该比例跨模型近乎不变；(iii) 在仅改变一个编译器标准标志时，同一补丁的编译率变化达1.8–2.7倍，且无回归；(iv) 对三个模型的排序与参考相似度指标完全相反；(v) 作为优化目标时会奖励"非修复"——编译器反馈循环提高编译率的同时降低与人工修复的相似度，人工检查发现新编译通过的输出中存在删除式和占位符式伪修复。作为天然备选的整函数CodeBLEU同样失效：未修改的漏洞输入副本得分高于所有模型。diff_F1则对完全无操作给零分，并对部分（非全部）删除式作弊补丁给近零分，同时仍能认可真实的局部修改。

Conclusion: 编译率不能作为单函数漏洞修复的有效评估指标，整函数CodeBLEU亦不适用。diff_F1可作为深入执行验证之前的低成本筛选工具，但它本身不是修复质量指标，作者也明确报告了其局限。研究呼吁对LLM漏洞修复采用变更感知、执行验证的评估方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to the automated repair of C/C++ security vulnerabilities, and compile rate is a commonly reported proxy for progress: whether the generated patch compiles. We argue that compile rate is a scientifically unreliable metric for single-function vulnerability repair, and we support this with five controlled experiments over 203 vulnerable functions from Big-Vul, three open-source code LLMs (350M to 6.7B parameters), and three prompting strategies. Compile rate (i) barely responds to an intervention that substantially improves the generated code; (ii) is dominated by evaluation-harness and dataset artifacts rather than model quality, with about 64% of compile failures not attributable to the model, a share that is nearly invariant across models; (iii) shifts by 1.8 to 2.7 times on identical patches under a single compiler-standard flag, with zero regressions; (iv) ranks the three models in the opposite order to reference-similarity metrics; and (v) rewards non-repairs when used as an optimization target, since a compiler-feedback loop raises compile rate while similarity to the human fix falls, with manual inspection finding deletion- and placeholder-style non-repairs among the newly compiling outputs. The natural fallback, whole-function CodeBLEU, also fails: an unchanged copy of the vulnerable input outscores every model. We also examine diff_F1, a change-aware screen that scores only the edited region. It gives exactly zero credit to a no-op and near-zero credit to some, though not all, of the deletion-based gaming patches we observed, while still crediting genuine partial edits, so it may serve as a cheap screen before deeper, execution-based analysis. It is not a repair-quality metric, and we report where it falls short. Our findings argue for change-aware, execution-grounded evaluation of LLM-based vulnerability repair.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Spend Classification Without Leakage: An Evaluation Harness and What It Changed in a Deployed System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25502)
*Harshit Gupta*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个公开的商品编码分类评测框架，基于126万条真实采购数据，揭示现有评测中的文本泄漏问题，并给出无泄漏协议下的性能对比与理论上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商品编码分类（将自由文本采购行映射到标准商品代码）是企业支出分析的基础，但其报告准确率无法核实：已有结果使用专有数据或私有样本，协议不透明，彼此不可比较，也缺乏公开基准。

Method: 构建包含四种评测协议的框架，使用两个美国政府共126万条已标注采购行。针对"企业重复采购"导致随机划分中测试集出现与训练集相同文本（泄漏）的问题，区分"重复文本"与"新文本"评估；对比经典基线、嵌入检索与微调transformer，并通过"相同文本对应冲突编码"推导文本分类器的准确率上界。

Result: 随机划分中60.7%/61.2%的测试行、54.7%/60.6%逐字节匹配训练集。加州数据上最优经典基线在重复文本得56.0%，新文本仅31.9%（差24点，随分类层级加深扩大）；嵌入检索将差距从23.8降至19.1点，微调transformer也未能摆脱。泄漏评测无法区分检索与transformer，而三种无泄漏协议显示transformer领先2.3–3.6点。文本上界在两个语料上分别为79.4%与98.4%。920,927行的部署中，新文本建议被接受率为32.4%，与基准区间29.7–35.2%相符。

Conclusion: 现有评测因文本泄漏而掩盖真实差异且跨数据集不可比；准确率无法跨数据集比较。作者公开该框架并已投入生产使用。

Abstract: Assigning a standard commodity code to a free-text purchase line underpins enterprise spend analytics, and its reported accuracy cannot be checked. Published results use proprietary data or private samples under undocumented protocols; no two compare and there is no public benchmark. We build a harness with four protocols over 1.26 million labelled purchase lines from two US state governments. Enterprises rebuy continuously, so random splits put matching item text on both sides: 60.7% and 61.2% of test rows, 54.7% and 60.6% byte for byte. On California orders the best classical baseline scores 56.0% on repeated text but 31.9% on novel text, a 24-point gap widening with taxonomy depth. Embedding retrieval shrinks the gap from 23.8 to 19.1 points; a fine-tuned transformer does not escape it. On one corpus leaky evaluation cannot separate retrieval from the transformer, while three leak-free protocols put the transformer 2.3 to 3.6 points ahead, so leaky splits hide real differences, not just flatter both. We derive an upper bound on text-only classifiers from identical text with conflicting codes: 79.4% commodity accuracy on one corpus against 98.4% on the other, so accuracy does not compare across datasets. Spend-weighting the bound reflects the amount field more than labels. In a deployment of 920,927 lines, reviewers accepted 32.4% of suggestions on the novel-text population; 29.7-35.2% brackets benchmark rates near 31.9% and 34.8%. We release the harness in production use.

</details>


### [170] [AkasicMEM: Governed Enterprise Memory for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25563)
*Jeongmin Bae,Yongjae Kim,Kyoung Hur,Donghyoung Han,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出"受治理的企业记忆"(Governed Enterprise Memory)概念及实现系统AkasicMEM，通过传递性血缘、记忆形成时的策略组合与检索时的策略再评估，实现源到记忆及记忆间的授权连续性，防止信息泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体记忆能让企业智能体保留并跨任务、跨智能体重用工作知识，将执行经验转化为持久组织知识。但这需要同时满足源-记忆集成与记忆治理两个要求，二者在源信息持续留存于记忆时相互作用：当信息在变化的主体与策略下被反复派生和重用时，源限制可能被绕过，导致信息泄漏。现有方法各自单独处理这些问题，未将源-记忆集成、记忆治理与授权连续性作为跨记忆生命周期的组合核心设计目标。

Method: 提出"受治理的企业记忆"这一设计范畴，并给出实现系统AkasicMEM。其通过三项机制实现授权连续性：传递性血缘(transitive lineage)、记忆形成时的策略组合(policy composition during memory formation)、检索时的策略再评估(policy re-evaluation during retrieval)。系统构建于GraphAI的AkasicDB之上，该统一向量-图-关系数据库的存储与执行底座支持上述机制底层操作的联合优化与执行。

Result: 摘要为概念与系统设计类描述，未提供实验数据或定量评估结果；主要成果是提出Governed Enterprise Memory概念并实现AkasicMEM系统作为其落地。

Conclusion: 企业智能体记忆需要将源-记忆集成、记忆治理与授权连续性整合为统一的设计目标。AkasicMEM通过传递性血缘、策略组合与策略再评估三重机制，并依托AkasicDB的统一存储执行底座，实现贯穿记忆生命周期的授权连续性，从而防止源限制被绕过与信息泄漏。

Abstract: Agent memory enables enterprise agents to retain knowledge acquired during work and reuse it across tasks and agents, turning execution experience into persistent organizational knowledge. Realizing this potential requires both source--memory integration, through which enterprise sources and accumulated memory can be utilized together, and memory governance, through which shared memory remains subject to organizational policies throughout its lifecycle. These requirements interact when information from enterprise sources persists in memory. As this information is repeatedly derived and reused under changing principals and policies, source restrictions may be bypassed, resulting in information leakage. Preventing such leakage requires authorization continuity, under which source restrictions remain effective throughout source-to-memory and memory-to-memory derivation and reuse. Existing approaches address these concerns individually, but do not treat source--memory integration, memory governance, and authorization continuity as combined core design targets across the memory lifecycle. We define Governed Enterprise Memory as agent memory designed around this combined scope and present AkasicMEM as its realization. AkasicMEM realizes authorization continuity through transitive lineage, policy composition during memory formation, and policy re-evaluation during retrieval. It is built on GraphAI's AkasicDB, a unified vector--graph--relational database whose storage and execution substrate enables the underlying operations of these mechanisms to be jointly optimized and executed.

</details>


### [171] [Generating Query Context for Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26200)
*Alekh Jindal,Jyoti Pandey,Christina Pavlopoulou,Ronith PR,Sharath Prakash,Shi Qiao,Shivani Tripathi,Wangda Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种自动生成查询上下文的方案，用于关系数据库自然语言查询，并在 50+ 真实数据库上部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系数据库是业务系统的记录载体，通过自然语言查询它们的需求日益增长。挑战在于 AI 模型需要合适的查询上下文（示例问题及其对应的数据模型片段）才能生成准确的 SQL。而目前构造该上下文依赖人工，耗时且需同时具备 SQL 与数据库模式专业知识，导致新数据库的冷启动问题，以及模式与查询模式演进带来的持续维护负担。

Method: 本文提出一种自动生成关系数据库查询上下文的方法：定义"查询流"以刻画常见数据检索与分析类问题模式；通过遍历这些查询流系统化生成数据模型；再使用加权策略、从数据库中采样具体值来实例化这些模型，从而最大化多样性与覆盖率。生成的问题—数据模型对可用于引导自然语言接口准确查询关系数据库。

Result: 报告了将该方法部署到 Tursio 所连接的 50 多个真实世界数据库上的实践经验。

Conclusion: 该自动化方法能够替代人工构造查询上下文，缓解冷启动与维护负担，并在大规模真实环境中得到部署验证。

Abstract: Relational databases are the systems of record for business applications, and there is growing demand to query them through natural language interfaces.
